### [1] [RTLocating: Intent-aware RTL Localization for Hardware Design Iteration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00434)
*Changwen Xing,Yanfeng Lu,Lei Qi,Chenxu Niu,Jie Li,Xi Wang,Yong Chen,Jun Yang*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 提出了RTLocating框架，用于将自然语言变更请求定位到受影响的RTL代码块，解决了工业芯片开发中迭代更新的需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业芯片开发本质上是迭代式的，倾向于局部化、意图驱动的更新而非从头重写RTL。然而现有LLM辅助硬件设计工作主要关注一次性综合，忽略了这一工作流程。需要填补自然语言变更请求到受影响RTL代码块定位的研究空白。

Method: 提出了RTLocating框架，包含动态路由器，自适应融合文本语义编码器、局部结构编码器以及全局交互与依赖编码器（GLIDE）的互补视图。同时创建了EvoRTL-Bench基准数据集，从OpenTitan的Git历史中提取了1,905个验证请求和13,583个ΔSpec-RTL块对。

Result: 在EvoRTL-Bench基准上，RTLocating达到了0.568的MRR和15.08%的R@1，分别比最强基线提升了+22.9%和+67.0%，在演化硬件设计的意图驱动定位方面建立了新的最先进水平。

Conclusion: 该工作首次形式化了ΔSpec-to-RTL定位问题，提出了有效的RTLocating框架和基准数据集，显著提升了意图驱动硬件设计更新的定位性能，填补了工业芯片开发迭代工作流程的研究空白。

Abstract: Industrial chip development is inherently iterative, favoring localized, intent-driven updates over rewriting RTL from scratch. Yet most LLM-Aided Hardware Design (LAD) work focuses on one-shot synthesis, leaving this workflow underexplored. To bridge this gap, we for the first time formalize $Δ$Spec-to-RTL localization, a multi-positive problem mapping natural language change requests ($Δ$Spec) to the affected Register Transfer Level (RTL) syntactic blocks. We propose RTLocating, an intent-aware RTL localization framework, featuring a dynamic router that adaptively fuses complementary views from a textual semantic encoder, a local structural encoder, and a global interaction and dependency encoder (GLIDE). To enable scalable supervision, we introduce EvoRTL-Bench, the first industrial-scale benchmark for intent-code alignment derived from OpenTitan's Git history, comprising 1,905 validated requests and 13,583 $Δ$Spec-RTL block pairs. On EvoRTL-Bench, RTLocating achieves 0.568 MRR and 15.08% R@1, outperforming the strongest baseline by +22.9% and +67.0%, respectively, establishing a new state-of-the-art for intent-driven localization in evolving hardware designs.

</details>


### [2] [Bentō: Optimizing Persistent Memory Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01889)
*Sebastião Amaro,João Gonçalves,Miguel Matos*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: Bentō是一个自动化的黑盒二进制重写器，通过优化持久内存程序中的持久性指令组合，在不改变崩溃一致性语义的前提下提升性能达15%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久内存(PM)编程需要开发者使用显式指令(如flush和fence)来保证崩溃一致性，这既难以正确实现，又容易导致过度保守的优化，从而部分牺牲PM的性能优势

Method: 研究不同持久性指令组合对PM程序的影响，发现特定组合可在保持崩溃一致性语义的同时提升性能；基于此开发Bentō——一个自动化的黑盒二进制重写器

Result: Bentō能够将现有PM程序的性能提升高达15%，且只需最小的程序员工作量

Conclusion: 通过自动优化持久性指令组合，可以在不改变程序语义的前提下显著提升PM程序性能，为PM编程提供了一种实用的性能优化方案

Abstract: Persistent Memory (PM) is a new storage technology thatbrings high performance, byte addressability, and persistency for a lesser cost than DRAM. Due to cache volatility and store reordering, developers must use explicit instructions (e.g.: flush and fence) to guarantee that the application state remains consistent upon crashes. This is difficult to get right and, in fact, several tools have been created to detect bugs in PM programs. To overcome this difficulty, programmers tend to be overly conservative, for instance, by enforcing unnecessary ordering constraints, which partially forfeits the performance benefits of using PM. In this paper, we study the impact that different combinations of persistency instructions have in several PM programs and found that a specific combination can lead to performance improvements while preserving the original crash-consistency semantics. Based on these results we developed Bentō an automatic and black-box binary rewriter that can boost the performance of existing PM programs by up to 15% with minimal programmer effort.

</details>


### [3] [Implicitly Parallel Neuromorphic Solver Design for Constraint Satisfaction Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01150)
*Recep Bugra Uludag,Ahmet Efe,Ismail Akturk,Ulya R Karpuzcu*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 该论文研究了神经形态求解器在约束满足问题中的原生并行性优势，相比经典求解器可实现两个数量级的速度提升而不损失精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多实际应用中的约束满足问题（从芯片设计到生物信息学）由于计算复杂性，经典求解器只能依赖启发式近似。神经形态求解器提供了独特的替代表示，能够实现解空间的固有并行探索，但需要理论表征和实践验证来释放其性能潜力。

Method: 论文提供了神经形态求解器原生并行性的理论表征，并通过实验演示验证了这种并行性。研究对比了神经形态求解器与经典求解器在约束满足问题上的性能表现。

Result: 研究发现神经形态求解器能够实现超过两个数量级的运算速度提升，同时不损害解的准确性。这种性能优势源于神经形态架构固有的并行探索能力。

Conclusion: 该研究是弥合理论与实践差距的第一步，为释放新兴神经形态求解器的性能潜力奠定了基础。神经形态求解器在约束满足问题上展现出显著优于经典求解器的并行计算优势。

Abstract: Many real-life problems of practical importance -- spanning a wide range of applications from chip design to bioinformatics -- represent constraint satisfaction problems, where classical solvers have to rely on heuristic approximations due to the computational complexity. Neuromorphic solvers, on the other hand, offer a unique alternative representation which enables an inherently parallel exploration of the solution space. This paper provides a theoretical characterization and experimental demonstration of this native type of parallelism that is hard to apply to classical solvers. We observe that more than two orders of magnitude faster operation is possible without compromising solution accuracy. Our study represents the first step toward bridging the theory vs. practice gap to unlock the performance potential of emerging neuromorphic solvers.

</details>

### [4] [Bug Severity Prediction in Software Projects Using Supervised Machine Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00004)
*Nafisha Tamanna Nice*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究比较了多种基于监督机器学习算法的分类器，用于预测Eclipse Bugzilla历史仓库数据中的bug严重程度，发现集成树方法和DistilBERT模型在整体准确率上表现最佳，而线性模型在关键bug的召回率上表现最好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大型软件项目中，bug仓库快速增长使得手动分类严重程度变得劳动密集、不可靠且容易受到人为偏见影响。因此需要自动化方法来预测bug严重程度，以帮助开发团队优先处理对系统运行、稳定性和安全性有重大影响的bug。

Method: 使用Eclipse Bugzilla的历史仓库数据，比较基于监督机器学习算法的不同分类器，包括线性分类器、梯度提升树、距离方法和基于Transformer的模型。采用文本特征（通过分词、TF-IDF和n-gram获得）和不平衡校正方法。使用准确率、精确率、召回率、F1分数、AUC-ROC和混淆矩阵进行评估。

Result: 集成树方法和DistilBERT模型获得了最高的整体准确率。线性模型在关键bug的召回率上表现最佳，这表明在不平衡的严重程度预测中存在精确率-召回率的权衡。

Conclusion: 研究结果为选择自动化bug分类算法提供了有用的可操作见解，可以通过有效的可扩展优先级排序来提高软件质量。不同算法在不同指标上的表现差异表明需要根据具体需求（如更关注关键bug的召回率还是整体准确率）选择合适的模型。

Abstract: Bug severity prediction is important in software maintenance, because it helps the development teams to prioritize bugs that have a significant impact on the operation, stability and security of the system. In large software projects bug repositories will grow at very rapid rate making classification of severity manual work labourious and unreliable and prone to human biasness. Many efforts have thus been dedicated on automated ways of severity prediction in the literature of software engineering research.This study compares different classifiers that are based on supervised machine learning algorithms for predicting bug severity levels using historical repository data from Eclipse Bugzilla. Evaluated methods range from linear classifiers, gradient boosting trees, distance method and transformer-based models, and text features, which are obtained from tokenization, TF-IDF, and n-grams and imbalance correction methods. Models were evaluated in terms of accuracy, precision, recall, F1 score, (AUC-ROC) and confusion matrix. Ensemble tree methods and DistilBERT achieved the top overall accuracy, while linear models performed best in recall of critical bugs which indicates some precision-recall tradeoff in imbalanced severity prediction. These findings provide useful actionable insight in choosing algorithms for automated bug triage, which can improve the quality of software through effective scalable prioritization.
  Keywords: Bug severity prediction, Supervised machine learning, Classification models, Software quality assurance, Historical bug data, Predictive analytics.

</details>


### [5] [Test Case Prioritization: A Snowballing Literature Review and TCPFramework with Approach Combinators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00183)
*Tomasz Chojnacki,Lech Madeyski*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文通过系统文献综述、开发TCP研究平台、提出新评估指标和集成TCP方法，改进了测试用例优先级排序技术，在RTPTorrent数据集上表现优于基准方法，回归测试时间减少达2.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试用例优先级排序（TCP）是加速回归测试的常用技术，但现有知识缺乏系统化，且需要更有效的TCP方法和评估指标。

Method: 1. 进行TCP的雪球式文献综述；2. 实现TCP研究综合平台（TCPFramework）；3. 分析现有评估指标并提出两个新指标（rAPFDc和ATR）；4. 开发名为"方法组合器"的集成TCP方法族。

Result: 1. 文献综述识别出324项TCP相关研究；2. 提出的方法在RTPTorrent数据集上评估，在大多数测试程序中持续优于基准方法；3. 在rAPFDc、NTR和ATR指标上达到与当前启发式算法相当的性能；4. 回归测试时间减少达2.7%。

Conclusion: 提出的方法能有效用于TCP，显著减少回归测试时间。方法组合器因其可组合性，为未来TCP研究提供了重要改进潜力。

Abstract: Context: Test case prioritization (TCP) is a technique widely used by software development organizations to accelerate regression testing. Objectives: We aim to systematize existing TCP knowledge and to propose and empirically evaluate a new TCP approach. Methods: We conduct a snowballing review (SR) on TCP, implement a~comprehensive platform for TCP research (TCPFramework), analyze existing evaluation metrics and propose two new ones (\rAPFDc{} and ATR), and develop a~family of ensemble TCP methods called approach combinators. Results: The SR helped identify 324 studies related to TCP. The techniques proposed in our study were evaluated on the RTPTorrent dataset, consistently outperforming their base approaches across the majority of subject programs, and achieving performance comparable to the current state of the art for heuristical algorithms (in terms of \rAPFDc{}, NTR, and ATR), while using a distinct approach. Conclusions: The proposed methods can be used efficiently for TCP, reducing the time spent on regression testing by up to 2.7\%. Approach combinators offer significant potential for improvements in future TCP research, due to their composability.

</details>


### [6] [ClarEval: A Benchmark for Evaluating Clarification Skills of Code Agents under Ambiguous Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00187)
*Jialin Li,Yuan Wu,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ClarEval：评估代码智能体协作能力的框架，通过模拟真实沟通中的模糊性来测量智能体的"协作商数"，强调从被动代码生成到主动需求澄清的范式转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前代码智能体评估主要关注理想条件下的意图猜测能力，忽视了智能体通过对话与用户对齐的协作能力，而这对于实际软件工程中的无缝集成至关重要。需要推动评估范式从被动跟随向主动协作转变。

Method: 提出ClarEval框架，通过系统注入三种现实模糊性（缺失目标、缺失前提、模糊术语）到标准任务中，迫使智能体从"生成器"角色转向需求澄清。提出以平均澄清轮数（ATC）和关键问题覆盖率（KQC）为核心的评估指标，衡量协作效率和精准度。

Result: 对11个最先进智能体的实验显示，虽然GPT-5-Coder等模型在编码方面表现出色，但普遍缺乏高效协作所需的战略沟通技能，揭示了强大编码能力与协作能力之间的显著差距。

Conclusion: ClarEval为弥合优秀编码者与能力协作伙伴之间的差距提供了关键路线图，强调了评估范式转变对于推动代码智能体成为真正协作伙伴的重要性。

Abstract: To integrate seamlessly into real-world software engineering, Code Agents must evolve from passive instruction followers into proactive collaborative partners. However, current evaluation paradigms predominantly reward "guessing" user intent under ideal conditions, neglecting the agent's ability to align with users through dialogue--a critical trait for collaborative intelligence. In this work, we propose a paradigm shift in evaluation to drive this transition. We introduce ClarEval, a framework designed to assess an agent's "Collaborative Quotient" by simulating the inherent ambiguity of human communication. By systematically injecting three types of realistic ambiguity (missing goals, premises, and ambiguous terminology) into standard tasks, we force agents to step out of their "generator" role and engage in requirement elicitation. To quantify this capability, we propose a metric suite led by Average Turns to Clarify (ATC) and Key Question Coverage (KQC), which measure not just the correctness of the generated code, but the efficiency and precision of the collaboration. Our experiments on eleven state-of-the-art agents reveal a stark reality: while models like GPT-5-Coder excel at coding, they often lack the strategic communication skills required for efficient partnership. ClarEval thus serves as a crucial roadmap for bridging the gap between strong coders and capable collaborators.The code is available at https://github.com/JialinLi13/ClarEval

</details>


### [7] [Towards the Systematic Testing of Regular Expression Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00311)
*Berk Çakar,Dongyoon Lee,James C. Davis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ReTest框架结合语法感知模糊测试和蜕变测试，系统测试正则表达式引擎，相比现有方法获得3倍边覆盖率并发现3个新内存安全缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正则表达式引擎存在bug和漏洞，现有测试方法存在局限：差分测试受方言差异影响，传统模糊测试生成无效输入仅测试解析逻辑而非匹配内部逻辑。

Method: 结合语法感知模糊测试（提高代码覆盖率）和蜕变测试（生成方言无关的测试预言），调查22个引擎测试实践，分析1007个bug和156个CVE，基于Kleene代数推导16个蜕变关系。

Result: 在PCRE上的初步评估显示，ReTest比现有模糊测试方法获得3倍边覆盖率，并发现3个新的内存安全缺陷。

Conclusion: 最终目标是帮助正则表达式引擎开发者识别bug，而不依赖跨实现的一致性标准，描述了下一步工作计划。

Abstract: Software engineers use regular expressions (regexes) across a wide range of domains and tasks. To support regexes, software projects must integrate a regex engine, whether provided natively by the language runtime (e.g., Python's re) or included as an external dependency (e.g., PCRE). However, these engines may contain bugs and introduce vulnerabilities. A common strategy for testing regex engines involves differential testing -- comparing outputs across different implementations. However, this approach is concerning because regex syntax and semantics vary significantly between dialects (e.g., POSIX vs. PCRE). Fuzzing is also utilized to ease testing of feature-rich regex implementations to expose defects, but naive byte-level mutations generate syntactically invalid inputs that exercise only parsing logic, not matching internals.
  In this work, we describe our progress towards ReTest, a framework that systematically tests regular expression engines by combining grammar-aware fuzzing for high code coverage with metamorphic testing to generate dialect-independent test oracles. So far, we have surveyed testing practices across 22 regex engines, analyzed 1,007 regex engine bugs and 156 CVEs to characterize failure modes, and curated 16 metamorphic relations for regexes derived from Kleene algebra. Our preliminary evaluation on PCRE shows that ReTest achieves 3x higher edge coverage than existing fuzzing approaches and has identified three new memory safety defects. We conclude by describing our next steps toward our ultimate goal: helping regex engine developers identify bugs without depending on a consistent cross-implementation standard.

</details>


### [8] [Does My README File Need To Be Updated? Exploring LLM-Based README Maintenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00489)
*Haoyu Gao,Hong Yi Lin,Christoph Treude,Gregory Gay,Mansooreh Zahedi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出基于LLM的轻量级方法，通过PR分析自动检测README是否需要更新，定位具体更新位置并提供理由，减少开源项目文档过时问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源项目中"过时"文档是开发者面临的最常见且严重的问题之一，现有解决方案要么依赖高度工程化的特定组件方法，要么使用不适合增量维护的生成方法

Method: 提出轻量级LLM驱动方法，在人工参与的工作流程中实现精确、局部化的README更新。给定PR时，管道判断是否需要更新，如果需要则识别具体更新位置并提供基于触发事件的理由

Result: 在714个流行仓库的27,772个PR上评估显示高精度和实用性。定性失败案例分析提供改进方向。回顾性研究和案例研究表明工具能有效识别被忽略的需要README更新的PR

Conclusion: 该方法有助于减轻文档过时风险，为实践者和研究者提供具体启示，强调需要进一步探索有效交互模式将文档更新工具整合到OSS开发工作流中

Abstract: The README file serves as a critical source of information for gaining an overview and helping developers onboard to an Open Source Software (OSS) project. Yet, documentation issues persist; in particular, ``outdated'' documentation is perceived by developers as one of the most frequent and severe challenges with gaining project understanding. While previous studies have aimed to mitigate this problem, they typically either rely on highly-engineered solutions focused on specific code components or employ generative methods that are ineffective for incremental maintenance. In this study, we propose a lightweight Large Language Model (LLM)-driven approach to facilitate precise, localised README file updates within a human-in-the-loop workflow. Specifically, given a pull request (PR), our pipeline determines whether an update is necessary; if so, it identifies the precise locations where updates should be applied and provides a justification based on the triggering events.
  Our evaluation on 27,772 PRs across 714 popular repositories demonstrates high precision and utility. Furthermore, we performed a qualitative failure case analysis to provide deeper insights and directions for improvement. We also conducted a retrospective study on 20 sampled repositories, complemented by a case study with a developer of a large OSS project. These evaluations demonstrate that the tool effectively identifies overlooked PRs requiring README updates, thereby helping to mitigate the risk of outdated documentation. Finally, we provide concrete implications for practitioners and researchers, highlighting the need to further explore effective interaction patterns to incorporate documentation update tools into the OSS development workflow.

</details>


### [9] [Are LLMs Reliable Code Reviewers? Systematic Overcorrection in Requirement Conformance Judgement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00539)
*Haolin Jin,Huaming Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LLMs在匹配代码与自然语言需求时存在系统性失败，详细提示设计反而导致更高的误判率，作者提出基于修复引导的验证过滤器来提升可靠性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在软件开发中广泛用于代码验证，但尚不清楚它们是否能可靠地判断代码是否符合自然语言任务描述，这关系到代码的健壮性和准确性

Method: 使用广泛采用的基准测试和统一提示设计，分析LLMs在代码匹配任务中的失败机制；提出Fix-guided Verification Filter，将模型提出的修复作为可执行的反事实证据，通过基准测试和规范约束增强测试来验证原始和修订实现

Result: LLMs经常将正确的代码实现误分类为不合规或有缺陷；更详细的提示设计（特别是需要解释和提出修正的）导致更高的误判率；基于修复引导的验证过滤器能有效提升判断可靠性

Conclusion: 揭示了LLM基于代码审查能力的先前未被充分探索的局限性，为在自动化审查和开发流程中集成LLM审查器提供了实用指导，强调需要安全防护机制

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become essential tools in software development, widely used for requirements engineering, code generation and review tasks. Software engineers often rely on LLMs to verify if code implementation satisfy task requirements, thereby ensuring code robustness and accuracy. However, it remains unclear whether LLMs can reliably determine code against the given task descriptions, which is usually in a form of natural language specifications. In this paper, we uncover a systematic failure of LLMs in matching code to natural language requirements. Specifically, with widely adopted benchmarks and unified prompts design, we demonstrate that LLMs frequently misclassify correct code implementation as non-compliant or defective. Surprisingly, we find that more detailed prompt design, particularly with those requiring explanations and proposed corrections, leads to higher misjudgment rates, highlighting critical reliability issues for LLM-based code assistants. We further analyze the mechanisms driving these failures and evaluate the reliability of rationale-required judgments. Building on these findings, we propose a Fix-guided Verification Filter that treats the model proposed fix as executable counterfactual evidence, and validates the original and revised implementations using benchmark tests and spec-constrained augmented tests. Our results expose previously under-explored limitations in LLM-based code review capabilities, and provide practical guidance for integrating LLM-based reviewers with safeguards in automated review and development pipelines.

</details>


### [10] [Historian: Reducing Manual Validation in APR Benchmarking via Evidence-Based Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00649)
*Sahand Moslemi,Mayasah Lami,Anil Koyuncu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Historian：基于历史补丁知识库的自动化补丁正确性评估框架，利用大语言模型进行多参考比较，减少人工验证工作量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化程序修复（APR）生成的补丁正确性评估存在瓶颈：人工验证劳动密集且有限，现有自动化评估方法依赖不透明的预测模型，将每个补丁视为新颖，重复评估语义冗余的补丁

Method: 提出Historian框架，利用大语言模型对补丁进行多参考比较，对照历史验证过的补丁知识库，生成可追溯、基于证据的判定，同时保守地将新颖案例标记为未知

Result: 在留一工具评估中，Historian达到95.0%的覆盖率和88.4%的准确率，将人工验证减少到补丁的5%；作为基于证据的预过滤器，将独立APCA工具的准确率提高达21.8%，实现86.2%的整体准确率和100%覆盖率的混合管道

Conclusion: 2020-2024年工具生成补丁的纵向分析表明，修复尝试中的冗余很常见，许多补丁重复发现已建立的修复方案，这加强了基于证据的APR评估的可持续性

Abstract: Assessing the correctness of patches generated by Automated Program Repair (APR) is a major bottleneck. Manual validation is labor-intensive and limited: exact matching overlooks valid variants, while semantic inspection is subjective and hard to reproduce. Existing Automated Patch Correctness Assessment (APCA) often relies on opaque predictive models that treat each patch as novel, repeatedly re-assessing semantically redundant patches.
  Our analysis of a large corpus of tool-generated patches reveals a duality: about 39% of unique correct patches are syntactic clones, suggesting opportunities for automation, yet about 65% of bugs have multiple distinct correct fixes, making single-reference assessment insufficient.
  We present Historian, a framework that leverages Large Language Models to perform multi-reference comparisons against a knowledge base of historically validated patches, producing traceable, evidence-based verdicts while conservatively isolating novel cases as Unknown.
  In leave-one-tool-out evaluation, Historian achieves 95.0% coverage with 88.4% accuracy, reducing manual validation to 5% of patches. As an evidence-based pre-filter, enhancing the accuracy of standalone APCA tools by up to 21.8% and enabling a hybrid pipeline with 86.2% overall accuracy and 100% coverage. A longitudinal analysis of tool-generated patches (2020-2024) shows that redundancy in repair attempts is common, indicating that many patches repeatedly rediscover established ones and strengthening the sustainability of evidence-based APR assessment.

</details>


### [11] [Taking a Closer Look at Warnings Generated by PMD and SonarQube, their Rules and Compliance to Established Coding Standards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00821)
*Lakmal Deshapriya,Sherlock A. Licorish,Brendon J. Woodford*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究分析了静态代码分析工具PMD和SonarQube在Stack Overflow和Apache Tomcat代码上的假阳性警告，发现只有少数规则产生假阳性，且消除与编码标准冲突的规则能显著降低假阳性率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有静态代码分析工具存在高假阳性和假阴性率，但缺乏高质量数据集来学习警告模式。现有研究未充分利用此类数据集，也未识别易产生假阳性的规则范围及其与编码标准的符合性。

Method: 分析Stack Overflow和Apache Tomcat代码以捕获代码长度和风格的多样性，使用PMD和SonarQube检测假阳性警告，依据已建立的编码标准对工具输出进行标注，深入分析易产生假阳性的规则、原因及SCA规则与标准之间的差异。

Result: 1) 只有少数SCA规则产生假阳性，在四个数据集上假阳性率范围为4.64%到18.45%；2) 消除与已建立标准冲突的规则能显著降低假阳性率；3) 发现工具与已建立标准之间存在差异。

Conclusion: 建议进一步研究工具与标准之间的差距，包括使用机器学习方法标注更大规模的数据集。

Abstract: Context: Static code analysis (SCA) tools play a vital role in software development, reducing the cost and time required for code reviews. However, high false-positive and false-negative rates are reported for the best tools in the community. Accordingly, studies often aim to develop datasets for learning SCA warning patterns to reduce false results. These datasets are meant to possess high-quality and high-volume in covering the full range of faults/rules that typically result in false warnings and be compliant with established coding standards. However, existing studies have not utilised such datasets or identified the breadth of rules that are prone to false positives and their compliance to coding standards. Objectives: We analysed code from Stack Overflow and Apache Tomcat to capture variations in code length and style in detecting false-positive warnings from best-performing tools PMD and SonarQube, addressing this gap. Method: In deriving false-positive warnings, outcomes from the tools were labelled using established coding standards. Deeper analyses were then conducted to identify the rules that are prone to false-positives, reasons for these, and agreement/gaps between SCA rules and established standards. Results: Among our main outcomes, we observe that only a few SCA rules generate false positives, ranging from 4.64% to 18.45% across four datasets. Additionally, eliminating rules that contradict established standards significantly reduce the false-positive rate. Additionally, our findings reveal discrepancies between tools and established standards. Conclusion: Given the evidence established in this study, we recommend further investigations into gaps between tools and established standards, including the use of machine learning approaches to annotate larger datasets.

</details>


### [12] [PARCER as an Operational Contract to Reduce Variance, Cost, and Risk in LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00856)
*Elzo Brito dos Santos Filho*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: PARCER是一个工程框架，通过声明式YAML操作契约将非结构化LLM交互转化为版本化可执行工件，解决LLM系统的随机方差、长上下文利用不足和治理挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM系统在需要绝对一致性、可审计性以及成本和延迟可预测控制的场景中面临治理挑战。现有问题包括：1) 模型判断的随机方差（常被视为"系统性噪声"）；2) 长输入中上下文利用的显著退化，关键信息在提示中间被稀释时出现严重损失。

Method: PARCER框架作为声明式"操作契约"在YAML中实现，将非结构化交互转化为版本化可执行工件。它实施严格治理结构，包含七个操作阶段：引入受法律判决启发的决策卫生实践来减轻噪声、自适应令牌预算、用于上下文保存的正式化恢复路径（回退机制）以及通过OpenTelemetry实现的系统性可观测性。

Result: PARCER提供了从简单"提示工程"到"具有可治理性的上下文工程"的必要过渡，通过结构化治理框架解决了LLM系统的关键限制，实现了版本控制、可审计性和可预测性。

Conclusion: PARCER框架代表了LLM系统治理的重要工程进展，通过将非结构化交互转化为结构化、版本化、可执行的契约，解决了随机方差、上下文利用和治理控制等核心挑战，为需要严格技术要求的应用场景提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Systems based on Large Language Models (LLMs) have become formidable tools for automating research and software production. However, their governance remains a challenge when technical requirements demand absolute consistency, auditability, and predictable control over cost and latency. Recent literature highlights two phenomena that aggravate this scenario: the stochastic variance inherent in the model's judgment (often treated as "systemic noise") and the substantial degradation of context utilization in long inputs, with critical losses when decisive information is diluted in the middle of the prompt. This article proposes PARCER as an engineering response to these limitations. The framework acts as a declarative "operational contract" in YAML, transforming unstructured interactions into versioned and executable artifacts. PARCER imposes strict governance structured into seven operational phases, introducing decision hygiene practices inspired by legal judgments to mitigate noise, adaptive token budgeting, formalized recovery routes (fallbacks) for context preservation, and systemic observability via OpenTelemetry. The objective of this work is to present the conceptual and technical architecture of PARCER, positioning it as a necessary transition from simple "prompt engineering" to "context engineering with governable governance".

</details>


### [13] [Detect Repair Verify for Securing LLM Generated Code: A Multi-Language Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00897)
*Cheng Cheng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究针对LLM生成软件的安全工作流，填补了项目级基准、管道级有效性评估、检测报告可靠性验证和修复可信度评估四个研究空白，并提出了新的基准数据集和评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM生成软件的安全实践通常采用检测-修复-验证（DRV）循环，但存在四个关键研究空白：缺乏项目级可执行基准、管道级有效性证据不足、检测报告可靠性不明、修复可信度和副作用不确定。

Method: 引入新的基准数据集，包含可运行的Web应用项目、功能测试和安全测试，支持项目、需求和函数三个提示粒度。评估比较生成式、单次DRV和有界迭代DRV变体，在可比预算约束下使用测试验证测量安全正确产出。

Result: 通过测试验证测量安全正确产出，分析中间产物以评估报告可操作性，识别修复后失败模式如回归、语义漂移和新引入的安全问题。

Conclusion: 该研究为LLM生成软件的安全工作流提供了系统评估框架，填补了项目级基准和管道级有效性评估的研究空白，有助于理解DRV循环在实际应用中的效果和局限性。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to produce runnable software. In practice, security is often addressed through a Detect--Repair--Verify (DRV) loop that detects issues, applies fixes, and verifies the result. This work studies such a workflow for project-level artifacts and addresses four gaps: L1, the lack of project-level benchmarks with executable function and security tests; L2, limited evidence on pipeline-level effectiveness beyond studying detection or repair alone; L3, unclear reliability of detection reports as repair guidance; and L4, uncertain repair trustworthiness and side effects under verification. A new benchmark dataset\footnote{https://github.com/Hahappyppy2024/EmpricalVDR} is introduced, consisting of runnable web-application projects paired with functional tests and targeted security tests, and supporting three prompt granularities at the project, requirement, and function level. The evaluation compares generation-only, single-pass DRV, and bounded iterative DRV variants under comparable budget constraints. Outcomes are measured by secure and correct yield using test-grounded verification, and intermediate artifacts are analyzed to assess report actionability and post-repair failure modes such as regressions, semantic drift, and newly introduced security issues.

</details>


### [14] [Sustainable Code Generation Using Large Language Models: A Systematic Literature Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00989)
*Sabiya Banu Masthan Ali,Oussema Kirmani,Syed Muhammad Danish,Gautam Srivastava*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过系统文献综述分析了LLM生成代码的可持续性研究现状，发现该领域研究有限且碎片化，缺乏统一的评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在软件工程中广泛应用，但现有研究主要关注模型训练和推理的能耗，而忽视了生成代码本身的可持续性。低效代码会增加CPU使用、内存消耗、执行时间和整体能耗，随着LLM生成代码在真实项目中普及，即使微小低效也会导致长期环境成本。

Method: 采用系统文献综述方法，分析选定的主要研究，探讨LLM生成可持续代码的能力。研究内容包括：可持续性如何定义和测量（指标和评估策略），以及微调和提示工程等技术是否影响生成代码的可持续性。通过结构化分析，根据方法论、评估实践和实验设置对研究进行分类。

Result: 研究发现该领域研究相对有限且碎片化，缺乏广泛接受的LLM生成代码可持续性测量或基准测试框架。现有研究在方法论、评估标准和实验设置上存在差异。

Conclusion: 需要更清晰的定义、标准化的评估方法和系统研究，以支持环境友好的AI辅助软件工程。建立统一的可持续性评估框架对于减少LLM生成代码的环境影响至关重要。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are widely used in software engineering to generate, complete, translate, and fix code, improving developer productivity. While most research focuses on the energy consumption and carbon emissions of model training and inference, far less attention has been given to the sustainability of the code these models produce. The efficiency of generated code affects the long-term environmental impact of software systems. Inefficient code can increase CPU usage, memory consumption, execution time, and overall energy use during deployment and operation. As LLM-generated code becomes more common in real-world projects, even small inefficiencies can lead to high environmental costs over time. This paper examines existing research on the sustainability of code generated by LLMs. We conduct a systematic literature review to analyze selected primary studies and investigate the extent to which LLMs are capable of producing sustainable code. In addition, we examine how sustainability is defined and measured in this context, including the metrics and evaluation strategies used to assess energy efficiency and resource usage. We also explore whether techniques such as fine-tuning and prompt engineering influence the sustainability of generated code. Through a structured analysis of the selected studies, we categorize research efforts based on their methodological approaches, evaluation practices, and experimental settings. The findings indicate that research in this area remains relatively limited and fragmented, with no widely accepted framework for measuring or benchmarking the sustainability of LLM-generated code. These observations highlight the need for clearer definitions, standardized evaluation methods, and systematic research to support environmentally friendly AI-assisted software engineering.

</details>


### [15] [SWE-Adept: An LLM-Based Agentic Framework for Deep Codebase Analysis and Structured Issue Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01327)
*Kang He,Kaushik Roy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SWE-Adept是一个基于LLM的双智能体框架，通过定位智能体识别问题相关代码位置，解决智能体实现相应修复，专门针对仓库级软件工程任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在独立编程任务上表现良好，但在仓库级软件工程方面仍存在困难，需要深度代码库导航和有效的上下文管理进行准确定位，以及系统化的迭代、测试驱动的代码修改方法来解决复杂问题。

Method: 提出SWE-Adept双智能体框架：1) 定位智能体采用智能体导向的深度优先搜索，选择性遍历代码依赖关系以最小化问题无关内容；2) 解决智能体采用自适应规划和结构化问题解决，配备专门工具用于进度跟踪和基于Git的版本控制，通过共享工作内存存储按执行步骤索引的代码状态检查点。

Result: 在SWE-Bench Lite和SWE-Bench Pro上的实验表明，SWE-Adept在问题定位和解决方面持续优于先前方法，端到端解决率提升高达4.7%。

Conclusion: SWE-Adept通过双智能体架构和专门设计的工具，有效解决了仓库级软件工程中的代码定位和系统化修复问题，显著提升了LLM在复杂软件工程任务上的性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit strong performance on self-contained programming tasks. However, they still struggle with repository-level software engineering (SWE), which demands (1) deep codebase navigation with effective context management for accurate localization, and (2) systematic approaches for iterative, test-driven code modification to resolve issues. To address these challenges, we propose SWE-Adept, an LLM-based two-agent framework where a localization agent identifies issue-relevant code locations and a resolution agent implements the corresponding fixes. For issue localization, we introduce agent-directed depth-first search that selectively traverses code dependencies. This minimizes issue-irrelevant content in the agent's context window and improves localization accuracy. For issue resolution, we employ adaptive planning and structured problem solving. We equip the agent with specialized tools for progress tracking and Git-based version control. These tools interface with a shared working memory that stores code-state checkpoints indexed by execution steps, facilitating precise checkpoint retrieval. This design enables reliable agent-driven version-control operations for systematic issue resolution, including branching to explore alternative solutions and reverting failed edits. Experiments on SWE-Bench Lite and SWE-Bench Pro demonstrate that SWE-Adept consistently outperforms prior approaches in both issue localization and resolution, improving the end-to-end resolve rate by up to 4.7%.

</details>


### [16] [PymooLab: An Open-Source Visual Analytics Framework for Multi-Objective Optimization using LLM-Based Code Generation and MCDM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01345)
*Thiago Santos,Sebastiao Xavier,Luiz Gustavo de Oliveira Carneiro,Gustavo de Souza*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: PymooLab：基于pymoo的可视化分析环境，通过统一配置、执行监控和决策支持降低多目标优化的使用门槛


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流进化框架（如pymoo）依赖命令式编程进行问题定义、算法配置和后验分析，这对缺乏软件工程训练的实践者构成障碍，并影响实验的可重复性

Method: 开发基于pymoo的开源可视化分析环境PymooLab，采用解耦的面向对象架构，支持LLM辅助代码生成、交互式多准则决策工具，并利用JAX进行高性能计算加速

Result: PymooLab实现了配置、执行监控和正式决策支持的统一工作流，自动记录超参数、评估预算和随机种子，保持与pymoo生态系统的兼容性，同时提高高维评估的可扩展性

Conclusion: 该框架结合可视化实验、基于LLM的建模和确定性编排，缩小了严谨运筹学与领域专家实际可访问性之间的差距

Abstract: Multi-objective optimization is now a core paradigm in engineering design and scientific discovery. Yet mainstream evolutionary frameworks, including \textit{pymoo}, still depend on imperative coding for problem definition, algorithm configuration, and post-hoc analysis. That requirement creates a non-trivial barrier for practitioners without strong software-engineering training and often complicates reproducible experimentation. We address this gap through PymooLab, an open-source visual analytics environment built on top of \textit{pymoo}. The platform unifies configuration, execution monitoring, and formal decision support in a single reproducible workflow that automatically records hyperparameters, evaluation budgets, and random seeds. Its decoupled object-oriented architecture preserves compatibility with the base ecosystem while enabling LLM-assisted code generation for rapid model formulation. The interface also embeds interactive Multi-Criteria Decision Making (MCDM) tools, which reduces the cognitive burden of Pareto-front inspection. For computationally intensive studies, PymooLab relies on the native \textit{pymoo} acceleration pathway through JAX, improving scalability in high-dimensional evaluations. Overall, the framework combines visual experimentation, LLM-based modeling, and deterministic orchestration to narrow the gap between rigorous operations research and practical accessibility for domain experts. Source code is publicly available at https://github.com/METISBR/pymoolab.

</details>


### [17] [Production-Grade AI Coding System for Client-Side Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01460)
*Ruihan Wang,Chencheng Guo,Guangjing Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种面向客户端开发的生产级AI编码系统，通过结构化多阶段流程整合设计图、需求文档和工程知识，提升代码生成与产品需求的匹配度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实客户端开发中部署基于大语言模型的代码生成面临三大挑战：输入异构性、严格工程约束以及产品需求文档中表达的复杂交互逻辑。现有设计到代码方法通常专注于视觉翻译或单次生成，难以可靠地将生成代码与生产需求对齐。

Method: 系统采用结构化多阶段流水线，将Figma设计图、自然语言产品需求文档和领域特定工程知识整合为明确的中间产物，实现可控规划和增量代码生成。通过将需求理解锚定在具体UI组件上，提升产品需求与实现之间的对齐度。

Result: 在基于生产客户端项目的专有但真实数据集上评估显示：领域特定适配显著提高了需求理解准确性；端到端评估表明在真实案例中实现了高UI保真度和稳健的交互逻辑实现。

Conclusion: 结构化、以产物为中心的流水线为生产级AI编码系统提供了实用基础，能够有效应对工业约束下的客户端开发挑战。

Abstract: Deploying large language model-based code generation in real-world client-side development remains challenging due to heterogeneous inputs, strict engineering constraints, and complex interaction logic expressed in product requirement documents (PRDs). Existing design-to-code approaches often focus on visual translation or single-shot generation, and struggle to reliably align generated code with production requirements.
  This paper presents a production-grade AI coding system designed for client-side development under realistic industrial constraints. The system adopts a structured, multi-stage pipeline that integrates Figma designs, natural-language PRDs, and domain-specific engineering knowledge into explicit intermediate artifacts, enabling controlled planning and incremental code generation. By grounding PRD understanding in concrete UI components, the system improves alignment between product requirements and implementation.
  We evaluate the system on proprietary but realistic datasets derived from production client-side projects. Results show that domain-specific adaptation significantly improves PRD understanding accuracy, while end-to-end evaluations demonstrate high UI fidelity and robust implementation of interaction logic in real-world cases. These findings suggest that structured, artifact-centric pipelines provide a practical foundation for production-grade AI coding systems.

</details>


### [18] [Inference-Time Safety For Code LLMs Via Retrieval-Augmented Revision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01494)
*Manisha Mukherjee,Vincent J. Hellendoorn*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一种基于检索增强生成的安全机制，在推理时通过检索Stack Overflow安全讨论来指导LLM代码修订，提升代码生成的安全性、可解释性和适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在代码生成中缺乏安全推理透明度，对不断演变的漏洞模式脆弱，且基于静态数据集训练的模型难以适应新发现的安全漏洞或变化的安全标准，导致重复生成不安全代码。

Method: 采用检索增强生成方法，在推理时从精心策划的Stack Overflow知识库中检索相关安全风险和讨论，利用这些信息指导LLM进行代码修订。强调三个可信赖性方面：可解释性（基于专家社区解释的透明安全干预）、鲁棒性（无需重新训练即可适应变化的安全实践）和安全对齐（在部署前进行实时干预）。

Result: 在真实世界和基准数据集上，相比仅使用提示的方法，该方法提高了LLM生成代码的安全性，同时静态分析未检测到引入新漏洞。

Conclusion: 基于原则的检索增强推理时干预可作为补充机制提升基于LLM的代码生成安全性，并突显了社区知识在支持可信赖AI部署中的持续价值。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed for code generation in high-stakes software development, yet their limited transparency in security reasoning and brittleness to evolving vulnerability patterns raise critical trustworthiness concerns. Models trained on static datasets cannot readily adapt to newly discovered vulnerabilities or changing security standards without retraining, leading to the repeated generation of unsafe code.
  We present a principled approach to trustworthy code generation by design that operates as an inference-time safety mechanism. Our approach employs retrieval-augmented generation to surface relevant security risks in generated code and retrieve related security discussions from a curated Stack Overflow knowledge base, which are then used to guide an LLM during code revision. This design emphasizes three aspects relevant to trustworthiness: (1) interpretability, through transparent safety interventions grounded in expert community explanations; (2) robustness, by allowing adaptation to evolving security practices without model retraining; and (3) safety alignment, through real-time intervention before unsafe code reaches deployment.
  Across real-world and benchmark datasets, our approach improves the security of LLM-generated code compared to prompting alone, while introducing no new vulnerabilities as measured by static analysis. These results suggest that principled, retrieval-augmented inference-time interventions can serve as a complementary mechanism for improving the safety of LLM-based code generation, and highlight the ongoing value of community knowledge in supporting trustworthy AI deployment.

</details>


### [19] [ICSE 2022 Sustainability Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01541)
*Patricia Lago,Marcel Boehme,Markus Funke*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 对ICSE 2022参会者的问卷调查显示，约19%的受访者认为学术会议带来的碳排放超过了线下参会的收益，凸显了学术界对会议碳足迹的日益关注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着学术会议碳足迹问题日益受到关注，需要评估线下参会带来的收益是否足以抵消社区产生的碳排放，从而为学术会议的可持续发展提供决策依据。

Method: 采用会后问卷调查方法，对参加第44届国际软件工程会议（ICSE 2022）的53名参会者进行调查，从可持续性角度收集他们对会议体验和碳足迹平衡的反馈。

Result: 在42名回答相关问题的受访者中，8人（约19%）认为社区的碳足迹没有被线下参会的好处所抵消，表明相当一部分参会者对当前会议模式的可持续性存在担忧。

Conclusion: 学术会议需要更系统地评估碳足迹与参会收益之间的平衡，考虑采用混合会议模式等可持续方案，以在保持学术交流质量的同时减少环境影响。

Abstract: The carbon footprint of academic conferences becomes a topic of increasing debate. It is important to consider whether the benefits derived from attending conferences in person outweigh the community's carbon footprint. Therefore, we need to evaluate the overall ecological consequences in relation to the perceived advantages. To that extent, we conducted a post-conference questionnaire survey among participants of the 44th International Conference on Software Engineering (ICSE) 2022 in Pittsburgh, USA, seeking their feedback about the conference and experience from a sustainability perspective. In total, 53 participants filled out our survey. Overall, 8 of 42 respondents felt that the community's carbon footprint was not offset by the benefits of in-person attendance.

</details>


### [20] [MigMate: A VS Code Extension for LLM-based Library Migration of Python Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01596)
*Matthias Kebede,May Mahmoud,Mohayeminul Islam,Sarah Nadi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 开发了基于VS Code的IDE插件MigMate，集成自动化库迁移功能，提供交互式体验，显著减少迁移时间并提升可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代软件开发严重依赖第三方库，当库过时或不适合项目时需要迁移。手动迁移耗时耗力，需要将自动化迁移工具集成到开发环境中，提供更好的用户体验。

Method: 在先前开发的命令行LLM迁移工具MigrateLib基础上，开发了开源的VS Code IDE插件MigMate，将自动化迁移过程集成到开发者的现有开发环境中，提供交互式体验让开发者查看和确认更改。

Result: 初步用户研究表明，使用插件能持续减少完成库迁移任务的时间，在系统可用性量表上得分很高。

Conclusion: MigMate成功将自动化库迁移集成到IDE中，通过交互式体验提高了迁移效率和用户体验，是库迁移工具的重要进展。

Abstract: Modern software relies heavily on third-party software libraries to streamline the development process. The act of switching one library for a similar counterpart, called library migration, naturally occurs as libraries become outdated or unsuitable for the project. Manually migrating from one library to another is a time-consuming task. Our previous research developed MigrateLib, a command-line LLM-based migration tool that can automate the complete migration process. In this paper, we present our open-source VS Code IDE plugin, MigMate, that builds on MigrateLib by integrating the automated migration process into the developer's existing development environment. MigMate provides an interactive experience, allowing developers to view and confirm changes before they are applied. A preliminary user study shows that plugin usage consistently reduces the time taken to complete a library migration task, and it scores highly on the System Usability Scale.

</details>


### [21] [MetaRCA: A Generalizable Root Cause Analysis Framework for Cloud-Native Systems Powered by Meta Causal Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02032)
*Shuai Liang,Pengfei Chen,Bozhe Tian,Gou Tan,Maohong Xu,Youjun Qu,Yahui Zhao,Yiduo Shang,Chongkang Tan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MetaRCA：一个用于云原生系统的可泛化根因分析框架，通过元因果图知识库和轻量级在线推理实现高效故障定位


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云原生系统的动态性和复杂性给根因分析带来重大挑战。现有基于因果关系的RCA方法面临三个相互关联的挑战：对系统复杂性的扩展性差、跨不同系统拓扑的泛化能力脆弱、领域知识整合不足。这些限制阻碍了稳健高效RCA解决方案的发展。

Method: MetaRCA框架包含离线构建元因果图（MCG）和在线轻量级推理两个阶段。离线阶段采用证据驱动算法，系统性地融合大型语言模型知识、历史故障报告和可观测性数据构建可重用知识库。在线阶段根据当前上下文动态实例化MCG为局部化图，利用实时数据对因果链接进行加权和剪枝，实现精确根因定位。

Result: 在252个公共故障和59个生产故障上评估，MetaRCA表现出最先进的性能。在服务级准确率上超越最强基线29个百分点，在指标级准确率上超越48个百分点。随着系统复杂性增加，性能优势更加明显，开销呈近线性扩展。关键的是，MetaRCA展现出强大的跨系统泛化能力，在不同系统上保持超过80%的准确率。

Conclusion: MetaRCA通过元因果图知识库和轻量级在线推理的创新设计，有效解决了云原生系统根因分析中的可扩展性、泛化性和领域知识整合挑战，为实际部署提供了稳健高效的解决方案。

Abstract: The dynamics and complexity of cloud-native systems present significant challenges for Root Cause Analysis (RCA). While causality-based RCA methods have shown significant progress in recent years, their practical adoption is fundamentally limited by three intertwined challenges: poor scalability against system complexity, brittle generalization across different system topologies, and inadequate integration of domain knowledge. These limitations create a vicious cycle, hindering the development of robust and efficient RCA solutions. This paper introduces MetaRCA, a generalizable RCA framework for cloud-native systems. MetaRCA first constructs a Meta Causal Graph (MCG) offline, a reusable knowledge base defined at the metadata level. To build the MCG, we propose an evidence-driven algorithm that systematically fuses knowledge from Large Language Models (LLMs), historical fault reports, and observability data. When a fault occurs, MetaRCA performs a lightweight online inference by dynamically instantiating the MCG into a localized graph based on the current context, and then leverages real-time data to weight and prune causal links for precise root cause localization. Evaluated on 252 public and 59 production failures, MetaRCA demonstrates state-of-the-art performance. It surpasses the strongest baseline by 29 percentage points in service-level and 48 percentage points in metric-level accuracy. This performance advantage widens as system complexity increases, with its overhead scaling near-linearly. Crucially, MetaRCA shows robust cross-system generalization, maintaining over 80% accuracy across diverse systems.

</details>


### [22] [Generative AI in Software Testing: Current Trends and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02141)
*Tanish Singla,Qusay H. Mahmoud*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文探讨了如何将生成式AI集成到软件测试系统中以提升测试覆盖率、效率和成本效益，特别关注其在IoT测试领域的应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前软件测试系统面临效率、覆盖率和成本方面的挑战，而生成式AI技术为改进这些方面提供了新的可能性，特别是在测试用例生成、验证和优先级排序等关键环节。

Method: 通过文献综述方法，系统分析AI在软件测试中的现有应用，重点研究生成式AI在输入生成、预言生成、数据生成、测试数据创建和测试用例优先级排序等具体任务中的应用方法，包括提示工程和微调等效率提升技术。

Result: 研究表明生成式AI能够显著改进软件测试流程，特别是在测试用例生成和验证方面，通过适当的工程化方法可以提升AI系统的效率，为IoT等快速发展的测试领域提供更健壮、全面的测试结果。

Conclusion: 生成式AI有潜力显著提升软件测试的效率、效果和可靠性，但需要持续的创新和有针对性的开发工作来克服现有障碍，特别是在IoT测试等新兴领域，为从业者和研究者提供了有价值的见解和建议。

Abstract: This paper investigates current software testing systems and explores how artificial intelligence, specifically Generative AI, can be integrated to enhance these systems. It begins by examining different types of AI systems and focuses on the potential of Generative AI to transform software testing processes by improving test coverage, increasing efficiency, and reducing costs. The study provides a com-prehensive overview of the current applications of AI in software testing, emphasizing its significant contributions in areas such as test case generation and validation. Through an extensive literature re-view, it highlights how Generative AI can streamline these processes, resulting in more robust and thorough testing outcomes. The paper also examines methods to improve the efficiency of Generative AI systems, such as prompt engineering and fine-tuning. Additionally, it explores the use of AI in specific tasks, including input generation, oracle generation, data generation, test data creation, and test case prioritization. By analyzing the current landscape and identifying both the opportunities and challenges in integrating Generative AI, this paper provides valuable insights and recommendations for practitioners and researchers. It underscores the need for ongoing advancements and targeted development efforts to overcome existing hurdles and fully leverage AI's capabilities. The findings further show that with continued innovation and careful implementation, Generative AI has the potential to significantly enhance the efficiency, effectiveness, and reliability of software testing, particularly in the rapidly evolving field of IoT testing.

</details>
