### [23] [Reverse CAPTCHA: Evaluating LLM Susceptibility to Invisible Unicode Instruction Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00164)
*Marcus Graves*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Reverse CAPTCHA框架通过嵌入不可见Unicode指令测试LLM的合规性，发现工具使用显著增强合规性，不同模型提供商对编码方案有偏好，显式解码指令可大幅提升合规率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CAPTCHA用于区分人类和机器，而本研究利用LLM能感知人类不可见的Unicode控制字符这一能力差距，探索通过不可见Unicode载荷进行提示注入的攻击面。

Method: 评估五个来自两个提供商的模型，使用两种编码方案（零宽度二进制和Unicode标签），四个提示级别，两种载荷框架，启用或禁用工具使用，共收集8,308个模型输出进行分析。

Result: 工具使用显著增强合规性（Cohen's h高达1.37）；OpenAI模型偏好零宽度二进制解码，Anthropic模型偏好Unicode标签；显式解码指令可将单个模型和编码的合规性提升高达95个百分点；所有成对模型差异均具有统计显著性。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了通过不可见Unicode载荷进行提示注入的未充分探索的攻击面，强调了LLM安全评估中考虑不可见字符的重要性。

Abstract: We introduce Reverse CAPTCHA, an evaluation framework that tests whether large language models follow invisible Unicode-encoded instructions embedded in otherwise normal-looking text. Unlike traditional CAPTCHAs that distinguish humans from machines, our benchmark exploits a capability gap: models can perceive Unicode control characters that are invisible to human readers. We evaluate five models from two providers across two encoding schemes (zero-width binary and Unicode Tags), four hint levels, two payload framings, and with tool use enabled or disabled. Across 8,308 model outputs, we find that tool use dramatically amplifies compliance (Cohen's h up to 1.37, a large effect), that models exhibit provider-specific encoding preferences (OpenAI models decode zero-width binary; Anthropic models prefer Unicode Tags), and that explicit decoding instructions increase compliance by up to 95 percentage points within a single model and encoding. All pairwise model differences are statistically significant (p < 0.05, Bonferroni-corrected). These results highlight an underexplored attack surface for prompt injection via invisible Unicode payloads.

</details>


### [24] [Hidden in the Metadata: Stealth Poisoning Attacks on Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00172)
*Kennedy Edemacu,Mohammad Mahdi Shokri*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MM-MEPA是一种针对多模态检索增强生成系统的元数据投毒攻击，仅通过操纵图像-文本条目的元数据而不修改视觉内容，就能操控检索结果并诱导模型生成攻击者期望的响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态检索增强生成系统通过外部知识库增强模型响应，但这也引入了新的攻击面。攻击者可能注入恶意多模态内容影响检索和生成过程，而现有研究主要关注视觉内容修改，忽略了元数据这一关键攻击向量。

Method: 提出MM-MEPA攻击方法，专门针对图像-文本条目的元数据组件进行投毒，保持视觉内容不变。通过操纵元数据来操控多模态检索过程，进而诱导下游生成模型产生攻击者期望的响应。

Result: 攻击成功率高达91%，在四种检索器和两种多模态生成器上均能持续破坏系统行为。评估代表性防御策略发现对这类纯元数据投毒攻击基本无效。

Conclusion: 多模态RAG系统存在严重安全漏洞，元数据投毒攻击暴露了当前防御策略的不足，迫切需要开发更鲁棒的、具备防御意识的检索和知识集成方法。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for enhancing multimodal large language models by grounding their responses in external, factual knowledge and thus mitigating hallucinations. However, the integration of externally sourced knowledge bases introduces a critical attack surface. Adversaries can inject malicious multimodal content capable of influencing both retrieval and downstream generation. In this work, we present MM-MEPA, a multimodal poisoning attack that targets the metadata components of image-text entries while leaving the associated visual content unaltered. By only manipulating the metadata, MM-MEPA can still steer multimodal retrieval and induce attacker-desired model responses. We evaluate the attack across multiple benchmark settings and demonstrate its severity. MM-MEPA achieves an attack success rate of up to 91\% consistently disrupting system behaviors across four retrievers and two multimodal generators. Additionally, we assess representative defense strategies and find them largely ineffective against this form of metadata-only poisoning. Our findings expose a critical vulnerability in multimodal RAG and underscore the urgent need for more robust, defense-aware retrieval and knowledge integration methods.

</details>


### [25] [Detecting Cognitive Signatures in Typing Behavior for Non-Intrusive Authorship Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00177)
*David Condrey*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出基于认知签名的非侵入式作者验证框架，利用打字界面捕获的击键时序模式区分真实创作与机械转录，保护隐私同时抵抗时序伪造攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成文本的泛滥使得基于输出的作者验证方法日益不可靠，需要寻找更可靠的验证方式。研究者观察到普通打字界面能够捕获反映真实创作过程中规划、翻译和修订阶段的认知签名。

Method: 基于大规模击键数据集（超过1.36亿个事件），定义认知负荷相关性（CLC）指标来区分真实创作与机械转录。提出非侵入式验证框架，仅收集时序元数据以保护隐私，通过证据量化限制生物特征泄露。

Result: 在所述假设下，分析评估显示具有85-95%的区分准确率。认知签名对时序伪造攻击具有鲁棒性，因为认知通道与语义内容纠缠，能抵抗击败运动级认证的攻击。

Conclusion: 将作者验证重新定义为一个人机交互问题，提供了保护隐私的替代方案，避免了侵入式监控。认知签名方法为AI时代可靠的身份验证提供了新途径。

Abstract: The proliferation of AI-generated text has intensified the need for reliable authorship verification, yet current output-based methods are increasingly unreliable. We observe that the ordinary typing interface captures rich cognitive signatures, measurable patterns in keystroke timing that reflect the planning, translating, and revising stages of genuine composition. Drawing on large-scale keystroke datasets comprising over 136 million events, we define the Cognitive Load Correlation (CLC) and show it distinguishes genuine composition from mechanical transcription. We present a non-intrusive verification framework that operates within existing writing interfaces, collecting only timing metadata to preserve privacy. Our analytical evaluation estimates 85 to 95 percent discrimination accuracy under stated assumptions, while limiting biometric leakage via evidence quantization. We analyze the adversarial robustness of cognitive signatures, showing they resist timing-forgery attacks that defeat motor-level authentication because the cognitive channel is entangled with semantic content. We conclude that reframing authorship verification as a human-computer interaction problem provides a privacy-preserving alternative to invasive surveillance.

</details>


### [26] [Privacy-Preserving Proof of Human Authorship via Zero-Knowledge Process Attestation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00179)
*David Condrey*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ZK-PoP：使用零知识证明解决过程认证中的隐私悖论，允许验证创作过程真实性而不泄露敏感行为生物特征数据


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过程认证需要收集行为生物特征数据（如击键动态、打字模式、编辑行为）来验证人类作者身份，但这些数据可能泄露作者的认知状态、健康状况和身份等敏感信息，构成GDPR第9条下的敏感生物特征数据，形成了隐私与认证之间的悖论。

Method: 提出ZK-PoP构造，使用零知识证明技术：基于Groth16证明的算术电路，结合Pedersen承诺和Bulletproof范围证明。系统允许验证者确认：(a)顺序工作函数链计算正确，(b)行为特征向量在人类群体分布范围内，(c)内容演化与增量人类编辑一致，而不学习底层行为数据、精确时间或中间内容。

Result: 证明ZK-PoP具有计算零知识性、计算可靠性，并实现跨会话不可链接性。评估显示：1小时写作会话的证明生成时间小于30秒，192字节的证明验证时间为8.2毫秒，在实际隐私级别（epsilon ≥ 1.0）下，与非隐私基线相比准确率损失小于5%。

Conclusion: ZK-PoP成功解决了过程认证中的隐私悖论，通过零知识证明技术实现了在不泄露敏感行为生物特征数据的情况下验证创作过程的真实性，为隐私保护的过程认证提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: Process attestation verifies human authorship by collecting behavioral biometric evidence, including keystroke dynamics, typing patterns, and editing behavior, during the creative process. However, the very data needed to prove authenticity can reveal intimate details about an author's cognitive state, health conditions, and identity, constituting sensitive biometric data under GDPR Article 9. We resolve this privacy-attestation paradox using zero-knowledge proofs. We present ZK-PoP, a construction that allows a verifier to confirm that (a) sequential work function chains were computed correctly, (b) behavioral feature vectors fall within human population distributions, and (c) content evolution is consistent with incremental human editing, all without learning the underlying behavioral data, exact timing, or intermediate content. Our construction uses Groth16 proofs over arithmetic circuits with Pedersen commitments and Bulletproof range proofs. We prove that ZK-PoP is computationally zero-knowledge, computationally sound, and achieves unlinkability across sessions. Evaluation shows proof generation in under 30 seconds for a 1-hour writing session, with 192-byte proofs verifiable in 8.2 ms, while incurring less than 5% accuracy loss in simulation at practical privacy levels (epsilon >= 1.0) compared to non-private baselines.

</details>


### [27] [Formal Analysis and Supply Chain Security for Agentic AI Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00195)
*Varun Pratap Bhardwaj*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SkillFortify是首个针对AI智能体技能供应链的形式化分析框架，通过六项技术贡献实现了96.95%的F1分数和100%的精确度，有效防御技能供应链攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体技能生态系统（如OpenClaw和Anthropic Agent Skills）的快速发展引入了严重的供应链攻击面。ClawHavoc活动在OpenClaw市场渗透了1200多个恶意技能，而MalTool记录了6487个规避传统检测的恶意工具。现有的12种反应式安全工具都依赖启发式方法，缺乏形式化保证。

Method: 提出了SkillFortify框架，包含六项核心贡献：1) DY-Skill攻击者模型（Dolev-Yao模型在五阶段技能生命周期的适配）；2) 基于抽象解释的静态分析框架；3) 能力基础的沙箱隔离；4) 基于SAT求解的智能体依赖图与锁文件语义；5) 形式化单调性的信任评分代数；6) SkillFortifyBench基准测试集（540个技能）。

Result: 在540个技能基准测试中，SkillFortify实现了96.95%的F1分数（95%置信区间：[95.1%, 98.4%]），精确度为100%，误报率为0%。SAT求解器能在100毫秒内处理1000个节点的依赖图。

Conclusion: SkillFortify是首个为AI智能体技能供应链提供形式化安全保证的框架，通过系统性的形式化方法有效防御了供应链攻击，填补了现有启发式安全工具的空白。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of agentic AI skill ecosystems -- exemplified by OpenClaw (228,000 GitHub stars) and Anthropic Agent Skills (75,600 stars) -- has introduced a critical supply chain attack surface. The ClawHavoc campaign (January-February 2026) infiltrated over 1,200 malicious skills into the OpenClaw marketplace, while MalTool catalogued 6,487 malicious tools that evade conventional detection. In response, twelve reactive security tools emerged, yet all rely on heuristic methods that provide no formal guarantees. We present SkillFortify, the first formal analysis framework for agent skill supply chains, with six contributions: (1) the DY-Skill attacker model, a Dolev-Yao adaptation to the five-phase skill lifecycle with a maximality proof; (2) a sound static analysis framework grounded in abstract interpretation; (3) capability-based sandboxing with a confinement proof; (4) an Agent Dependency Graph with SAT-based resolution and lockfile semantics; (5) a trust score algebra with formal monotonicity; and (6) SkillFortifyBench, a 540-skill benchmark. SkillFortify achieves 96.95% F1 (95% CI: [95.1%, 98.4%]) with 100% precision and 0% false positive rate on 540 skills, while SAT-based resolution handles 1,000-node graphs in under 100 ms.

</details>


### [28] [Your Inference Request Will Become a Black Box: Confidential Inference for Cloud-based Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00196)
*Chung-ju Huang,Huiqiang Zhao,Yuanpeng He,Lijian Li,Wenpin Jiao,Zhi Jin,Peixuan Chen,Leye Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Talaria是一个保护隐私的LLM推理框架，通过分区计算在客户端CVM中执行敏感操作，云端执行权重相关计算，使用ReMO协议保护中间数据，实现隐私保护且无损性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云托管LLM暴露客户端敏感数据（提示词和响应）给服务提供商，现有方法无法同时保证隐私、模型性能和计算效率。

Method: 1. 分区LLM流水线：客户端CVM执行权重无关的敏感操作，云端GPU执行权重相关计算；2. 使用Reversible Masked Outsourcing (ReMO)协议，通过混合掩蔽技术可逆地模糊中间数据后再外包计算。

Result: 1. 防御最先进的token推理攻击，将token重构准确率从97.5%以上降低到平均1.34%；2. 无损机制保证输出与原模型相同；3. 不显著降低效率和可扩展性。

Conclusion: Talaria是首个同时确保客户端提示词和响应对云端不可访问，同时保护模型隐私、性能和效率的工作。

Abstract: The increasing reliance on cloud-hosted Large Language Models (LLMs) exposes sensitive client data, such as prompts and responses, to potential privacy breaches by service providers. Existing approaches fail to ensure privacy, maintain model performance, and preserve computational efficiency simultaneously. To address this challenge, we propose Talaria, a confidential inference framework that partitions the LLM pipeline to protect client data without compromising the cloud's model intellectual property or inference quality. Talaria executes sensitive, weight-independent operations within a client-controlled Confidential Virtual Machine (CVM) while offloading weight-dependent computations to the cloud GPUs. The interaction between these environments is secured by our Reversible Masked Outsourcing (ReMO) protocol, which uses a hybrid masking technique to reversibly obscure intermediate data before outsourcing computations. Extensive evaluations show that Talaria can defend against state-of-the-art token inference attacks, reducing token reconstruction accuracy from over 97.5% to an average of 1.34%, all while being a lossless mechanism that guarantees output identical to the original model without significantly decreasing efficiency and scalability. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work that ensures clients' prompts and responses remain inaccessible to the cloud, while also preserving model privacy, performance, and efficiency.

</details>


### [29] [LiaisonAgent: An Multi-Agent Framework for Autonomous Risk Investigation and Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00200)
*Chuanming Tang,Ling Qing,Shifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: LiaisonAgent是一个基于QWQ-32B大语言模型的多智能体系统，旨在弥合技术风险检测与业务级风险治理之间的差距，通过端到端调查工作流实现自动化安全运营。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SOC依赖基于规则或签名的检测系统，产生大量缺乏组织上下文的技术告警，导致分析师疲劳和事件响应延迟，需要连接技术风险检测与业务风险治理的自动化解决方案。

Method: 基于QWQ-32B大推理模型构建多智能体系统，包含人机交互、综合判断和自动处置等专门子智能体，采用结合确定性工作流（合规性）和基于ReAct范式的自主推理（处理模糊场景）的混合规划架构。

Result: 端到端工具调用成功率97.8%，风险判断准确率95%，对分布外噪声和对抗性提示注入具有显著韧性，手动调查开销减少92.7%。

Conclusion: LiaisonAgent通过多智能体架构和混合规划方法，有效连接技术安全检测与业务风险治理，显著提升SOC效率和韧性。

Abstract: The rapid evolution of sophisticated cyberattacks has strained modern Security Operations Centers (SOC), which traditionally rely on rule-based or signature-driven detection systems. These legacy frameworks often generate high volumes of technical alerts that lack organizational context, leading to analyst fatigue and delayed incident responses. This paper presents LiaisonAgent, an autonomous multi-agent system designed to bridge the gap between technical risk detection and business-level risk governance. Built upon the QWQ-32B large reasoning model, LiaisonAgent integrates specialized sub-agents, including human-computer interaction agents, comprehensive judgment agents, and automated disposal agents-to execute end-to-end investigation workflows. The system leverages a hybrid planning architecture that combines deterministic workflows for compliance with autonomous reasoning based on the ReAct paradigm to handle ambiguous operational scenarios. Experimental evaluations across diverse security contexts, such as large-scale data exfiltration and unauthorized account borrowing, achieve an end-to-end tool-calling success rate of 97.8% and a risk judgment accuracy of 95%. Furthermore, the system exhibits significant resilience against out-of-distribution noise and adversarial prompt injections, while achieving a 92.7% reduction in manual investigation overhead.

</details>


### [30] [Challenges in Enabling Private Data Valuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00342)
*Yiwei Fu,Tianhao Wang,Varun Chandrasekaran*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文分析了在差分隐私约束下进行数据估值的可行性，揭示了隐私保护与估值效用之间的根本性冲突，并提出了更隐私友好的估值设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据估值方法在数据集管理、审计和数据市场中的广泛应用，这些技术引发了严重的隐私问题。估值分数可能泄露个人数据是否被用于训练、是否具有异常影响力或专有数据集中的敏感模式。这促使研究隐私保护的数据估值方法，但差分隐私要求输出对单个记录不敏感，而估值方法恰恰旨在衡量每个记录的影响，两者存在根本性冲突。

Method: 论文分析了常见估值框架中的核心算法原语，识别了导致过高敏感性的因素，解释了为什么简单的差分隐私机制会失败。进一步推导出更隐私友好的估值程序的设计原则，并通过实验表征隐私约束如何降低代表性方法和数据集中的排序保真度。

Result: 研究结果表明，在异构数据集中，稀有样本通常具有不成比例的影响，而朴素的隐私化方法往往会破坏排名或属性价值所需的细粒度区分。论文阐明了当前方法的局限性，并提供了在严格隐私保证下保持实用性的估值方法开发基础。

Conclusion: 差分隐私兼容的数据估值面临根本性挑战，因为隐私保护要求与估值目标之间存在内在张力。论文通过理论分析和实证研究揭示了这一冲突的本质，为开发在隐私约束下仍能保持实用性的估值方法提供了理论基础和设计指导。

Abstract: Data valuation methods quantify how individual training examples contribute to a model's behavior, and are increasingly used for dataset curation, auditing, and emerging data markets. As these techniques become operational, they raise serious privacy concerns: valuation scores can reveal whether a person's data was included in training, whether it was unusually influential, or what sensitive patterns exist in proprietary datasets. This motivates the study of privacy-preserving data valuation. However, privacy is fundamentally in tension with valuation utility under differential privacy (DP). DP requires outputs to be insensitive to any single record, while valuation methods are explicitly designed to measure per-record influence. As a result, naive privatization often destroys the fine-grained distinctions needed to rank or attribute value, particularly in heterogeneous datasets where rare examples exert outsized effects. In this work, we analyze the feasibility of DP-compatible data valuation. We identify the core algorithmic primitives across common valuation frameworks that induce prohibitive sensitivity, explaining why straightforward DP mechanisms fail. We further derive design principles for more privacy-amenable valuation procedures and empirically characterize how privacy constraints degrade ranking fidelity across representative methods and datasets. Our results clarify the limits of current approaches and provide a foundation for developing valuation methods that remain useful under rigorous privacy guarantees.

</details>


### [31] [Extending Adaptive Cruise Control with Machine Learning Intrusion Detection Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01173)
*Lotfi Ben Othmane,Yasaswini Konapalli,Naga Prudhvi Mareedu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ACC系统易受速度注入攻击，KF滤波器的容忍度有限，提出ACC-IDS方案通过入侵检测切换紧急制动来保证安全


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ACC系统通常被认为对故障注入攻击具有弹性，因为KF滤波器可以平滑噪声速度测量。然而，本文发现KF滤波器只能容忍有限阈值的注入值，超过阈值会导致滤波器失准，使ACC控制器做出不安全加速决策，可能引发碰撞。

Method: 提出ACC-IDS方案，在基于PID的控制器基础上集成入侵检测系统输出。当检测到攻击时，通过二进制入侵标志将控制律切换为紧急制动。该方法简单易实现，在假设的检测性能和延迟约束下工作。

Result: 通过理论证明和仿真结果验证：1) KF滤波器只能容忍有限阈值的速度注入攻击；2) 当注入值超过阈值时，滤波器会失准，导致ACC控制器做出不安全决策；3) ACC-IDS方案能够缓解这些攻击，保持ACC的防碰撞保证。

Conclusion: 传统ACC系统对速度注入攻击的弹性有限，KF滤波器的容忍度有界。提出的ACC-IDS方案通过集成入侵检测系统，在检测到攻击时切换为紧急制动，能够有效缓解攻击并保持系统的安全保证，为ACC系统安全提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: An Adaptive Cruise Control (ACC) system automatically adjusts the host vehicle's speed to maintain a safe following distance from a lead vehicle. In typical implementations, a feedback controller (e.g., a Proportional-Integral-Derivative (PID) controller) computes the host vehicle's acceleration using a target speed and a spacing error, defined as the difference between the measured inter-vehicle distance and a desired safe distance. ACC is often assumed to be resilient to fault-injection attacks because a Kalman filter (KF) can smooth noisy speed measurements. However, we show--through analytical proofs and simulation results--that a KF can tolerate injected speed values only up to a bounded threshold. When injected values exceed this threshold, the filter can be driven off track, causing the ACC controller to make unsafe acceleration decisions and potentially leading to collisions. Our main contribution is to augment the PID-based controller with Intrusion Detection System (IDS) outputs, yielding Intrusion Detection Systems-Based Adaptive Cruise Control (ACC-IDS). The ACC-IDS controller is simple and implementable: a binary intrusion flag switches the control law to emergency braking. We prove that augmenting ACC with an IDS, under assumed detection-performance and latency constraints, can mitigate these attacks and help preserve ACC's collision-avoidance guarantees.

</details>


### [32] [ProtegoFed: Backdoor-Free Federated Instruction Tuning with Interspersed Poisoned Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00516)
*Haodong Zhao,Jinming Hu,Zhaomin Wu,Zongru Wu,Wei Du,Junyi Hou,Caibei Zhao,Zhuosheng Zhang,Bingsheng He,Gongshen Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ProtegoFed：首个针对联邦指令调优中分散式中毒数据攻击的防御框架，通过频域梯度分析和全局二次聚类实现高效检测与净化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦指令调优中，中毒样本可能广泛分布于所有客户端（即使客户端是良性的），现有防御机制对此类分散式中毒攻击无效，需要新的防御方案

Method: 1）识别频域梯度作为区分中毒数据的鲁棒信号；2）提出全局二次聚类机制，促进跨客户端的中毒样本协同识别；3）构建ProtegoFed框架，在训练过程中检测、移除并净化分散的中毒数据

Result: 在四个联邦学习数据集上，ProtegoFed能识别92.00%～100.00%的中毒样本，将攻击成功率降至接近零，同时保持主任务性能

Conclusion: ProtegoFed是首个能够有效防御联邦指令调优中分散式中毒数据攻击的框架，通过频域梯度分析和协同防御机制实现了高效的中毒数据检测与净化

Abstract: Federated Instruction Tuning (FIT) enables collaborative instruction tuning of large language models across multiple organizations (clients) in a cross-silo setting without requiring the sharing of private instructions. Recent findings on natural backdoors and the existing training data collection method suggest that poisoned samples may be pervasive and inadvertently embedded in real-world datasets, potentially distributed across all clients, even if the clients are benign. This work systematically examine this threat in FIT, demonstrating that existing defenses are ineffective when poisoned data is interspersed among all clients. Addressing this challenge entails two major difficulties: identifying the distinctive characteristics of poisoned samples at each client and enabling collaborative defense when some clients are heavily dominated by poisoned samples. To address these difficulties, we identify gradients in the frequency domain as a robust signal to distinguish poisoned data. We further propose a global secondary clustering mechanism that facilitates collaborative identification of poisoned samples across clients. In summary, this paper introduces ProtegoFed, the first backdoor-free FIT framework that accurately detects, removes, and even purifies interspersed poisoned data across clients during the training. Experimental results on four FL datasets show that ProtegoFed identifies $92.00\% \sim 100.00\%$ of poisoned samples, reduces the attack success rate to almost zero, and maintains utility on the main task. Code is available at https://github.com/dongdongzhaoUP/ProtegoFed.

</details>


### [33] [On Best-Possible One-Time Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00544)
*Aparna Gupte,Jiahui Liu,Luowen Qian,Justin Raizes,Bhaskar Roberts,Mark Zhandry*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文研究一次性程序(OTP)的最佳可能安全性，证明了通用最佳可能一次性编译器不存在，但通过引入可测试一次性程序编译器并构建SEQ安全OTP，实现了量子功能的最佳可能一次性安全，并提出了状态量子不可区分混淆作为实现途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一次性程序(OTP)允许用户在单个输入上评估程序而不泄露其他信息。经典OTP需要硬件假设，即使使用量子信息，确定性功能的OTP由于温和测量攻击而不可行。虽然近期工作对某些随机化功能取得了积极结果，但基本限制和最强可达到的安全概念仍不清楚。本文旨在探索"最佳可能"的OTP，实现任何OTP构造能达到的最强一次性安全性。

Method: 1. 证明通用最佳可能一次性编译器不存在（即使对于经典随机化功能，假设存在有损加密方案）
2. 引入"可测试一次性程序"编译器子类，输出带有反射oracle的量子状态
3. 为量子信道制定广义单有效查询(SEQ)模拟安全概念，证明SEQ安全蕴含最佳可能可测试一次性安全
4. 在经典oracle模型中为所有量子功能构建SEQ安全OTP
5. 提出状态量子不可区分混淆(stateful quantum iO)，证明其蕴含最佳可能可测试OTP，并在经典oracle模型中可实现

Result: 1. 证明了通用最佳可能一次性编译器的不可能性
2. 实现了最佳可能可测试OTP编译器，首次为任意量子信道（超越经典随机化功能）构建OTP
3. 建立了状态量子iO与最佳可能可测试OTP之间的蕴含关系
4. 在经典oracle模型中实现了状态量子iO

Conclusion: 该论文通过引入可测试一次性程序编译器和SEQ安全概念，克服了通用最佳可能一次性编译器的不可能性，实现了量子功能的最佳可能一次性安全。状态量子不可区分混淆被确定为实现最佳可能可测试OTP的有前景途径，为量子密码学中的一次性程序研究提供了新的理论框架和构造方法。

Abstract: One-time programs (OTPs) aim to let a user evaluate a program on a single input while revealing nothing else. Classical OTPs require hardware assumptions, and even with quantum information, OTPs for deterministic functionalities remain impossible due to gentle-measurement attacks (Broadbent, Gutoski and Stebila, 2013). While recent works achieve positive results for certain randomized functionalities, the fundamental limits and the strongest achievable security notions remain poorly understood.
  In this paper, we ask for a "best-possible" OTP that achieves the strongest one-time security achievable by any OTP construction. We first show that a generic best-possible one-time compiler cannot exist, even for classical randomized functionalities (assuming lossy encryption schemes exist).
  Given this impossibility, we introduce a natural subclass of one-time compilers called "testable one-time program" compilers, which output quantum states augmented with reflection oracles for these program states. We show that best-possible testable OTP compilers are achievable by (1) formulating a generalized Single-Effective-Query (SEQ) simulation security notion for quantum channels and show that SEQ security implies best-possible testable one-time security, and (2) constructing SEQ-secure OTPs for all quantum functionalities in the classical oracle model. This yields the first OTP for arbitrary quantum channels beyond classical randomized functionalities.
  Finally, we propose stateful quantum indistinguishability obfuscation (stateful quantum iO) -- quantum state obfuscation for stateful quantum programs. We show that (1) stateful quantum iO implies best-possible testable OTPs and (2) stateful quantum iO is also achievable in the classical oracle model. These results identify stateful quantum iO as a promising approach towards best-possible testable OTPs.

</details>


### [34] [Where Do Smart Contract Security Analyzers Fall Short?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00890)
*Tamer Abdelaziz,Salma Alsaghir,Karim Ali*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究通过大规模基准测试和开发者调查评估了6种智能合约安全分析工具的实际效果，发现准确性差异大、误报率高、分析时间长等问题，并揭示了这些工具在实践中的采用障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管存在众多智能合约安全分析工具，但现实中的攻击仍然频繁发生，这引发了对这些工具实际有效性的质疑。现有基准测试主要使用合成或仅包含漏洞的合约数据集，无法全面评估误报率、漏报率和影响采用率的可用性因素。

Method: 采用混合方法研究：1）大规模基准测试：在653个真实智能合约上评估6个广泛使用的分析工具（Confuzzius、Dlva、Mythril、Osiris、Oyente、Slither），覆盖OWASP智能合约十大漏洞中的三类高影响漏洞（重入、自杀式合约终止、整数算术错误）；2）实践者调查：对150名专业开发者和审计人员进行调查，了解他们对这些工具的使用情况和看法。

Result: 基准测试结果显示：工具准确性差异显著（F1分数31.2%至94.6%），误报率高达32.6%，单合约分析时间超过700秒。调查发现：过高的误报率、模糊的解释和过长的分析时间是阻碍工具信任和采用的主要障碍。

Conclusion: 通过将可测量的性能差距与开发者认知联系起来，该研究为提高智能合约安全分析工具的精确性、可解释性和可用性提供了具体建议。研究强调了改进工具实际可用性的重要性，而不仅仅是技术检测能力。

Abstract: Smart contracts underpin high-value ecosystems such as decentralized finance (DeFi), yet recurring vulnerabilities continue to cause losses worth billions of dollars. Although numerous security analyzers that detect such flaws exist, real-world attacks remain frequent, raising the question of whether these tools are truly effective or simply under-used due to low developer trust. Prior benchmarks have evaluated analyzers on synthetic or vulnerable-only contract datasets, limiting their ability to measure false positives, false negatives, and usability factors that drive adoption. To close this gap, we present a mixed-methods study that combines large-scale benchmarking with practitioner insights. We evaluate six widely used analyzers (i.e., Confuzzius, Dlva, Mythril, Osiris, Oyente, and Slither) on 653 real-world smart contracts that cover three high-impact vulnerability classes from the OWASP Smart Contract Top Ten (i.e., reentrancy, suicidal contract termination, and integer arithmetic errors). Our results show substantial variation in accuracy (F1 = 31.2 to 94.6%), high false-positive rates (up to 32.6%), and runtimes exceeding 700 seconds per contract. We then survey 150 professional developers and auditors to understand how they use and perceive these tools. Our findings reveal that excessive false positives, vague explanations, and long analysis times are the main barriers to trust and adoption in practice. By linking measurable performance gaps to developer perceptions, we provide concrete recommendations for improving the precision, explainability, and usability of smart-contract security analyzers.

</details>


### [35] [AWE: Adaptive Agents for Dynamic Web Penetration Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00960)
*Akshat Singh Jaswal,Ashish Baghel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AWE是一个基于记忆增强多智能体框架的自主Web渗透测试系统，通过将结构化漏洞分析流程嵌入轻量级LLM编排层，显著提升了注入类漏洞检测的准确性、效率和确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代Web应用开发日益依赖AI辅助和低代码部署，导致软件交付速度与安全工具适应性之间的差距扩大。传统模式扫描器无法处理新颖上下文，而新兴的基于LLM的渗透测试器依赖无约束探索，存在成本高、行为不稳定和可重现性差的问题。

Method: AWE采用记忆增强多智能体框架，将结构化、特定漏洞的分析流程嵌入轻量级LLM编排层。系统结合上下文感知的有效载荷变异和生成、持久性内存以及基于浏览器的验证，产生确定性的、以利用为导向的结果。

Result: 在104个挑战的XBOW基准测试中，AWE在注入类漏洞检测方面取得显著提升：XSS成功率87%（比MAPTA提高30.5%），盲SQL注入成功率66.7%（提高33.3%）。尽管使用中端模型（Claude Sonnet 4）而非MAPTA的GPT-5，AWE在速度、成本和令牌效率方面表现更优。

Conclusion: 架构设计与模型推理能力同等重要：将LLM集成到有原则的、漏洞感知的流程中，可以显著提升注入类漏洞利用的准确性、效率和确定性。专业化和通用化架构具有互补优势，MAPTA在整体覆盖范围上更高，而AWE在特定漏洞类型上更精确高效。

Abstract: Modern web applications are increasingly produced through AI-assisted development and rapid no-code deployment pipelines, widening the gap between accelerating software velocity and the limited adaptability of existing security tooling. Pattern-driven scanners fail to reason about novel contexts, while emerging LLM-based penetration testers rely on unconstrained exploration, yielding high cost, unstable behavior, and poor reproducibility.
  We introduce AWE, a memory-augmented multi-agent framework for autonomous web penetration testing that embeds structured, vulnerability-specific analysis pipelines within a lightweight LLM orchestration layer. Unlike general-purpose agents, AWE couples context aware payload mutations and generations with persistent memory and browser-backed verification to produce deterministic, exploitation-driven results.
  Evaluated on the 104-challenge XBOW benchmark, AWE achieves substantial gains on injection-class vulnerabilities - 87% XSS success (+30.5% over MAPTA) and 66.7% blind SQL injection success (+33.3%) - while being much faster, cheaper, and more token-efficient than MAPTA, despite using a midtier model (Claude Sonnet 4) versus MAPTA's GPT-5. MAPTA retains higher overall coverage due to broader exploratory capabilities, underscoring the complementary strengths of specialized and general-purpose architectures. Our results demonstrate that architecture matters as much as model reasoning capabilities: integrating LLMs into principled, vulnerability-aware pipelines yields substantial gains in accuracy, efficiency, and determinism for injection-class exploits. The source code for AWE is available at: https://github.com/stuxlabs/AWE

</details>


### [36] [BadRSSD: Backdoor Attacks on Regularized Self-Supervised Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01019)
*Jiayao Wang,Yiping Zhang,Mohammad Maruf Hasan,Xiaoying Lei,Jiale Zhang,Junwu Zhu,Qilin Wu,Dongfang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: BadRSSD：针对自监督扩散模型表示层的首个后门攻击方法，通过在PCA空间中劫持语义表示并施加多空间约束，实现隐蔽的后门植入


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督扩散模型的表示层存在独特安全威胁：其无约束的潜在语义空间允许隐蔽的后门植入，使攻击者能够在触发时恶意控制模型行为，而传统攻击主要针对生成输出层

Method: 1. 在PCA空间中劫持带触发器的中毒样本的语义表示，使其朝向目标图像；2. 在扩散过程中通过潜在空间、像素空间和特征分布空间的协调约束控制去噪轨迹；3. 集成表示分散正则化到约束框架中，保持特征空间均匀性以增强攻击隐蔽性

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，BadRSSD在FID和MSE指标上显著优于现有攻击方法，能够可靠地在不同架构和配置中建立后门，并能有效抵抗最先进的后门防御技术

Conclusion: BadRSSD是首个针对自监督扩散模型表示层的后门攻击方法，通过多空间约束和表示分散正则化，在保持正常模型功能的同时实现高特异性的目标生成，揭示了扩散模型表示层的安全漏洞

Abstract: Self-supervised diffusion models learn high-quality visual representations via latent space denoising. However, their representation layer poses a distinct threat: unlike traditional attacks targeting generative outputs, its unconstrained latent semantic space allows for stealthy backdoors, permitting malicious control upon triggering. In this paper, we propose BadRSSD, the first backdoor attack targeting the representation layer of self-supervised diffusion models. Specifically, it hijacks the semantic representations of poisoned samples with triggers in Principal Component Analysis (PCA) space toward those of a target image, then controls the denoising trajectory during diffusion by applying coordinated constraints across latent, pixel, and feature distribution spaces to steer the model toward generating the specified target. Additionally, we integrate representation dispersion regularization into the constraint framework to maintain feature space uniformity, significantly enhancing attack stealth. This approach preserves normal model functionality (high utility) while achieving precise target generation upon trigger activation (high specificity). Experiments on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that BadRSSD substantially outperforms existing attacks in both FID and MSE metrics, reliably establishing backdoors across different architectures and configurations, and effectively resisting state-of-the-art backdoor defenses.

</details>


### [37] [Turning Black Box into White Box: Dataset Distillation Leaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01053)
*Huajie Chen,Tianqing Zhu,Yuchen Zhong,Yang Zhang,Shang Wang,Feng He,Lefeng Zhang,Jialiang Shen,Minghao Wang,Wanlei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文揭示数据集蒸馏方法存在严重隐私泄露风险，合成数据集会编码模型权重轨迹，使攻击者能够推断真实数据集中的敏感信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然合成数据集通常被认为是隐私保护的，但现有蒸馏方法实际上会导致严重的隐私泄露，因为合成数据集隐式编码了蒸馏模型的权重轨迹，变得过度信息丰富且可被攻击者利用。

Method: 提出信息揭示攻击（IRA），针对最先进的蒸馏技术进行攻击。该攻击能够准确预测蒸馏算法和模型架构，并成功推断成员关系和恢复真实数据集中的敏感样本。

Result: 实验表明，IRA攻击能够准确预测蒸馏算法和模型架构，并成功推断成员关系和恢复真实数据集中的敏感样本，证实了现有蒸馏方法存在严重隐私风险。

Conclusion: 现有数据集蒸馏方法存在严重隐私泄露风险，合成数据集并非天然隐私保护，需要开发更安全的蒸馏技术来防止信息泄露攻击。

Abstract: Dataset distillation compresses a large real dataset into a small synthetic one, enabling models trained on the synthetic data to achieve performance comparable to those trained on the real data. Although synthetic datasets are assumed to be privacy-preserving, we show that existing distillation methods can cause severe privacy leakage because synthetic datasets implicitly encode the weight trajectories of the distilled model, they become over-informative and exploitable by adversaries. To expose this risk, we introduce the Information Revelation Attack (IRA) against state-of-the-art distillation techniques. Experiments show that IRA accurately predicts both the distillation algorithm and model architecture, and can successfully infer membership and recover sensitive samples from the real dataset.

</details>


### [38] [Hide&Seek: Remove Image Watermarks with Negligible Cost via Pixel-wise Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01067)
*Huajie Chen,Tianqing Zhu,Hailin Yang,Yuchen Zhong,Yang Zhang,Hui Sun,Heng Xu,Zuobin Ying,Lihua Yin,Wanlei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出HIDE&SEEK攻击框架，有效移除SOTA图像水印防御，同时保持高视觉保真度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水印技术是防御机器生成图像滥用的关键手段，但其鲁棒性尚未得到充分探索。为了揭示当前最先进主动图像水印防御的局限性，需要开发有效的攻击方法。

Method: 提出HIDE&SEEK（HS）攻击套件，这是一套多功能且成本效益高的攻击方法，能够可靠地移除嵌入的水印。

Result: 攻击方法能够有效移除水印，同时保持图像的高视觉保真度，揭示了现有水印防御的脆弱性。

Conclusion: 当前最先进的图像水印防御存在严重漏洞，HIDE&SEEK攻击框架证明了这些防御可以被有效绕过，需要更鲁棒的水印技术来应对机器生成图像的滥用问题。

Abstract: Watermarking has emerged as a key defense against the misuse of machine-generated images (MGIs). Yet the robustness of these protections remains underexplored. To reveal the limits of SOTA proactive image watermarking defenses, we propose HIDE&SEEK (HS), a suite of versatile and cost-effective attacks that reliably remove embedded watermarks while preserving high visual fidelity.

</details>


### [39] [On the Practical Feasibility of Harvest-Now, Decrypt-Later Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01091)
*Javier Blanco-Romero,Florina Almenares Mendoza,Carlos García Rubio,Celeste Campo,Daniel Díaz Sánchez*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文将"现在收集、稍后解密"攻击重新定义为经济问题，量化了TLS 1.2/1.3、QUIC和SSH协议中对手的成本，并提出通过存储开销和量子工作负载两个成本轴进行防御的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算威胁现有加密通信，"现在收集、稍后解密"攻击通过存档密文等待量子计算机破解密钥交换。需要量化攻击者成本并评估防御策略的经济可行性。

Method: 开发开源测试平台重现完整攻击序列，建立经济模型量化对手成本，评估TLS 1.2、TLS 1.3、QUIC和SSH协议配置策略，重点关注存储开销和量子工作负载两个独立成本轴。

Result: 保留截获流量在经济上是微不足道的，防御重点从能否存档转向解密成本。加密客户端Hello强制批量收集增加存储开销，而积极重新密钥协商和更大的密钥交换参数则增加量子计算工作量。

Conclusion: 存储膨胀对双方都有惩罚，而量子成本膨胀仅针对攻击者，因此重新密钥协商和密钥大小选择提供了最强的防御杠杆。这些策略在当前基础设施上提供了深度防御，补充了向后量子密码学的迁移。

Abstract: Harvest-now, decrypt-later (HN-DL) attacks threaten today's encrypted communications by archiving ciphertext until a quantum computer can break the underlying key exchange. This paper reframes HN-DL as an economic problem, quantifying adversary costs across Transport Layer Security (TLS) 1.2, TLS 1.3, QUIC, and Secure Shell (SSH) with an open-source testbed that reproduces the full attack sequence. Our model shows that retaining intercepted traffic is economically trivial, shifting the defensive question from whether an adversary can archive to how much decryption will cost. We evaluate protocol configuration strategies that act along two independent cost axes: storage overhead and quantum workload. Beyond the ongoing migration to post-quantum cryptography, these strategies provide defense in depth with current infrastructure. Encrypted Client Hello forces indiscriminate bulk collection, inflating the archive the adversary must retain, while aggressive rekeying and larger key exchange parameters multiply the quantum computations required to recover plaintext. Because storage inflation penalizes both sides while quantum cost inflation targets the adversary alone, rekeying and key size selection offer the strongest defensive levers.

</details>


### [40] [vEcho: A Paradigm Shift from Vulnerability Verification to Proactive Discovery with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01154)
*Mingcheng Jiang,Jiancheng Huang,Jiangfei Wang,Zhengzhu Xie,Nan Fang,Guang Cheng,Xiaoyan Hu,Hua Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: vEcho框架将LLM从被动过滤器转变为具备学习、记忆和推理能力的虚拟安全专家，通过Echoic Vulnerability Propagation机制实现从被动验证到主动发现的范式转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SAST工具存在高误报率导致告警疲劳，现有基于LLM的方法仅将其作为被动、无状态的分类器，缺乏项目级上下文和学习能力，无法发现未知的类似漏洞。

Method: 提出vEcho框架，将LLM转变为虚拟安全专家，配备开发者工具套件进行深度上下文感知验证；引入Echoic Vulnerability Propagation机制，通过Cognitive Memory Module模拟人类学习，从已验证漏洞中学习并主动推断未知的类似缺陷。

Result: 在CWE-Bench-Java数据集上，vEcho达到65%检测率（相对IRIS提升41.8%），误报率降至59.78%（相对IRIS降低28.3%）；主动识别出数据集中未记录的37个已知漏洞，并在开源项目中发现了51个新的0-day漏洞。

Conclusion: vEcho通过将LLM从被动过滤器转变为具备学习、记忆和推理能力的虚拟安全专家，实现了从被动验证到主动发现的范式转变，显著提升了漏洞检测效果并有效缓解了告警疲劳问题。

Abstract: Static Application Security Testing (SAST) tools often suffer from high false positive rates, leading to alert fatigue that consumes valuable auditing resources. Recent efforts leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs) as filters offer limited improvements; however, these methods treat LLMs as passive, stateless classifiers, which lack project-wide context and the ability to learn from analyses to discover unknown, similar vulnerabilities.In this paper, we propose vEcho, a novel framework that transforms the LLM from a passive filter into a virtual security expert capable of learning, memory, and reasoning. vEcho equips its core reasoning engine with a robust developer tool suite for deep, context-aware verification. More importantly, we introduce a novel Echoic Vulnerability Propagation (EVP) mechanism. Driven by a Cognitive Memory Module that simulates human learning, EVP enables vEcho to learn from verified vulnerabilities and proactively infer unknown, analogous flaws, achieving a paradigm shift from passive verification to active discovery.Extensive experiments on the CWE-Bench-Java dataset demonstrate vEcho's dual advantages over the state-of-the-art baseline, IRIS. Specifically, vEcho achieves a 65% detection rate, marking a 41.8% relative improvement over IRIS's 45.83%. Crucially, it simultaneously addresses alert fatigue by reducing the false positive rate to 59.78%, a 28.3% relative reduction from IRIS's 84.82%. Furthermore, vEcho proactively identified 37 additional known vulnerabilities beyond the 120 documented in the dataset, and has discovered 51 novel 0-day vulnerabilities in open-source projects.

</details>


### [41] [ATLAS: AI-Assisted Threat-to-Assertion Learning for System-on-Chip Security Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01170)
*Ishraq Tashdid,Kimia Tasnia,Alexander Garcia,Jonathan Valamehr,Sazadur Rahman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ATLAS是一个LLM驱动的框架，通过结合标准化威胁建模和基于属性的形式验证来增强SoC安全性，能够从CWE等漏洞知识库自动生成安全属性和验证脚本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SoC安全验证缺乏自动化、标准化的方法，难以将漏洞知识库中的威胁信息转化为可验证的安全属性，需要一种能够桥接威胁建模和形式验证的自动化框架。

Method: ATLAS从CWE等漏洞知识库出发，识别SoC特定资产，映射相关弱点，使用LLM生成基于断言的安全属性和JasperGold验证脚本，结合资产中心分析、标准化威胁模型模板和多源SoC上下文。

Result: 在三个HACK@DAC基准测试中，ATLAS检测到39/48个CWE，并为其中33个漏洞生成了正确的安全属性，展示了自动化、知识驱动的SoC安全验证能力。

Conclusion: ATLAS通过自动化将漏洞推理转化为形式证明，推动了SoC安全验证向安全设计范式的进步，为知识驱动的自动化安全验证提供了有效框架。

Abstract: This work presents ATLAS, an LLM-driven framework that bridges standardized threat modeling and property-based formal verification for System-on-Chip (SoC) security. Starting from vulnerability knowledge bases such as Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE), ATLAS identifies SoC-specific assets, maps relevant weaknesses, and generates assertion-based security properties and JasperGold scripts for verification. By combining asset-centric analysis with standardized threat model templates and multi-source SoC context, ATLAS automates the transformation from vulnerability reasoning to formal proof. Evaluated on three HACK@DAC benchmarks, ATLAS detected 39/48 CWEs and generated correct properties for 33 of those bugs, advancing automated, knowledge-driven SoC security verification toward a secure-by-design paradigm.

</details>


### [42] [A Systematic Study of LLM-Based Architectures for Automated Patching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01257)
*Qingxiao Xu,Ze Sheng,Zhicheng Chen,Jeff Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 系统评估四种LLM补丁架构：固定工作流、单智能体系统、多智能体系统和通用代码智能体，发现架构设计和迭代深度比模型能力本身对自动补丁的可靠性和成本影响更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在自动补丁方面显示出潜力，但其效果高度依赖于如何集成到补丁系统中。先前研究主要关注提示策略和单个智能体设计，缺乏对补丁架构的系统性比较。

Method: 使用统一的基准测试和评估框架，对四种LLM补丁范式进行受控评估：固定工作流、单智能体系统、多智能体系统和通用代码智能体。分析补丁正确性、失败模式、令牌使用量和执行时间。

Result: 固定工作流高效但脆弱；单智能体系统在灵活性和成本间取得平衡；多智能体设计提高泛化能力但代价是显著更高的开销和复杂任务中推理漂移风险增加；通用代码智能体整体补丁性能最强，受益于支持跨漏洞类型有效适应的通用工具接口。

Conclusion: 架构设计和迭代深度，而非单纯的模型能力，主导着基于LLM的自动补丁的可靠性和成本。通用代码智能体因其通用工具接口而表现出最强的整体性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise for automated patching, but their effectiveness depends strongly on how they are integrated into patching systems. While prior work explores prompting strategies and individual agent designs, the field lacks a systematic comparison of patching architectures. In this paper, we present a controlled evaluation of four LLM-based patching paradigms -- fixed workflow, single-agent system, multi-agent system, and general-purpose code agents -- using a unified benchmark and evaluation framework. We analyze patch correctness, failure modes, token usage, and execution time across real-world vulnerability tasks. Our results reveal clear architectural trade-offs: fixed workflows are efficient but brittle, single-agent systems balance flexibility and cost, and multi-agent designs improve generalization at the expense of substantially higher overhead and increased risk of reasoning drift on complex tasks. Surprisingly, general-purpose code agents achieve the strongest overall patching performance, benefiting from general-purpose tool interfaces that support effective adaptation across vulnerability types. Overall, we show that architectural design and iteration depth, rather than model capability alone, dominate the reliability and cost of LLM-based automated patching.

</details>


### [43] [Real Money, Fake Models: Deceptive Model Claims in Shadow APIs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01919)
*Yage Zhang,Yukun Jiang,Zeyuan Chen,Michael Backes,Xinyue Shen,Yang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究首次系统审计了官方LLM API与影子API之间的差异，发现影子API在性能、安全性和身份验证方面存在显著不一致，最高性能差异达47.21%，45.83%的指纹测试失败，严重影响科研可重复性和应用可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿大语言模型（如GPT-5、Gemini-2.5）的高定价、支付障碍和区域限制催生了影子API的泛滥。这些第三方服务声称通过间接访问提供无区域限制的官方模型服务，但其输出是否与官方API一致尚不明确，引发了对下游应用可靠性和研究成果有效性的担忧。

Method: 研究首先识别了187篇学术论文中使用的17个影子API，选取其中3个代表性影子API进行多维审计。审计维度包括：1) 实用性（性能表现）；2) 安全性（安全行为）；3) 模型验证（身份指纹测试）。通过系统对比官方API与影子API的输出差异来评估欺骗行为。

Result: 研究发现：1) 性能差异最高达47.21%；2) 安全行为存在显著不可预测性；3) 45.83%的指纹测试未能通过身份验证。这些证据表明影子API存在直接和间接的欺骗行为，其输出与官方API不一致。

Conclusion: 影子API的欺骗性实践严重损害了科学研究的可重复性和有效性，损害了影子API用户的利益，并损害了官方模型提供商的声誉。研究呼吁学术界和业界关注这一系统性风险，并建立更严格的API验证机制。

Abstract: Access to frontier large language models (LLMs), such as GPT-5 and Gemini-2.5, is often hindered by high pricing, payment barriers, and regional restrictions. These limitations drive the proliferation of $\textit{shadow APIs}$, third-party services that claim to provide access to official model services without regional limitations via indirect access. Despite their widespread use, it remains unclear whether shadow APIs deliver outputs consistent with those of the official APIs, raising concerns about the reliability of downstream applications and the validity of research findings that depend on them. In this paper, we present the first systematic audit between official LLM APIs and corresponding shadow APIs. We first identify 17 shadow APIs that have been utilized in 187 academic papers, with the most popular one reaching 5,966 citations and 58,639 GitHub stars by December 6, 2025. Through multidimensional auditing of three representative shadow APIs across utility, safety, and model verification, we uncover both indirect and direct evidence of deception practices in shadow APIs. Specifically, we reveal performance divergence reaching up to $47.21\%$, significant unpredictability in safety behaviors, and identity verification failures in $45.83\%$ of fingerprint tests. These deceptive practices critically undermine the reproducibility and validity of scientific research, harm the interests of shadow API users, and damage the reputation of official model providers.

</details>


### [44] [Towards Privacy-Preserving LLM Inference via Collaborative Obfuscation (Technical Report)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01499)
*Yu Lin,Qizhi Zhang,Wenqiang Ruan,Daode Zhang,Jue Hong,Ye Wu,Hanning Xia,Yunlong Mao,Sheng Zhong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AloePri：首个面向工业应用的大语言模型隐私保护推理方法，通过协变量混淆技术保护输入输出数据，在保持与现有基础设施兼容的同时实现高精度和高效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云基LLM推理服务的普及带来了显著的隐私风险，现有隐私保护方法无法同时满足工业应用的三个核心需求：最小化精度和效率损失、支持异构硬件集群、兼容现有LLM基础设施。

Method: 采用协变量混淆技术，联合变换数据和模型参数以平衡精度和隐私；为每个模型组件精心设计变换方案，确保推理精度和数据隐私，同时保持与语言模型即服务基础设施的完全兼容。

Result: 在Deepseek-V3.1-Terminus模型（671B参数）上评估显示，精度损失仅为0.0%~3.5%，效率与明文推理相当；成功抵御最先进攻击，令牌恢复率低于5%。

Conclusion: AloePri是首个在真实世界系统中展示大规模模型实用性的隐私保护推理方法，满足了工业应用的所有核心需求，为隐私保护LLM推理的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The rapid development of large language models (LLMs) has driven the widespread adoption of cloud-based LLM inference services, while also bringing prominent privacy risks associated with the transmission and processing of private data in remote inference. For privacy-preserving LLM inference technologies to be practically applied in industrial scenarios, three core requirements must be satisfied simultaneously: (1) Accuracy and efficiency losses should be minimized to mitigate degradation in service experience. (2) The inference process can be run on large-scale clusters consist of heterogeneous legacy xPUs. (3) Compatibility with existing LLM infrastructures should be ensured to reuse their engineering optimizations. To the best of our knowledge, none of the existing privacy-preserving LLM inference methods satisfy all the above constraints while delivering meaningful privacy guarantees. In this paper, we propose AloePri, the first privacy-preserving LLM inference method for industrial applications. AloePri protects both the input and output data by covariant obfuscation, which jointly transforms data and model parameters to achieve better accuracy and privacy. We carefully design the transformation for each model component to ensure inference accuracy and data privacy while keeping full compatibility with existing infrastructures of Language Model as a Service. AloePri has been integrated into an industrial system for the evaluation of mainstream LLMs. The evaluation on Deepseek-V3.1-Terminus model (671B parameters) demonstrates that AloePri causes accuracy loss of 0.0%~3.5% and exhibits efficiency equivalent to that of plaintext inference. Meanwhile, AloePri successfully resists state-of-the-art attacks, with less than 5\% of tokens recovered. To the best of our knowledge, AloePri is the first method to exhibit practical applicability to large-scale models in real-world systems.

</details>


### [45] [DualSentinel: A Lightweight Framework for Detecting Targeted Attacks in Black-box LLM via Dual Entropy Lull Pattern](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01574)
*Xiaoyi Pang,Xuanyi Hao,Pengyu Liu,Qi Luo,Song Guo,Zhibo Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DualSentinel是一个轻量级统一防御框架，通过检测LLM生成过程中的"熵低谷"模式来准确识别后门和提示注入等针对性攻击，采用双重检查机制实现高效检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能系统通过API集成大型语言模型，但其可信度可能受到后门攻击和提示注入等针对性攻击的严重威胁。现有防御方法通常需要高访问权限、成本高昂且影响正常推理，难以在实际场景中应用。

Method: 首先识别受损LLM的"熵低谷"特征：当针对性攻击成功劫持生成过程时，LLM会表现出异常低且稳定的令牌概率熵。DualSentinel采用双重检查方法：1) 使用幅度和趋势感知监控方法主动敏感地标记运行时熵低谷模式；2) 触发基于任务翻转的轻量级二次验证，只有在原始任务和翻转任务中都持续存在熵低谷模式时才确认攻击。

Result: 广泛评估表明DualSentinel高度有效（检测准确率优越，接近零误报）且效率显著（额外成本可忽略），为保护已部署LLM提供了真正实用的解决方案。

Conclusion: DualSentinel通过创新的双重检查方法，利用LLM被攻击时的熵低谷特征，实现了对针对性攻击的准确、及时检测，为实际部署的LLM安全提供了轻量级、高效的防御框架。

Abstract: Recent intelligent systems integrate powerful Large Language Models (LLMs) through APIs, but their trustworthiness may be critically undermined by targeted attacks like backdoor and prompt injection attacks, which secretly force LLMs to generate specific malicious sequences. Existing defensive approaches for such threats typically rely on high access rights, impose prohibitive costs, and hinder normal inference, rendering them impractical for real-world scenarios. To solve these limitations, we introduce DualSentinel, a lightweight and unified defense framework that can accurately and promptly detect the activation of targeted attacks alongside the LLM generation process. We first identify a characteristic of compromised LLMs, termed Entropy Lull: when a targeted attack successfully hijacks the generation process, the LLM exhibits a distinct period of abnormally low and stable token probability entropy, indicating it is following a fixed path rather than making creative choices. DualSentinel leverages this pattern by developing an innovative dual-check approach. It first employs a magnitude and trend-aware monitoring method to proactively and sensitively flag an entropy lull pattern at runtime. Upon such flagging, it triggers a lightweight yet powerful secondary verification based on task-flipping. An attack is confirmed only if the entropy lull pattern persists across both the original and the flipped task, proving that the LLM's output is coercively controlled. Extensive evaluations show that DualSentinel is both highly effective (superior detection accuracy with near-zero false positives) and remarkably efficient (negligible additional cost), offering a truly practical path toward securing deployed LLMs. The source code can be accessed at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.18479273.

</details>


### [46] [Information-Theoretic Digital Twins for Stealthy Attack Detection in Industrial Control Systems: A Closed-Form KL Divergence Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01621)
*Inda Kreso,Mehran Tarif,Fatemeh Moradi,Iman Khazrak,Mostafa M Rezaee,Mohammadhossein Homaei*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出信息论数字孪生框架，用于检测工业控制系统中的隐蔽虚假数据注入攻击，相比深度学习基线在计算效率和精度上均有优势


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生用于监控工业控制系统，但检测隐蔽的虚假数据注入攻击仍具挑战性。深度学习异常检测器容易过度泛化，而传统故障检测方法在多变量系统中扩展性差。

Method: 提出闭环信息论数字孪生框架，结合N4SID辨识和稳态卡尔曼滤波，通过闭式KL散度量化残差分布偏移，捕捉均值偏差和恶意协方差偏移。

Result: 在SWaT和WADI数据集上，IT-DT分别达到0.832和0.615的F1分数，精度优于TranAD等深度学习基线。计算分析显示该方法内存需求小，推理速度比基于Transformer的方法快约600倍。

Conclusion: 该框架适用于资源受限的工业边缘控制器，无需GPU加速，为实时检测隐蔽虚假数据注入攻击提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Digital twins (DTs) are increasingly used to monitor and secure Industrial Control Systems (ICS), yet detecting stealthy False Data Injection Attacks (FDIAs) that manipulate system states within normal physical bounds remains challenging. Deep learning anomaly detectors often over-generalize such subtle manipulations, while classical fault detection methods do not scale well in highly correlated multivariate systems. We propose a closed-loop Information-Theoretic Digital Twin (IT-DT) framework for real-time anomaly detection. N4SID identification is combined with steady-state Kalman filtering to quantify residual distribution shifts via closed-form KL divergence, capturing both mean deviations and malicious cross-covariance shifts. Evaluations on the SWaT and WADI datasets show that IT-DT achieves F1-scores of 0.832 and 0.615, respectively, with better precision than deep learning baselines such as TranAD. Computational profiling indicates that the analytical approach requires minimal memory and provides approximately a 600x inference speedup over transformer-based methods on CPU hardware. This makes the framework suitable for resource-constrained industrial edge controllers without GPU acceleration.

</details>


### [47] [Co-Evolutionary Multi-Modal Alignment via Structured Adversarial Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01784)
*Guoxin Shi,Haoyu Wang,Zaihui Yang,Yuxing Wang,Yongzhe Chang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CEMMA框架通过进化攻击者和自适应防御者的协同进化实现多模态安全对齐，超越静态对抗监督


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐方法依赖静态对抗设置，在多模态环境下攻击面更大时限制了鲁棒性，需要动态自适应的方法来提升安全对齐效果

Method: 提出CEMMA框架：1) 进化攻击者将对抗提示分解为方法模板和有害意图，通过遗传算子（突变、交叉、差分进化）使简单种子攻击继承复杂越狱的结构有效性；2) 自适应防御者在合成的困难负例上迭代更新，形成适应进化攻击的闭环过程

Result: 进化攻击者显著提高红队越狱攻击成功率(ASR)，自适应防御者提高跨基准的鲁棒性和泛化能力，具有更高数据效率，不会导致过度良性拒绝，且与AdaShield等推理时防御兼容

Conclusion: CEMMA通过协同进化框架实现了超越静态对抗监督的多模态安全对齐，为动态自适应安全对齐提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: Adversarial behavior plays a central role in aligning large language models with human values. However, existing alignment methods largely rely on static adversarial settings, which fundamentally limit robustness, particularly in multimodal settings with a larger attack surface. In this work, we move beyond static adversarial supervision and introduce co-evolutionary alignment with evolving attacks, instantiated by CEMMA (Co-Evolutionary Multi-Modal Alignment), an automated and adaptive framework for multimodal safety alignment. We introduce an Evolutionary Attacker that decomposes adversarial prompts into method templates and harmful intents. By employing genetic operators, including mutation, crossover, and differential evolution, it enables simple seed attacks to inherit the structural efficacy of sophisticated jailbreaks. The Adaptive Defender is iteratively updated on the synthesized hard negatives, forming a closed-loop process that adapts alignment to evolving attacks. Experiments show that the Evolutionary Attacker substantially increases red-teaming jailbreak attack success rate (ASR), while the Adaptive Defender improves robustness and generalization across benchmarks with higher data efficiency, without inducing excessive benign refusal, and remains compatible with inference-time defenses such as AdaShield.

</details>


### [48] [Phishing the Phishers with SpecularNet: Hierarchical Graph Autoencoding for Reference-Free Web Phishing Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01874)
*Tailai Song,Pedro Casas,Michela Meo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SpecularNet是一个轻量级、无参考的网页钓鱼检测框架，仅使用域名和HTML结构，通过分层图自编码架构实现高效检测，在保持竞争力的检测性能同时大幅降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有钓鱼检测方法存在局限性：基于参考的方法依赖外部知识库和云服务，限制了实用性、可扩展性和可复现性；基于生成式AI的方法需要复杂的多模态管道；传统深度学习方法难以适应不断演变的钓鱼攻击。需要一种轻量级、无参考的解决方案。

Method: SpecularNet仅使用域名和HTML结构，将DOM建模为树结构，采用分层图自编码架构，结合定向、层级感知的消息传递机制，捕获钓鱼网页的高阶结构不变性，支持在标准CPU上进行端到端快速推理。

Result: 在13个最先进的钓鱼检测器对比评估中，SpecularNet达到93.9%的F1分数，略低于最佳基于参考的方法，但推理时间从数秒减少到约20毫秒/网页。在2026年新收集的开放世界数据集和对抗攻击测试中进一步验证了其鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SpecularNet证明了精心设计的紧凑架构能够媲美重量级系统，为网页钓鱼检测提供了一种实用、可扩展、可复现的轻量级解决方案，特别适合大规模部署和实时检测场景。

Abstract: Phishing remains the most pervasive threat to the Web, enabling large-scale credential theft and financial fraud through deceptive webpages. While recent reference-based and generative-AI-driven phishing detectors achieve strong accuracy, their reliance on external knowledge bases, cloud services, and complex multimodal pipelines fundamentally limits practicality, scalability, and reproducibility. In contrast, conventional deep learning approaches often fail to generalize to evolving phishing campaigns. We introduce SpecularNet, a novel lightweight framework for reference-free web phishing detection that demonstrates how carefully designed compact architectures can rival heavyweight systems. SpecularNet operates solely on the domain name and HTML structure, modeling the Document Object Model (DOM) as a tree and leveraging a hierarchical graph autoencoding architecture with directional, level-wise message passing. This design captures higher-order structural invariants of phishing webpages while enabling fast, end-to-end inference on standard CPUs. Extensive evaluation against 13 state of the art phishing detectors, including leading reference-based systems, shows that SpecularNet achieves competitive detection performance with dramatically lower computational cost. On benchmark datasets, it reaches an F1 score of 93.9%, trailing the best reference-based method slightly while reducing inference time from several seconds to approximately 20 milliseconds per webpage. Field and robustness evaluations further validate SpecularNet in real-world deployments, on a newly collected 2026 open-world dataset, and against adversarial attacks.

</details>


### [49] [Systematic Survey on Privacy-Preserving Architectures for IoT and Vehicular Data Sharing: Techniques, Challenges, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01876)
*Phat T. Tran-Truong,Vinh X. Q. Nguyen,Ha X. Son,Phien Nguyen-Ngoc,Khanh H. Vo,Triet M. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该调查系统分析了75篇技术论文（2007-2025），通过三维分类法将隐私保护架构分为去中心化计算、密码学基础和分布式账本三类，验证了隐私-效率-信任三难困境，并指出混合架构是未来发展方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网和车联网系统在边缘产生大量敏感数据，需要隐私保护架构以实现安全共享而不暴露原始信息。现有解决方案面临隐私-效率-信任三难困境：同时实现强隐私保证、资源受限设备的计算效率和去中心化信任在单一范式方法中难以实现。

Method: 系统分析了75篇技术论文（2007-2025），采用新颖的三维分类法将架构分为：1）去中心化计算方法，2）基于密码学的方法，3）分布式账本方法。进行时间分析、安全威胁映射和技术成熟度评估。

Result: 时间分析显示2024-2025年发表加速，占所有论文的48%；去中心化计算占贡献的44%和2025年发表的59%。成熟解决方案仅在设计空间的狭窄区域表现出色，通常在一或两个维度上优秀而牺牲其他维度，验证了三难困境假设。

Conclusion: 结合互补范式的混合架构是未来发展关键方向。需要解决跨层安全保证组合、多层协调开销最小化和后量子安全集成等关键挑战，才能在实际的下一代智能交通系统和物联网生态中部署。

Abstract: The proliferation of IoT and V2X systems generates unprecedented sensitive data at the network edge, demanding privacy-preserving architectures that enable secure sharing without exposing raw information. Contemporary solutions face a fundamental privacy-efficiency-trust trilemma: achieving strong privacy guarantees, computational efficiency for resource-constrained devices, and decentralized trust simultaneously remains intractable with single-paradigm approaches. This survey systematically analyzes 75 technical papers (2007--2025) through a novel three-dimensional taxonomy classifying architectures into Decentralized Computation, Cryptography-based, and Distributed Ledger approaches. Temporal analysis reveals dramatic acceleration during 2024--2025, with 48% of all papers published in this period -- Decentralized Computation dominates at 44% of contributions and 59% of 2025 publications. Comprehensive Security Threat Mapping and Technology Maturity Assessment demonstrate that mature solutions occupy narrow design regions excelling in one or two dimensions while compromising others, conclusively validating the trilemma hypothesis. We identify emerging hybrid architectures combining complementary paradigms as the essential path forward. Critical challenges including security guarantee composition across layers, multi-layer coordination overhead minimization, and post-quantum security integration must be addressed for practical deployment in next-generation intelligent transportation systems and IoT ecosystems.

</details>


### [50] [Accurate, private, secure, federated U-statistics with higher degree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01986)
*Quentin Sinh,Jan Ramon*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种基于多方计算的安全协议，用于在中心化差分隐私下高效计算k≥2阶U统计量，相比现有方法显著提升精度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅在本地差分隐私模型下提供解决方案，且计算效率受限于域离散化规模，需要更高效、更准确的安全计算方法

Method: 设计基于多方计算的安全协议，在中心化差分隐私框架下计算U统计量，通过理论分析验证其准确性、通信和计算特性

Result: 方法显著提升精度，例如对于Kendall's τ系数，均方误差相比现有基线降低高达四个数量级，并在实证评估中表现优异

Conclusion: 提出的MPC协议为安全计算高阶U统计量提供了高效解决方案，在中心化差分隐私下实现了精度和效率的显著改进

Abstract: We study the problem of computing a U-statistic with a kernel function f of degree k $\ge$ 2, i.e., the average of some function f over all k-tuples of instances, in a federated learning setting. Ustatistics of degree 2 include several useful statistics such as Kendall's $τ$ coefficient, the Area under the Receiver-Operator Curve and the Gini mean difference. Existing methods provide solutions only under the lower-utility local differential privacy model and/or scale poorly in the size of the domain discretization. In this work, we propose a protocol that securely computes U-statistics of degree k $\ge$ 2 under central differential privacy by leveraging Multi Party Computation (MPC). Our method substantially improves accuracy when compared to prior solutions. We provide a detailed theoretical analysis of its accuracy, communication and computational properties. We evaluate its performance empirically, obtaining favorable results, e.g., for Kendall's $τ$ coefficient, our approach reduces the Mean Squared Error by up to four orders of magnitude over existing baselines.

</details>


### [51] [Protection against Source Inference Attacks in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02017)
*Andreas Athanasiou,Kangsoo Jung,Catuscia Palamidessi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对联邦学习中源推断攻击（SIA）的防御方法，通过参数级洗牌与余数系统（RNS）结合，在保持模型精度的同时将攻击准确率降至随机猜测水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习（FL）虽然最初被设计为隐私保护的机器学习范式，但已被证明容易受到一系列隐私攻击。最近，源推断攻击（SIA）引起了关注，在这种攻击中，诚实但好奇的中央服务器试图识别训练阶段使用的特定数据点属于哪个客户端。令人担忧的是，标准的差分隐私梯度混淆技术对SIA无效，除非严重牺牲模型精度。

Method: 本文提出在广泛研究的FL洗牌模型中进行防御，其中诚实的洗牌器作为客户端和服务器之间的中介。首先证明标准朴素洗牌不足以防止SIA。为了有效防御SIA，需要在更细粒度级别应用洗牌；提出了一种新颖的参数级洗牌与余数系统（RNS）组合方法。该方法在不影响联合模型精度的情况下提供对SIA的鲁棒保护，并可无缝集成到其他隐私保护机制中。

Result: 在一系列模型和数据集上的实验证实，标准洗牌方法无法防止SIA，而相比之下，本文提出的方法将攻击准确率降至随机猜测水平。

Conclusion: 本文提出的参数级洗牌与余数系统组合方法为联邦学习中的源推断攻击提供了有效的防御解决方案，在保持模型精度的同时实现了强大的隐私保护，可与其他隐私保护机制协同工作。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) was initially proposed as a privacy-preserving machine learning paradigm. However, FL has been shown to be susceptible to a series of privacy attacks. Recently, there has been concern about the Source Inference Attack (SIA), where an honest-but-curious central server attempts to identify exactly which client owns a given data point which was used in the training phase. Alarmingly, standard gradient obfuscation techniques with Differential Privacy have been shown to be ineffective against SIAs, at least without severely diminishing the accuracy.
  In this work, we propose a defense against SIAs within the widely studied shuffle model of FL, where an honest shuffler acts as an intermediary between the clients and the server. First, we demonstrate that standard naive shuffling alone is insufficient to prevent SIAs. To effectively defend against SIAs, shuffling needs to be applied at a more granular level; we propose a novel combination of parameter-level shuffling with the residue number system (RNS). Our approach provides robust protection against SIAs without affecting the accuracy of the joint model and can be seamlessly integrated into other privacy protection mechanisms.
  We conduct experiments on a series of models and datasets, confirming that standard shuffling approaches fail to prevent SIAs and that, in contrast, our proposed method reduce the attack's accuracy to the level of random guessing.

</details>


### [52] [Boosting Device Utilization in Control Flow Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02161)
*Alexandra Lengert,Adam Ilyas Caulfield,Ivan De Oliveira Nunes*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CARAMEL提出了一种新的控制流审计架构，通过硬件-软件协同设计的可信根，允许证明方应用在传输控制流证据时继续执行，显著提高了CPU利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有控制流审计架构存在"忙等待"阶段，硬件可信根需要保持执行控制以确保向验证方传输控制流证据，这严重降低了MCU的CPU利用率。

Method: CARAMEL采用硬件-软件可信根协同设计，设计了一个具有独立专用通信接口的新可信根，允许证明方应用在传输控制流证据时恢复执行，避免传输延迟导致的争用。

Result: CARAMEL显著提高了CPU利用率，同时以适度的硬件成本保持了安全性，实现和评估已开源。

Conclusion: CARAMEL解决了现有控制流审计架构的CPU利用率瓶颈，通过创新的硬件-软件协同设计实现了高效的安全审计，适用于安全关键系统的MCU保护。

Abstract: Micro-Controller Units (MCUs) are widely used in safety-critical systems, making them attractive targets for attacks. This calls for lightweight defenses that remain effective despite software compromise. Control Flow Auditing (CFAud) is one such mechanism wherein a remote verifier (Vrf) is guaranteed to received evidence about the control flow path taken on a prover (Prv) MCU, even when Prv software is compromised. Despite promising benefits, current CFAud architectures unfortunately require a ``busy-wait'' phase where a hardware-anchored root-of-trust (RoT) in Prv retains execution control to ensure delivery of control flow evidence to Vrf. This drastically reduces the CPU utilization on Prv.
  In this work, we addresses this limitation with an architecture for Contention Avoidance in Runtime Auditing with Minimized Execution Latency (CARAMEL). CARAMEL is a hardware-software RoT co-design that enables Prv applications to resume while control flow evidence is transmitted to Vrf. This significantly reduces contention due to transmission delays and improves CPU utilization without giving up on security. Key to CARAMEL is our design of a new RoT with a self-contained (and minimal) dedicated communication interface. CARAMEL's implementation and accompanying evaluation are made open-source. Our results show substantially improved CPU utilization at a modest hardware cost.

</details>

### [53] [Beyond Static Instruction: A Multi-agent AI Framework for Adaptive Augmented Reality Robot Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00016)
*Nicolas Leins,Jana Gonnermann-Müller,Malte Teichmann,Sebastian Pokutta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出用于工业机器人培训的AR应用，并设计多智能体AI框架，通过LLM智能体实时适应学习者的认知特征


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AR培训界面多为静态设计，未能考虑学习者多样化的认知特征，导致培训效果存在差异，需要动态适应性系统

Method: 开发AR机器人培训应用，评估基线界面；提出多智能体AI框架，整合语音、生理、机器人数据等多模态输入，利用自主LLM智能体进行实时推理和动态适应

Result: 基线AR界面整体可用性高，但任务完成时间和学习者特征存在显著差异，验证了动态适应的必要性

Conclusion: 多智能体AI框架能够桥接静态可视化与教学智能，通过LLM驱动的实时适应提升AR机器人培训效果

Abstract: Augmented Reality (AR) offers powerful visualization capabilities for industrial robot training, yet current interfaces remain predominantly static, failing to account for learners' diverse cognitive profiles. In this paper, we present an AR application for robot training and propose a multi-agent AI framework for future integration that bridges the gap between static visualization and pedagogical intelligence. We report on the evaluation of the baseline AR interface with 36 participants performing a robotic pick-and-place task. While overall usability was high, notable disparities in task duration and learner characteristics highlighted the necessity for dynamic adaptation. To address this, we propose a multi-agent framework that orchestrates multiple components to perform complex preprocessing of multimodal inputs (e.g., voice, physiology, robot data) and adapt the AR application to the learner's needs. By utilizing autonomous Large Language Model (LLM) agents, the proposed system would dynamically adapt the learning environment based on advanced LLM reasoning in real-time.

</details>


### [54] [A User Study on the Suitability of Teleoperation Interfaces for Primitive Manipulation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00020)
*Jun Aoki,Shunki Itadera*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 比较3D鼠标与VR控制器在机器人臂遥操作中的性能差异，发现界面选择应与任务主导运动原语相匹配


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管遥操作系统已被广泛研究，但不同界面在不同任务中的系统比较仍然有限。本研究旨在探究实际遥操作系统中界面特性对性能的影响，特别是针对不同任务类型时3D鼠标和VR控制器的适用性差异。

Method: 采用23名参与者，使用六自由度机器人臂执行两种基础任务：重复按压按钮和旋转旋钮。每个任务进行3分钟试验，分别使用3D鼠标和VR控制器两种界面。试验后使用NASA-TLX量表评估工作负荷。

Result: 界面与任务性能存在明确关联：VR控制器在按压按钮任务中表现更优，而3D鼠标在旋转旋钮任务中性能更好且工作负荷更低。这表明不同界面在不同运动原语任务中具有各自的优势。

Conclusion: 设计实用遥操作界面时，必须考虑任务的主导运动原语。界面选择应根据具体任务类型进行优化，而不是采用"一刀切"的方案。VR控制器更适合平移主导任务，而3D鼠标更适合旋转主导任务。

Abstract: The application of teleoperation to control robotic arms has been widely explored, and user-friendly teleoperation systems have been studied for facilitating higher performance and lower operational burden. To investigate the dominant factors in a practical teleoperation system, this study focused on the characteristics of an interface used to operate a robotic arm. The usability of an interface depends on the characteristics of the manipulation tasks to be completed; however, systematic comparisons of different interfaces across different tasks remain limited. In this study, we compared two widely used teleoperation interfaces, a 3D mouse and a VR controller, for two simple yet broadly applicable tasks with a six-degree-of-freedom (6DoF) robotic arm: repetitively pushing buttons and rotating knobs. Participants (N = 23) controlled a robotic arm with 6DoF to push buttons and rotate knobs as many times as possible in 3-minute trials. Each trial was followed by a NASA-TLX workload rating. The results showed a clear connection between the interface and task performance: the VR controller yielded higher performance for pushing buttons, whereas the 3D mouse performed better and was less demanding for knob rotation. These findings highlight the importance of considering dominant motion primitives of the task when designing practical teleoperation interfaces.

</details>


### [55] [Autonomous Block Assembly for Boom Cranes with Passive Joint Dynamics: Integrated Vision MPC Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00103)
*Gerald Ebmer,Minh Nhat Vu,Tobias Glück,Wolfgang Kemmetmüller*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种用于建筑环境中预制块组装的关节臂式起重机自主控制框架，通过集成实时视觉姿态估计、碰撞感知B样条路径规划和非线性模型预测控制，实现了自主拾取、放置和避障装配操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决建筑环境中关节臂式起重机在预制块组装时面临的精确放置控制挑战，特别是被动关节动力学引起的摆锤式摆动问题，这严重影响了建筑构件的准确定位。

Method: 采用集成方法结合实时视觉姿态估计、碰撞感知B样条路径规划和非线性模型预测控制(NMPC)。在实验室规模测试平台上验证，该平台模拟起重机运动学和被动动力学，支持快速实验。碰撞感知规划器在CPU硬件上实时生成可行的B样条参考轨迹，NMPC控制器主动抑制被动关节摆动并跟踪规划轨迹。

Result: 实验结果表明实现了自主块体堆叠和避障装配，摆动阻尼使稳定时间比未控制的被动动力学减少了一个数量级以上，证实了集成方法在建筑自动化中的实时可行性。

Conclusion: 该集成自主控制框架有效解决了关节臂式起重机在建筑装配中的精确放置问题，通过实时视觉反馈、碰撞感知路径规划和NMPC控制，显著提高了操作精度和效率，为建筑自动化提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: This paper presents an autonomous control framework for articulated boom cranes performing prefabricated block assembly in construction environments. The key challenge addressed is precise placement control under passive joint dynamics that cause pendulum-like sway, complicating the accurate positioning of building components. Our integrated approach combines real-time vision-based pose estimation of building blocks, collision-aware B-spline path planning, and nonlinear model predictive control (NMPC) to achieve autonomous pickup, placement, and obstacle-avoidance assembly operations. The framework is validated on a laboratory-scale testbed that emulates crane kinematics and passive dynamics while enabling rapid experimentation. The collision-aware planner generates feasible B-spline references in real-time on CPU hardware with anytime performance, while the NMPC controller actively suppresses passive joint sway and tracks the planned trajectory under continuous vision feedback. Experimental results demonstrate autonomous block stacking and obstacle-avoidance assembly, with sway damping reducing settling times by more than an order of magnitude compared to uncontrolled passive dynamics, confirming the real-time feasibility of the integrated approach for construction automation.

</details>


### [56] [Designing Social Robots with Ethical, User-Adaptive Explainability in the Era of Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00102)
*Fethiye Irmak Dogan,Alva Markelius,Hatice Gunes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文主张将伦理、用户适应的可解释性作为基础模型驱动社交机器人的核心设计目标，提出基于更小、更公平数据集构建用户适应、模态感知、协同设计的解释策略建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型越来越多地嵌入社交机器人，不仅中介其言行，还控制其适应用户的方式。传统"一刀切"解释策略变得尤其问题重重：通用解释现在包裹着由在庞大、异构、不透明数据集上训练的模型产生的行为。需要解决基础模型驱动社交机器人中可解释性和伦理问题。

Method: 首先识别当适应和解释都委托给基础模型时出现的可解释性和伦理挑战。基于此分析，提出四项建议：1) 用户适应的解释策略；2) 模态感知的解释；3) 协同设计方法；4) 基于更小、更公平数据集的解释策略。通过LLM驱动的社交辅助机器人用例展示这些建议在敏感现实领域的具体实施。

Result: 论文提出了一个框架性建议，而非具体实验结果。通过分析识别了基础模型驱动社交机器人中的关键可解释性和伦理挑战，并提出了四项具体建议来推动该领域向更负责任的方向发展。

Conclusion: 伦理、用户适应的可解释性应成为基础模型驱动社交机器人的核心设计目标。需要从通用解释转向基于更小、更公平数据集构建的用户适应、模态感知、协同设计的解释策略，以确保社交机器人的行为透明、可信且符合伦理要求。

Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly embedded in social robots, mediating not only what they say and do but also how they adapt to users over time. This shift renders traditional ``one-size-fits-all'' explanation strategies especially problematic: generic justifications are now wrapped around behaviour produced by models trained on vast, heterogeneous, and opaque datasets. We argue that ethical, user-adapted explainability must be treated as a core design objective for foundation-model-driven social robotics. We first identify open challenges around explainability and ethical concerns that arise when both adaptation and explanation are delegated to foundation models. Building on this analysis, we propose four recommendations for moving towards user-adapted, modality-aware, and co-designed explanation strategies grounded in smaller, fairer datasets. An illustrative use case of an LLM-driven socially assistive robot demonstrates how these recommendations might be instantiated in a sensitive, real-world domain.

</details>


### [57] [Embedding Morphology into Transformers for Cross-Robot Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00182)
*Kei Suzuki,Jing Liu,Ye Wang,Chiori Hori,Matthew Brand,Diego Romeres,Toshiaki Koike-Akino*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种具身感知的Transformer策略，通过三种机制注入机器人形态信息，提高跨机器人策略学习的性能和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨机器人策略学习（训练单一策略在多种机器人形态上表现良好）是机器人学习中的核心挑战。现有的Transformer策略（如视觉-语言-动作模型）通常是形态无关的，必须从观察中推断运动学结构，这会降低跨形态的鲁棒性，甚至限制单个形态内的性能。

Method: 提出具身感知的Transformer策略，通过三种机制注入形态信息：1）运动学token：将动作按关节分解并通过关节级时间分块压缩时间；2）拓扑感知注意力偏置：将运动学拓扑编码为自注意力中的归纳偏置，鼓励沿运动学边的消息传递；3）关节属性条件：用每个关节的描述符增强拓扑，捕捉连接性之外的语义信息。

Result: 在多种机器人形态上，这种结构化集成相比基础的pi0.5 VLA基线持续提高了性能，表明在单个形态内和跨形态上都获得了改进的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过结构化地集成形态信息到Transformer策略中，可以有效提高跨机器人策略学习的性能和鲁棒性，解决了传统形态无关方法在推断运动学结构方面的局限性。

Abstract: Cross-robot policy learning -- training a single policy to perform well across multiple embodiments -- remains a central challenge in robot learning. Transformer-based policies, such as vision-language-action (VLA) models, are typically embodiment-agnostic and must infer kinematic structure purely from observations, which can reduce robustness across embodiments and even limit performance within a single embodiment. We propose an embodiment-aware transformer policy that injects morphology via three mechanisms: (1) kinematic tokens that factorize actions across joints and compress time through per-joint temporal chunking; (2) a topology-aware attention bias that encodes kinematic topology as an inductive bias in self-attention, encouraging message passing along kinematic edges; and (3) joint-attribute conditioning that augments topology with per-joint descriptors to capture semantics beyond connectivity. Across a range of embodiments, this structured integration consistently improves performance over a vanilla pi0.5 VLA baseline, indicating improved robustness both within an embodiment and across embodiments.

</details>


### [58] [SurgFusion-Net: Diversified Adaptive Multimodal Fusion Network for Surgical Skill Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00108)
*Runlong He,Freweini M. Tesfai,Matthew W. E. Boal,Nazir Sirajudeen,Dimitrios Anastasiou,Jialang Xu,Mobarak I. Hoque,Philip J. Edwards,John D. Kelly,Ashwin Sridhar,Abdolrahim Kadkhodamohammadi,Dhivya Chandrasekaran,Matthew J. Clarkson,Danail Stoyanov,Nader Francis,Evangelos B. Mazomenos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SurgFusion-Net和DRA：用于多模态手术技能评估的创新融合方法，在两个新的临床数据集上验证，显著优于现有基线


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术技能评估方法主要依赖RGB视频且仅限于干实验室环境，无法应对真实临床场景中的复杂挑战，如手术环境、相机和组织运动等。多模态方法开发面临任务复杂性、标注数据有限和跨模态信息融合技术不足等困难。

Method: 提出SurgFusion-Net和发散调节注意力(DRA)融合策略，包含自适应双重注意力和多样性促进多头注意力机制，从三种模态（RGB、光流、工具分割掩码）基于手术上下文融合多模态信息。贡献了两个首创的临床数据集：RAH-skill（37个机器人辅助子宫切除术视频）和RARP-skill（33个机器人辅助根治性前列腺切除术视频）。

Result: 在JIGSAWS基准测试、RAH-skill和RARP-skill数据集上验证，方法显著优于近期基线：在JIGSAWS任务中LOSO和LOUO的SCC分别提升0.02和0.04；在RAH-skill和RARP-skill数据集上SCC分别提升0.0538和0.0493。

Conclusion: 提出的SurgFusion-Net和DRA方法通过创新的多模态融合策略有效解决了临床手术技能评估的挑战，在两个新的临床数据集上验证了其优越性能，为手术分析和教育提供了有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Robotic-assisted surgery (RAS) is established in clinical practice, and automated surgical skill assessment utilizing multimodal data offers transformative potential for surgical analytics and education. However, developing effective multimodal methods remains challenging due to the task complexity, limited annotated datasets and insufficient techniques for cross-modal information fusion. Existing state-of-the-art relies exclusively on RGB video and only applies on dry-lab settings, failing to address the significant domain gap between controlled simulation and real clinical cases, where the surgical environment together with camera and tissue motion introduce substantial complexities. This work introduces SurgFusion-Net and Divergence Regulated Attention (DRA), an innovative fusion strategy for multimodal surgical skill assessment. We contribute two first-of-their-kind clinical datasets: the RAH-skill dataset containing 279,691 RGB frames from 37 videos of Robot-assisted Hysterectomy (RAH), and the RARP-skill dataset containing 70,661 RGB frames from 33 videos of Robot-Assisted Radical Prostatectomy (RARP). Both datasets include M-GEARS skill annotations, corresponding optical flow and tool segmentation masks. DRA incorporates adaptive dual attention and diversity-promoting multi-head attention to fuse multimodal information, from three modalities, based on surgical context, enhancing assessment accuracy and reliability. Validated on the JIGSAWS benchmark, RAH-skill, and RARP-skill datasets, our approach outperforms recent baselines with SCC improvements of 0.02 in LOSO, 0.04 in LOUO across JIGSAWS tasks, and 0.0538 and 0.0493 gains on RAH-skill and RARP-skill, respectively.

</details>


### [59] [Modeling PWM-Time-SOC Interaction in a Simulated Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00319)
*Vidyut Pradeep,Shirantha Welikala*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开发了一个物理和数据驱动的模型，通过仿真预测四轮Arduino机器人在不同PWM占空比下的电池荷电状态(SOC)随时间变化，用于能量感知的运动规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人需要准确预测电池荷电状态，以便在不耗尽所有可用电源的情况下规划运动。现有方法需要更精确的SOC预测模型来支持能量感知的路径规划。

Method: 1. 建立包含电机电气特性（电阻、电感、反电动势、扭矩常数）和机械动力学（质量、阻力、滚动阻力、车轮半径）的前向运动仿真模型；2. 在PWM占空比1-100%范围内生成SOC时间序列数据；3. 应用稀疏非线性动力学辨识(SINDy)结合最小二乘回归构建统一的非线性模型SOC(t, p)。

Result: 成功开发了一个能够预测SOC随时间t和PWM占空比p变化的统一非线性模型。该模型能够准确描述电池荷电状态的消耗动态。

Conclusion: 该框架为类似机器人提供了能量感知规划能力，并可扩展到包含任意初始SOC水平和环境相关参数，支持实际部署应用。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of battery state of charge is needed for autonomous robots to plan movements without using up all available power. This work develops a physics and data-informed model from a simulation that predicts SOC depletion as a function of time and PWM duty cycle for a simulated 4-wheel Arduino robot. A forward-motion simulation incorporating motor electrical characteristics (resistance, inductance, back-EMF, torque constant) and mechanical dynamics (mass, drag, rolling resistance, wheel radius) was used to generate SOC time-series data across PWM values from 1-100%. Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamics (SINDy), combined with least-squares regression, was applied to construct a unified nonlinear model that captures SOC(t, p). The framework allows for energy-aware planning for similar robots and can be extended to incorporate arbitrary initial SOC levels and environment-dependent parameters for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [60] [Geometric Look-Angle Shaping Strategy for Enclosed Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00325)
*Amit Shivam,Manuel C. R. M. Fernandes,Sergio Vinha,Fernando A. C. C. Fontes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GLASS是一种用于无人机封闭区域检测的几何视角整形策略，通过有界的几何一致视角整形实现无人机对期望保持路径的收敛


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统检测方法存在远场限制和奇异性问题，需要一种能够在封闭区域内稳定收敛到预设检测距离的鲁棒制导策略

Method: 提出几何视角整形策略，在极坐标几何框架中嵌入平滑的双曲正切型整形函数，确保制导动力学的全局存在性，并纳入最大转弯率约束

Result: 通过Lyapunov稳定性分析证明了在显式曲率可行性条件下的渐近收敛性，获得了解析的稳定时间特性，六自由度四旋翼仿真验证了策略有效性

Conclusion: GLASS为自主封闭检测任务提供了一个实用可行的制导框架，避免了奇异性并克服了传统方法的远场限制

Abstract: This paper introduces inspection through GLASS, a Geometric Look-Angle Shaping Strategy for enclosed regions using unmanned aerial vehicles. In doing so, the vehicles guidance command is constructed through a bounded, geometry-consistent shaping of the look angle relative to a desired standoff path. By embedding a smooth, hyperbolic-tangent-type shaping function within a polar geometric framework, GLASS ensures global existence of the guidance dynamics.
