The key insight is that factor graph structure is preserved under Shor's relaxation and Burer-Monteiro factorization: applying these transformations to a QCQP with an associated factor graph representation yields a lifted problem admitting a factor graph model with identical connectivity, in which variables and factors are simple one-to-one algebraic transformations of those in the original QCQP. This structural preservation enables the Riemannian Staircase methodology for certifiable estimation to be implemented using the same mature, highly-performant factor graph libraries and workflows already ubiquitously employed throughout robotics and computer vision, making certifiable estimation as straightforward to design and deploy as conventional factor graph inference.

</details>


### [100] [Spherical Latent Motion Prior for Physics-Based Simulated Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01294)
*Jing Tan,Weisheng Xu,Xiangrui Jiang,Jiaxi Zhang,Kun Yang,Kai Wu,Jiaqi Xiong,Shiting Chen,Yangfan Li,Yixiao Feng,Yuetong Fang,Yujia Zou,Yiqun Song,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SLMP是一种两阶段球形潜在运动先验学习方法，通过高质量运动跟踪控制器和球形潜在空间蒸馏，解决了VAE信息损失和AMP模式崩溃问题，在物理人形控制中实现了稳定随机采样和多样化运动技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动先验学习方法存在明显缺陷：变分自编码器（VAE）存在信息损失，随机潜在采样可能产生无效行为；对抗运动先验（AMP）存在模式崩溃问题，难以捕捉多样化运动技能。需要一种既能保留精细运动细节，又能实现稳定随机采样的方法。

Method: 提出球形潜在运动先验（SLMP）两阶段方法：第一阶段训练高质量运动跟踪控制器；第二阶段将跟踪控制器蒸馏到球形潜在空间。通过蒸馏、判别器和判别器引导的局部语义一致性约束的组合，构建结构化潜在动作空间，实现无信息损失的稳定随机采样。

Result: 在收集的两小时人类战斗动作捕捉数据集上，SLMP能够保留精细运动细节而无信息损失，随机采样产生语义有效且稳定的行为。应用于双智能体物理战斗任务时，仅使用简单基于规则的奖励就能产生类人且物理合理的战斗行为。SLMP还能泛化到不同人形机器人形态，展示了跨形态的迁移能力。

Conclusion: SLMP通过两阶段球形潜在空间学习方法，有效解决了现有运动先验方法的信息损失和模式崩溃问题，实现了高质量、多样化且物理合理的运动控制，在复杂物理人形控制任务中表现出优越性能，并具有良好的跨形态泛化能力。

Abstract: Learning motion priors for physics-based humanoid control is an active research topic. Existing approaches mainly include variational autoencoders (VAE) and adversarial motion priors (AMP). VAE introduces information loss, and random latent sampling may sometimes produce invalid behaviors. AMP suffers from mode collapse and struggles to capture diverse motion skills. We present the Spherical Latent Motion Prior (SLMP), a two-stage method for learning motion priors. In the first stage, we train a high-quality motion tracking controller. In the second stage, we distill the tracking controller into a spherical latent space. A combination of distillation, a discriminator, and a discriminator-guided local semantic consistency constraint shapes a structured latent action space, allowing stable random sampling without information loss. To evaluate SLMP, we collect a two-hour human combat motion capture dataset and show that SLMP preserves fine motion detail without information loss, and random sampling yields semantically valid and stable behaviors. When applied to a two-agent physics-based combat task, SLMP produces human-like and physically plausible combat behaviors only using simple rule-based rewards. Furthermore, SLMP generalizes across different humanoid robot morphologies, demonstrating its transferability beyond a single simulated avatar.

</details>


### [101] [Hybrid TD3: Overestimation Bias Analysis and Stable Policy Optimization for Hybrid Action Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01302)
*Thanh-Tuan Tran,Thanh Nguyen Canh,Nak Young Chong,Xiem HoangVan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Hybrid TD3方法，扩展TD3算法以原生处理参数化混合动作空间，通过理论分析过估计偏差并引入加权裁剪Q学习目标，在机器人操作任务中实现更好的训练稳定性和性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作中的强化学习面临离散-连续混合动作空间的挑战，现有方法要么离散化连续分量，要么将离散选择松弛为连续近似，在高维动作空间和领域随机化下存在可扩展性限制和训练不稳定性问题

Method: 提出Hybrid TD3方法，扩展TD3算法原生处理参数化混合动作空间；进行混合动作设置下过估计偏差的严格理论分析，推导双评论家架构下的形式化边界；引入加权裁剪Q学习目标，通过对离散动作分布的边缘化，在保持偏差减少的同时提高策略平滑性

Result: Hybrid TD3在训练稳定性和性能方面优于最先进的混合动作基线方法，实现了竞争性的表现

Conclusion: Hybrid TD3为机器人操作中的混合动作空间强化学习提供了一种原则性方法，通过理论分析和算法改进解决了现有方法的局限性，在训练稳定性和性能方面表现出优势

Abstract: Reinforcement learning in discrete-continuous hybrid action spaces presents fundamental challenges for robotic manipulation, where high-level task decisions and low-level joint-space execution must be jointly optimized. Existing approaches either discretize continuous components or relax discrete choices into continuous approximations, which suffer from scalability limitations and training instability in high-dimensional action spaces and under domain randomization. In this paper, we propose Hybrid TD3, an extension of Twin Delayed Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (TD3) that natively handles parameterized hybrid action spaces in a principled manner. We conduct a rigorous theoretical analysis of overestimation bias in hybrid action settings, deriving formal bounds under twin-critic architectures and establishing a complete bias ordering across five algorithmic variants. Building on this analysis, we introduce a weighted clipped Q-learning target that marginalizes over the discrete action distribution, achieving equivalent bias reduction to standard clipped minimization while improving policy smoothness. Experimental results demonstrate that Hybrid TD3 achieves superior training stability and competitive performance against state-of-the-art hybrid action baselines

</details>


### [102] [D-GVIO: A Buffer-Driven and Efficient Decentralized GNSS-Visual-Inertial State Estimator for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01404)
*Yarong Luo,Wentao Lu,Chi Guo,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: D-GVIO：一种基于缓冲区驱动的完全去中心化GNSS-视觉-惯性里程计框架，通过创新的缓冲策略实现高效鲁棒的分布式状态估计


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群体应用（如协作探索和搜救任务）需要协同定位，但在资源受限平台上保持实时性、鲁棒性和计算效率面临重大挑战

Method: 提出D-GVIO框架，包含四个核心机制：1）通过协方差分割、协方差交叉和缓冲策略模块化分布式状态估计的传播和更新步骤；2）采用左不变扩展卡尔曼滤波器进行信息融合；3）基于缓冲区的重传播策略处理延迟测量；4）自适应缓冲区驱动的异常值检测方法剔除GNSS异常值

Result: 显著降低了计算和通信负担，相比传统EKF具有更优的状态估计性能，能够高效准确地处理延迟测量，在GNSS挑战环境中增强了鲁棒性

Conclusion: D-GVIO框架通过创新的缓冲策略和分布式状态估计机制，有效解决了资源受限平台上协同定位的实时性、鲁棒性和计算效率挑战

Abstract: Cooperative localization is essential for swarm applications like collaborative exploration and search-and-rescue missions. However, maintaining real-time capability, robustness, and computational efficiency on resource-constrained platforms presents significant challenges. To address these challenges, we propose D-GVIO, a buffer-driven and fully decentralized GNSS-Visual-Inertial Odometry (GVIO) framework that leverages a novel buffering strategy to support efficient and robust distributed state estimation. The proposed framework is characterized by four core mechanisms. Firstly, through covariance segmentation, covariance intersection and buffering strategy, we modularize propagation and update steps in distributed state estimation, significantly reducing computational and communication burdens. Secondly, the left-invariant extended Kalman filter (L-IEKF) is adopted for information fusion, which exhibits superior state estimation performance over the traditional extended Kalman filter (EKF) since its state transition matrix is independent of the system state. Thirdly, a buffer-based re-propagation strategy is employed to handle delayed measurements efficiently and accurately by leveraging the L-IEKF, eliminating the need for costly re-computation. Finally, an adaptive buffer-driven outlier detection method is proposed to dynamically cull GNSS outliers, enhancing robustness in GNSS-challenged environments.

</details>


### [103] [PhysGraph: Physically-Grounded Graph-Transformer Policies for Bimanual Dexterous Hand-Tool-Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01436)
*Runfa Blark Li,David Kim,Xinshuang Liu,Keito Suzuki,Dwait Bhatt,Nikola Raicevic,Xin Lin,Ki Myung Brian Lee,Nikolay Atanasov,Truong Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhysGraph：基于物理图变换器的双手灵巧操作策略，通过图结构表示和物理先验注入，显著提升工具使用任务的性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双手灵巧操作工具使用在机器人领域面临高维状态空间和复杂接触动力学的挑战。现有方法将整个系统状态表示为单一配置向量，忽略了关节手的丰富结构和拓扑信息。

Method: 提出PhysGraph物理图变换器策略：1) 将双手系统表示为运动学图，引入每连杆标记化以保留细粒度局部状态信息；2) 设计物理基础偏置生成器，将结构先验（运动学空间距离、动态接触状态、几何接近度、解剖特性）直接注入注意力机制。

Result: PhysGraph在操作精度和任务成功率上显著优于基线ManipTrans，同时仅使用ManipTrans 51%的参数。架构的拓扑灵活性展示了对未见工具/物体几何的零样本迁移能力，并可在三种机器人手（Shadow、Allegro、Inspire）上训练。

Conclusion: PhysGraph通过图结构表示和物理先验注入，使策略能够显式推理物理交互而非从稀疏奖励中隐式学习，为双手灵巧操作提供了更有效和通用的解决方案。

Abstract: Bimanual dexterous manipulation for tool use remains a formidable challenge in robotics due to the high-dimensional state space and complicated contact dynamics. Existing methods naively represent the entire system state as a single configuration vector, disregarding the rich structural and topological information inherent to articulated hands. We present PhysGraph, a physically-grounded graph transformer policy designed explicitly for challenging bimanual hand-tool-object manipulation. Unlike prior works, we represent the bimanual system as a kinematic graph and introduce per-link tokenization to preserve fine-grained local state information. We propose a physically-grounded bias generator that injects structural priors directly into the attention mechanism, including kinematic spatial distance, dynamic contact states, geometric proximity, and anatomical properties. This allows the policy to explicitly reason about physical interactions rather than learning them implicitly from sparse rewards. Extensive experiments show that PhysGraph significantly outperforms baseline - ManipTrans in manipulation precision and task success rates while using only 51% of the parameters of ManipTrans. Furthermore, the inherent topological flexibility of our architecture shows qualitative zero-shot transfer to unseen tool/object geometries, and is sufficiently general to be trained on three robotic hands (Shadow, Allegro, Inspire).

</details>


### [104] [Non-Markovian Long-Horizon Robot Manipulation via Keyframe Chaining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01465)
*Yipeng Chen,Wentao Tan,Lei Zhu,Fengling Li,Jingjing Li,Guoli Yang,Heng Tao Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Keyframe-Chaining VLA框架，通过提取和链接关键历史帧来建模长时域依赖，解决现有VLA模型在非马尔可夫任务中的泛化问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型严重依赖即时观测，难以泛化到长时域任务。虽然近期研究引入检索机制或扩展上下文窗口，但仍难以捕捉非马尔可夫依赖关系（最优动作仅依赖于特定历史状态而非当前观测）

Method: 1) 自动关键帧选择器：学习判别性嵌入空间，有效识别不同的状态转换；2) 进度感知查询机制：基于当前执行阶段的时间相关性动态检索历史帧；3) 关键帧集成：将选定的关键帧作为交错视觉标记集成到VLA中，显式地将策略锚定在长时域时间上下文中

Result: 在基于ManiSkill模拟器构建的四个非马尔可夫操作任务上，该方法取得了优越性能，有效解决了具有长时域时间依赖的机器人操作任务

Conclusion: Keyframe-Chaining VLA框架通过提取和链接关键历史帧，成功建模了长时域依赖，为处理非马尔可夫机器人操作任务提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: Existing Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models often struggle to generalize to long-horizon tasks due to their heavy reliance on immediate observations. While recent studies incorporate retrieval mechanisms or extend context windows to handle procedural tasks, they often struggle to capture Non-Markovian dependencies, where optimal actions rely solely on specific past states rather than the current observation. To address this, we introduce Keyframe-Chaining VLA, a framework that extracts and links key historical frames to model long-horizon dependencies. Specifically, we propose an automatic keyframe selector that learns a discriminative embedding space, effectively identifying distinct state transitions. To capture task-critical information, we design a progress-aware query mechanism that dynamically retrieves historical frames based on their temporal relevance to the current execution phase. These selected keyframes are integrated into the VLA as interleaved visual tokens, explicitly grounding the policy in the long-horizon temporal context. Finally, we introduce a suite of four Non-Markovian manipulation tasks built upon the ManiSkill simulator to measure task success rates. Experimental results demonstrate that our method achieves superior performance, effectively tackling robot manipulation tasks characterized by long-horizon temporal dependencies. Code is available at https://github.com/cytoplastm/KC-VLA.

</details>


### [105] [Mean-Flow based One-Step Vision-Language-Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01469)
*Yang Chen,Xiaoguang Ma,Bin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于均值流的单步VLA方法，解决FlowMatching框架生成延迟问题，实现高效动作生成


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于FlowMatching的VLA框架虽然能生成高频动作块，但存在生成延迟问题，这源于迭代采样需求和架构限制，限制了实际应用

Method: 提出基于均值流的单步VLA方法，解决动作生成过程中的噪声诱导问题，消除传统Flow-Matching方法的一致性约束，实现单步动作生成

Result: 提出的方法生成速度比SmolVLA快8.7倍，比Diffusion Policy快83.9倍，在真实机器人实验中验证了其高效性

Conclusion: 该方法具有作为VLA机器人操作高效骨干的巨大潜力，显著提升了生成效率

Abstract: Recent advances in FlowMatching-based Vision-Language-Action (VLA) frameworks have demonstrated remarkable advantages in generating high-frequency action chunks, particularly for highly dexterous robotic manipulation tasks. Despite these notable achievements, their practical applications are constrained by prolonged generation latency, which stems from inherent iterative sampling requirements and architectural limitations. To address this critical bottleneck, we propose a Mean-Flow based One-Step VLA approach. Specifically, we resolve the noise-induced issues in the action generation process, thereby eliminating the consistency constraints inherent to conventional Flow-Matching methods. This significantly enhances generation efficiency and enables one-step action generation. Real-world robotic experiments show that the generation speed of the proposed Mean-Flow based One-Step VLA is 8.7 times and 83.9 times faster than that of SmolVLA and Diffusion Policy, respectively. These results elucidate its great potential as a high-efficiency backbone for VLA-based robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [106] [ROSER: Few-Shot Robotic Sequence Retrieval for Scalable Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01474)
*Zillur Rahman,Eddison Pham,Alejandro Daniel Noel,Cristian Meo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ROSER：一个轻量级少样本检索框架，从无标签的机器人交互日志中提取可重用的任务中心轨迹片段，仅需3-5个参考示例，无需任务特定训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习面临的关键瓶颈是缺乏任务标记、分段的训练数据，尽管存在大量以长连续交互日志形式记录的大规模机器人数据集。现有数据集包含大量多样化行为，但在结构上与需要干净分段、任务特定轨迹的现代学习框架不兼容。这导致了数据利用危机。

Method: 提出ROSER框架，将机器人序列检索形式化：从无标签日志中提取可重用、任务中心片段的少样本检索任务。ROSER学习时间窗口上的任务无关度量空间，仅需3-5个演示示例即可实现准确检索，无需任何任务特定训练。

Result: 在三个大规模数据集（LIBERO、DROID、nuScenes）上建立全面评估协议，将ROSER与经典对齐方法、学习嵌入和语言模型基线进行对比。ROSER在准确性和效率方面始终优于所有先前方法，实现每匹配亚毫秒级推理，同时保持优越的分布对齐。

Conclusion: 通过将数据整理重新定义为少样本检索，ROSER为解锁未充分利用的机器人数据集提供了实用途径，从根本上改善了机器人学习的数据可用性。

Abstract: A critical bottleneck in robot learning is the scarcity of task-labeled, segmented training data, despite the abundance of large-scale robotic datasets recorded as long, continuous interaction logs. Existing datasets contain vast amounts of diverse behaviors, yet remain structurally incompatible with modern learning frameworks that require cleanly segmented, task-specific trajectories. We address this data utilization crisis by formalizing robotic sequence retrieval: the task of extracting reusable, task-centric segments from unlabeled logs using only a few reference examples. We introduce ROSER, a lightweight few-shot retrieval framework that learns task-agnostic metric spaces over temporal windows, enabling accurate retrieval with as few as 3-5 demonstrations, without any task-specific training required. To validate our approach, we establish comprehensive evaluation protocols and benchmark ROSER against classical alignment methods, learned embeddings, and language model baselines across three large-scale datasets (e.g., LIBERO, DROID, and nuScenes). Our experiments demonstrate that ROSER consistently outperforms all prior methods in both accuracy and efficiency, achieving sub-millisecond per-match inference while maintaining superior distributional alignment. By reframing data curation as few-shot retrieval, ROSER provides a practical pathway to unlock underutilized robotic datasets, fundamentally improving data availability for robot learning.

</details>


### [107] [SFCo-Nav: Efficient Zero-Shot Visual Language Navigation via Collaboration of Slow LLM and Fast Attributed Graph Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01477)
*Chaoran Xiong,Litao Wei,Xinhao Hu,Kehui Ma,Ziyi Xia,Zixin Jiang,Zhen Sun,Ling Pei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SFCo-Nav：一种基于慢-快认知协作原理的高效零样本视觉语言导航框架，通过异步LLM触发机制显著降低计算成本和延迟，在保持导航成功率的同时实现超过50%的token消耗减少和3.5倍以上的速度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本视觉语言导航方法通常构建简单的观察图并进行每步VLM-LLM推理，导致高延迟和高计算成本，限制了实时部署。需要一种更高效的零样本VLN框架来降低计算开销同时保持导航性能。

Method: SFCo-Nav采用慢-快认知协作架构，包含三个核心模块：1）慢速LLM规划器生成战略性子目标链，每个子目标关联一个想象的对象图；2）快速反应导航器实时构建对象图并执行子目标；3）轻量级异步慢-快桥接器对齐结构化、带属性的想象图和感知图来估计导航置信度，仅在必要时触发慢速LLM规划器。

Result: 在R2R和REVERIE基准测试中，SFCo-Nav达到或超过先前最先进的零样本VLN成功率，同时将每轨迹总token消耗减少超过50%，运行速度提高3.5倍以上。在酒店套房的腿式机器人上展示了其室内环境中的效率和实用性。

Conclusion: SFCo-Nav是首个支持根据内部置信度异步触发LLM的慢-快协作零样本VLN系统，通过智能的慢-快认知协作机制显著提高了零样本视觉语言导航的效率，为实时机器人导航应用提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in large vision-language models (VLMs) and large language models (LLMs) have enabled zero-shot approaches to visual language navigation (VLN), where an agent follows natural language instructions using only ego perception and reasoning. However, existing zero-shot methods typically construct a naive observation graph and perform per-step VLM-LLM inference on it, resulting in high latency and computation costs that limit real-time deployment. To address this, we present SFCo-Nav, an efficient zero-shot VLN framework inspired by the principle of slow-fast cognitive collaboration. SFCo-Nav integrates three key modules: 1) a slow LLM-based planner that produces a strategic chain of subgoals, each linked to an imagined object graph; 2) a fast reactive navigator for real-time object graph construction and subgoal execution; and 3) a lightweight asynchronous slow-fast bridge aligns advanced structured, attributed imagined and perceived graphs to estimate navigation confidence, triggering the slow LLM planner only when necessary. To the best of our knowledge, SFCo-Nav is the first slow-fast collaboration zero-shot VLN system supporting asynchronous LLM triggering according to the internal confidence. Evaluated on the public R2R and REVERIE benchmarks, SFCo-Nav matches or exceeds prior state-of-the-art zero-shot VLN success rates while cutting total token consumption per trajectory by over 50% and running more than 3.5 times faster. Finally, we demonstrate SFCo-Nav on a legged robot in a hotel suite, showcasing its efficiency and practicality in indoor environments.

</details>


### [108] [Multimodal Adversarial Quality Policy for Safe Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01479)
*Kunlin Xie Chenghao Li Haolan Zhang,Nak Young Chong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MAQP框架实现多模态安全抓取，通过HDPOS缓解RGB与深度模态分布差异，GLMBS解决优化不平衡问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于DNN的视觉引导机器人抓取在HRI中存在安全风险，现有RGB模态对抗攻击方法对RGBD模态效果有限

Method: 提出MAQP框架：1) HDPOS采用模态特定初始化策略（深度用高斯分布，RGB用均匀分布）并在统一目标函数下联合优化；2) GLMBS基于通道敏感度分析重新加权梯度贡献并应用距离自适应扰动边界

Result: 在基准数据集和协作机器人上进行广泛实验，证明了MAQP的有效性

Conclusion: MAQP通过解决多模态分布差异和优化不平衡问题，实现了有效的多模态安全抓取

Abstract: Vision-guided robot grasping based on Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) generalizes well but poses safety risks in the Human-Robot Interaction (HRI). Recent works solved it by designing benign adversarial attacks and patches with RGB modality, yet depth-independent characteristics limit their effectiveness on RGBD modality. In this work, we propose the Multimodal Adversarial Quality Policy (MAQP) to realize multimodal safe grasping. Our framework introduces two key components. First, the Heterogeneous Dual-Patch Optimization Scheme (HDPOS) mitigates the distribution discrepancy between RGB and depth modalities in patch generation by adopting modality-specific initialization strategies, employing a Gaussian distribution for depth patches and a uniform distribution for RGB patches, while jointly optimizing both modalities under a unified objective function. Second, the Gradient-Level Modality Balancing Strategy (GLMBS) is designed to resolve the optimization imbalance from RGB and Depth patches in patch shape adaptation by reweighting gradient contributions based on per-channel sensitivity analysis and applying distance-adaptive perturbation bounds. We conduct extensive experiments on the benchmark datasets and a cobot, showing the effectiveness of MAQP.

</details>


### [109] [FATE: Closed-Loop Feasibility-Aware Task Generation with Active Repair for Physically Grounded Robotic Curricula](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01505)
*Bingchuan Wei,Bingqi Huang,Jingheng Ma,Zeyu zhang,Sen Cui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FATE是一个可行性感知的任务生成框架，通过闭环自校正机制将任务生成重构为迭代验证-精炼过程，确保机器人任务课程的物理可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的生成式模拟方法虽然能生成多样的机器人任务课程，但由于开环范式导致任务规范不接地气或目标公式错位，经常产生语言上连贯但物理上不可行的目标。这限制了生成任务的实际可用性。

Method: FATE采用闭环自校正框架，将通用具身智能体直接嵌入生成循环中。它实现了一个顺序审计管道：首先验证静态场景属性（如对象可供性、布局兼容性），然后通过模拟具身交互验证执行可行性。关键创新是主动修复模块，当检测到不可行任务时，自主调整场景配置或策略规范，将不可行提案转换为物理有效的任务实例。

Result: 大量实验验证表明，FATE能够生成语义多样且物理接地的任务课程，同时相对于最先进的生成基线方法，显著降低了执行失败率。

Conclusion: FATE通过将任务生成重构为迭代验证-精炼过程，成功解决了现有生成式模拟方法中物理可行性不足的问题，为机器人任务课程生成提供了更可靠、实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent breakthroughs in generative simulation have harnessed Large Language Models (LLMs) to generate diverse robotic task curricula, yet these open-loop paradigms frequently produce linguistically coherent but physically infeasible goals, stemming from ungrounded task specifications or misaligned objective formulations. To address this critical limitation, we propose FATE (Feasibility-Aware Task gEneration), a closed-loop, self-correcting framework that reimagines task generation as an iterative validation-and-refinement process. Unlike conventional methods that decouple generation and verification into discrete stages, FATE embeds a generalist embodied agent directly into the generation loop to proactively guarantee the physical groundedness of the resulting curriculum. FATE instantiates a sequential auditing pipeline: it first validates static scene attributes (e.g., object affordances, layout compatibility) and subsequently verifies execution feasibility via simulated embodied interaction. Critical to its performance, upon detecting an infeasible task, FATE deploys an active repair module that autonomously adapts scene configurations or policy specifications, converting unworkable proposals into physically valid task instances. Extensive experiments validate that FATE generates semantically diverse, physically grounded task curricula while achieving a substantial reduction in execution failure rates relative to state-of-the-art generative baselines.

</details>


### [110] [(hu)Man vs. Machine: In the Future of Motorsport, can Autonomous Vehicles Compete?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01560)
*Armand Amaritei,Amber-Lily Blackadder,Sebastian Donnelly,Lora Hernandez,James Vine,Alexander Rast,Matthias Rolf,Andrew Bradley*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 评估人类与自动驾驶车辆在赛车运动中同场竞技的可能性，分析技术性能、策略挑战和安全问题，提出未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 赛车运动历来是汽车技术创新的试验场，自动驾驶赛车可以推动AV系统性能极限。虽然AV在理论上可能比人类更快，但人类赛车具有更广泛的观众吸引力和战略挑战性。目前两者竞赛分离，本文探讨未来是否可以实现人类与AV同场竞技。

Method: 分析当前技术现状和近期竞赛结果，评估技术性能、策略挑战和安全问题。探讨混合人机赛车中的关键问题，包括系统级延迟、有效规划和风险保证等。同时考虑观众参与度和吸引力的非技术方面。

Result: 技术性能已达到可比水平，但在赛车技艺、策略和安全方面仍存在重大挑战。混合人机赛车面临系统延迟、规划算法和风险保证等技术问题，以及观众参与度等非技术挑战。

Conclusion: 虽然人类与自动驾驶车辆同场竞技在技术上是可行的，但需要克服赛车技艺、策略和安全等多方面挑战。未来研究应关注系统延迟、规划算法、风险保证等关键技术问题，同时考虑观众体验，以推动赛车运动在自动驾驶时代的发展。

Abstract: Motorsport has historically driven technological innovation in the automotive industry. Autonomous racing provides a proving ground to push the limits of performance of autonomous vehicle (AV) systems. In principle, AVs could be at least as fast, if not faster, than humans. However, human driven racing provides broader audience appeal thus far, and is more strategically challenging. Both provide opportunities to push each other even further technologically, yet competitions remain separate. This paper evaluates whether the future of motorsport could encompass joint competition between humans and AVs. Analysis of the current state of the art, as well as recent competition outcomes, shows that while technical performance has reached comparable levels, there are substantial challenges in racecraft, strategy and safety that need to be overcome. Outstanding issues involved in mixed human-AI racing, ranging from an initial assessment of critical factors such as system-level latencies, to effective planning and risk guarantees are explored. The crucial non-technical aspect of audience engagement and appeal regarding the changing character of motorsport is addressed. In the wider context of motorsport and AVs, this work outlines a proposed agenda for future research to 'keep pushing the possible', in the true spirit of motorsport.

</details>


### [111] [KERV: Kinematic-Rectified Speculative Decoding for Embodied VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01581)
*Zihao Zheng,Zhihao Mao,Maoliang Li,Jiayu Chen,Xinhao Sun,Zhaobo Zhang,Donggang Cao,Hong Mei,Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: KERV框架创新性地将基于token的VLA模型与基于运动学的预测相结合，通过运动学修正的推测解码技术，在不损失成功率的情况下实现27%~37%的推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在机器人控制中存在推理速度慢的问题，而传统的推测解码技术存在两个关键缺陷：1）依赖计算昂贵的重新推理来处理token错误；2）接受阈值需要仔细调整且难以确定。同时，现有具身智能研究忽视了机器人运动学的应用。

Method: 提出KERV框架，将token域的VLA模型与运动学域的预测相结合。采用基于运动学的卡尔曼滤波器预测动作并补偿推测解码错误，避免昂贵的重新推理。设计基于运动学的调整策略动态修正接受阈值，解决阈值确定难题。

Result: 在多种任务和环境中的实验结果表明，KERV能够实现27%~37%的推理加速，同时几乎不损失成功率。

Conclusion: KERV通过将运动学预测与推测解码相结合，有效解决了VLA模型推理速度慢的问题，同时避免了传统推测解码技术的缺陷，为具身智能系统提供了高效的推理加速方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models build a token-domain robot control paradigm, yet suffer from low speed. Speculative Decoding (SD) is an optimization strategy that can boost inference speed. Two key issues emerge when integrating VLA and SD: first, SD relies on re-inference to address token errors, which is computationally expensive; second, to mitigate token errors, the acceptance threshold in SD requires careful adjustment. Existing works fail to address the above two issues effectively. Meanwhile, as the bridge between AI and the physical world, existing embodied intelligence has overlooked the application of robotic kinematics. To address these issues, we innovatively combine token-domain VLA models with kinematic-domain prediction for SD, proposing a kinematic-rectified SD framework named KERV. We employ a kinematics-based Kalman Filter to predict actions and compensate for SD errors, avoiding costly re-inference. Moreover, we design a kinematics-based adjustment strategy to dynamically rectify the acceptance threshold, addressing the difficulty of threshold determination. Experimental results across diverse tasks and environments demonstrate that KERV achieves 27%~37% acceleration with nearly no Success Rate loss.

</details>


### [112] [Learning Thermal-Aware Locomotion Policies for an Electrically-Actuated Quadruped Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01631)
*Letian Qian,Yuhang Wan,Shuhan Wang,Xin Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种热感知控制方法，将电机温度纳入强化学习运动策略，通过热约束奖励防止温度超标，显著延长四足机器人在负载条件下的连续运行时间


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动四足机器人在复杂地形上具有高机动性，但其电机在高扭矩循环负载下容易积累热量，可能触发过热保护机制，从而限制长时间任务的执行能力

Method: 提出热感知控制方法：1) 将电机温度状态纳入强化学习运动策略的观测空间；2) 引入热约束奖励函数，在训练过程中惩罚温度超过安全阈值的行为；3) 在Unitree A1机器人上进行真实世界实验验证

Result: 在固定3kg负载条件下：基线策略约7分钟触发过热保护停机；所提方法可连续运行超过27分钟无热中断，同时保持相当的命令跟踪性能

Conclusion: 热感知控制方法能有效防止电机过热，显著延长四足机器人的可持续运行时间，增强其在长时间任务中的实用性，同时不牺牲运动性能

Abstract: Electrically-actuated quadrupedal robots possess high mobility on complex terrains, but their motors tend to accumulate heat under high-torque cyclic loads, potentially triggering overheat protection and limiting long-duration tasks. This work proposes a thermal-aware control method that incorporates motor temperatures into reinforcement learning locomotion policies and introduces thermal-constraint rewards to prevent temperature exceedance. Real-world experiments on the Unitree A1 demonstrate that, under a fixed 3 kg payload, the baseline policy triggers overheat protection and stops within approximately 7 minutes, whereas the proposed method can operate continuously for over 27 minutes without thermal interruptions while maintaining comparable command-tracking performance, thereby enhancing sustainable operational capability.

</details>


### [113] [B$^2$F-Map: Crowd-sourced Mapping with Bayesian B-spline Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01673)
*Yiping Xie,Yuxuan Xia,Erik Stenborg,Junsheng Fu,Axel Beauvisage,Gabriel E. Garcia,Tianyu Wu,Gustaf Hendeby*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: B²F-Map：一种基于众包数据的完整高精地图生成流程，使用单目相机、消费级GNSS和IMU，通过贝叶斯B样条融合处理不确定性，无需先验高精地图


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有众包建图方法要么依赖先验高精地图，要么忽视地图融合中的不确定性，需要一种仅使用低成本传感器且能处理不确定性的完整建图方案

Method: 包含三阶段流程：1) 基于轻量标准地图的云端定位；2) 车载端使用扩展对象轨迹PMB滤波器和吉布斯采样进行建图；3) 云端多行程优化和贝叶斯地图融合，使用高斯分布控制点的B样条表示车道线

Result: 在多样化驾驶条件下的大规模真实数据集上评估，B²F-Map能够生成几何一致的车道级地图

Conclusion: 提出的完整流程能够仅使用低成本传感器生成高精地图，通过贝叶斯融合框架处理不确定性，实现了几何一致的众包建图

Abstract: Crowd-sourced mapping offers a scalable alternative to creating maps using traditional survey vehicles. Yet, existing methods either rely on prior high-definition (HD) maps or neglect uncertainties in the map fusion. In this work, we present a complete pipeline for HD map generation using production vehicles equipped only with a monocular camera, consumer-grade GNSS, and IMU. Our approach includes on-cloud localization using lightweight standard-definition maps, on-vehicle mapping via an extended object trajectory (EOT) Poisson multi-Bernoulli (PMB) filter with Gibbs sampling, and on-cloud multi-drive optimization and Bayesian map fusion. We represent the lane lines using B-splines, where each B-spline is parameterized by a sequence of Gaussian distributed control points, and propose a novel Bayesian fusion framework for B-spline trajectories with differing density representation, enabling principled handling of uncertainties. We evaluate our proposed approach, B$^2$F-Map, on large-scale real-world datasets collected across diverse driving conditions and demonstrate that our method is able to produce geometrically consistent lane-level maps.

</details>


### [114] [TacMamba: A Tactile History Compression Adapter Bridging Fast Reflexes and Slow VLA Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01700)
*Zhenan Wang,Yanzhe Wang,Meixuan Ren,Peng Li,Yang Liu,Yifei Nie,Limin Long,Yun Ye,Xiaofeng Wang,Zhen Zhu,Huixu Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacMamba：一种分层架构，通过Mamba-based Tactile History Compressor将高频触觉反射与低频视觉规划对齐，解决触觉与视觉融合中的时空不匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在视觉模糊的操作任务中（如检测按钮点击），触觉反馈通常是唯一的地面真实来源。然而，融合触觉数据面临重大挑战，因为存在时空不匹配：触觉感知需要高频处理与长时记忆（系统1），而视觉策略在低控制频率下运行（系统2）。现有架构难以弥合这一差距：Transformer在高频循环（>100Hz）中计算成本过高，而LSTM在长时间交互历史中会遗忘信息。

Method: TacMamba包含三个核心贡献：(1) 为灵活集成设计的定制高频触觉接口；(2) 基于Mamba的触觉历史压缩器，将连续力历史编码为紧凑状态，具有O(1)推理延迟（0.45ms），无需联合预训练即可与VLA模型即插即用融合；(3) 触觉引导的双阶段训练策略，利用时间判别进行自监督表示学习，并使用相位均匀采样缓解数据稀疏性。

Result: 在离散计数和隐式状态切换任务上的实验表明，TacMamba实现了100%的成功率，显著优于仅视觉的pi_0.5基线，同时严格满足硬实时约束。

Conclusion: TacMamba成功解决了触觉与视觉融合中的时空不匹配问题，通过分层架构将高频触觉反射与低频视觉规划对齐，实现了高效、实时的多模态感知融合。

Abstract: In visually ambiguous manipulation such as detecting button click tactile feedback is often the sole source of ground truth. However, fusing tactile data poses a significant challenge due to a spatiotemporal mismatch: tactile perception requires high-frequency processing with long-horizon memory (System 1), whereas visual policies operate at low control frequencies (System 2). Existing architectures struggle to bridge this gap: Transformers are computationally prohibitive for high-frequency loops (>100Hz), while LSTMs suffer from forgetting over extended interaction histories. In this paper, we introduce TacMamba, a hierarchical architecture that aligns high-bandwidth tactile reflexes with low-frequency visual planning. Our approach comprises three core contributions: (1) a custom high-frequency tactile interface designed for flexible integration; (2) a Mamba-based Tactile History Compressor that encodes continuous force history into a compact state with O(1) inference latency (0.45 ms), enabling plug-and-play fusion with VLA models without joint pre-training and (3) a Tactile-Guided Dual-Stage Training strategy that leverages temporal discrimination for self-supervised representation learning and phase-uniform sampling to mitigate data sparsity. Experiments on discrete counting and implicit state switching demonstrate that TacMamba achieves 100% success rates, significantly outperforming the visual-only pi_0.5 baseline, while strictly satisfying hard real-time constraints.

</details>


### [115] [A Safety-Aware Shared Autonomy Framework with BarrierIK Using Control Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01705)
*Berk Guler,Kay Pompetzki,Yuanzheng Sun,Simon Manschitz,Jan Peters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在共享自主系统中，通过控制屏障函数（CBFs）在逆运动学层实现安全过滤，确保混合后的指令在杂乱环境中保持安全，同时维持任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在杂乱环境中，线性混合操作者意图和自主辅助指令可能产生不安全指令，即使每个源本身是避碰的。现有方法通过势场或代价项建模避障，仅作为软约束，缺乏安全关键控制所需的硬性保证。

Method: 在共享自主系统的逆运动学层应用控制屏障函数（CBFs），作为安全过滤器，确保混合后指令的安全性，同时保持任务性能。

Result: 仿真和VR遥操作研究表明：在逆运动学层使用CBFs减少了违规时间，增加了最小间隙，同时保持了任务性能。用户研究显示参与者报告了更高的感知安全性和信任度、更低的干扰，并总体上偏好带有安全过滤器的共享自主系统。

Conclusion: 在逆运动学层应用控制屏障函数为共享自主系统提供了安全关键控制的有效方法，能够在杂乱环境中提供硬性安全保证，同时保持任务性能，并提高用户感知安全性和信任度。

Abstract: Shared autonomy blends operator intent with autonomous assistance. In cluttered environments, linear blending can produce unsafe commands even when each source is individually collision-free. Many existing approaches model obstacle avoidance through potentials or cost terms, which only enforce safety as a soft constraint. In contrast, safety-critical control requires hard guarantees. We investigate the use of control barrier functions (CBFs) at the inverse kinematics (IK) layer of shared autonomy, targeting post-blend safety while preserving task performance. Our approach is evaluated in simulation on representative cluttered environments and in a VR teleoperation study comparing pure teleoperation with shared autonomy. Across conditions, employing CBFs at the IK layer reduces violation time and increases minimum clearance while maintaining task performance. In the user study, participants reported higher perceived safety and trust, lower interference, and an overall preference for shared autonomy with our safety filter. Additional materials available at https://berkguler.github.io/barrierik.

</details>


### [116] [Neural Implicit Action Fields: From Discrete Waypoints to Continuous Functions for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01766)
*Haoyun Liu,Jianzhuang Zhao,Xinyuan Chang,Tianle Shi,Chuanzhang Meng,Jiayuan Tan,Feng Xiong,Tong Lin,Dongjie Huo,Mu Xu,SongLin Dong,Zhiheng Ma,Yihong Gong,Sheng Zhong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NIAF将VLA模型从离散路径点预测转变为连续动作函数回归，通过MLLM作为分层谱调制器，生成无限分辨率的连续轨迹，实现解析可微分性和物理一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型预测离散路径点的方法与物理运动的连续性本质不匹配，存在采样率僵化、缺乏高阶可微性、量化伪影等问题，阻碍了精确、柔顺的交互。

Method: 提出神经隐式动作场(NIAF)，将动作预测重构为连续动作函数回归。使用MLLM作为分层谱调制器，在可学习的运动先验上合成无限分辨率的连续时间流形，支持速度、加速度和加加速度的显式监督。

Result: 在CALVIN和LIBERO基准测试中取得最先进结果，并在真实世界实验中实现了稳定的阻抗控制，弥合了高层语义理解与低层动态执行之间的差距。

Conclusion: NIAF通过连续动作函数回归范式，解决了离散路径点预测的固有限制，为VLA模型提供了数学一致、物理可信的动作生成能力，实现了从语义理解到动态执行的桥梁。

Abstract: Despite the rapid progress of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, the prevailing paradigm of predicting discrete waypoints remains fundamentally misaligned with the intrinsic continuity of physical motion. This discretization imposes rigid sampling rates, lacks high-order differentiability, and introduces quantization artifacts that hinder precise, compliant interaction. We propose Neural Implicit Action Fields (NIAF), a paradigm shift that reformulates action prediction from discrete waypoints to continuous action function regression. By utilizing an MLLM as a hierarchical spectral modulator over a learnable motion prior, NIAF synthesizes infinite-resolution trajectories as continuous-time manifolds. This formulation enables analytical differentiability, allowing for explicit supervision of velocity, acceleration, and jerk to ensure mathematical consistency and physical plausibility. Our approach achieves state-of-the-art results on CALVIN and LIBERO benchmarks across diverse backbones. Furthermore, real-world experiments demonstrate that NIAF enables stable impedance control, bridging the gap between high-level semantic understanding and low-level dynamic execution.

</details>


### [117] [SaferPath: Hierarchical Visual Navigation with Learned Guidance and Safety-Constrained Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01898)
*Lingjie Zhang,Zeyu Jiang,Changhao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SaferPath：一种分层视觉导航框架，利用端到端模型的学习指导，通过安全约束优化控制模块进行细化，在复杂环境中实现安全导航


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端学习导航方法在未见、杂乱或狭窄环境中泛化性和安全性不足，特别是在密集室内环境中容易发生碰撞。需要一种能够结合学习模型优势同时确保安全性的导航框架。

Method: 提出SaferPath分层框架：1) 将视觉观察转换为可通行区域地图；2) 利用端到端模型提供初始指导轨迹；3) 使用模型预测Stein变分进化策略(MP-SVES)进行安全约束优化，在少量迭代中生成安全轨迹；4) 通过MPC控制器跟踪优化后的轨迹

Result: 在未见障碍物、密集非结构化空间和狭窄走廊等场景中的广泛实验表明，SaferPath持续提高成功率、减少碰撞，优于ViNT和NoMaD等基线方法，能够在具有挑战性的真实世界环境中实现安全导航

Conclusion: SaferPath通过结合学习指导和基于优化的安全约束，有效解决了端到端视觉导航方法在复杂环境中的泛化性和安全性问题，为移动机器人在密集室内环境中的安全导航提供了可靠解决方案

Abstract: Visual navigation is a core capability for mobile robots, yet end-to-end learning-based methods often struggle with generalization and safety in unseen, cluttered, or narrow environments. These limitations are especially pronounced in dense indoor settings, where collisions are likely and end-to-end models frequently fail. To address this, we propose SaferPath, a hierarchical visual navigation framework that leverages learned guidance from existing end-to-end models and refines it through a safety-constrained optimization-control module. SaferPath transforms visual observations into a traversable-area map and refines guidance trajectories using Model Predictive Stein Variational Evolution Strategy (MP-SVES), efficiently generating safe trajectories in only a few iterations. The refined trajectories are tracked by an MPC controller, ensuring robust navigation in complex environments. Extensive experiments in scenarios with unseen obstacles, dense unstructured spaces, and narrow corridors demonstrate that SaferPath consistently improves success rates and reduces collisions, outperforming representative baselines such as ViNT and NoMaD, and enabling safe navigation in challenging real-world settings.

</details>


### [118] [Closed-Loop Action Chunks with Dynamic Corrections for Training-Free Diffusion Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01953)
*Pengyuan Wu,Pingrui Zhang,Zhigang Wang,Dong Wang,Bin Zhao,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DCDP是一个动态闭环扩散策略框架，通过分块动作生成和实时校正，在动态机器人操作场景中实现更好的适应性和实时响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散的策略在机器人操作中表现出色，但在动态场景中适应缓慢，导致响应延迟或任务失败，需要改进实时适应能力。

Method: DCDP框架整合了自监督动态特征编码器、交叉注意力融合和非对称动作编码器-解码器，在执行前注入环境动态信息，实现实时闭环动作校正。

Result: 在动态PushT仿真中，DCDP无需重新训练即可提高19%的适应性，仅需5%的额外计算开销。模块化设计支持即插即用集成，在包括真实世界操作任务在内的动态机器人场景中实现时间一致性和实时响应性。

Conclusion: DCDP通过动态特征编码和实时校正机制，显著提升了扩散策略在动态场景中的适应性和响应速度，同时保持计算效率。

Abstract: Diffusion-based policies have achieved remarkable results in robotic manipulation but often struggle to adapt rapidly in dynamic scenarios, leading to delayed responses or task failures. We present DCDP, a Dynamic Closed-Loop Diffusion Policy framework that integrates chunk-based action generation with real-time correction. DCDP integrates a self-supervised dynamic feature encoder, cross-attention fusion, and an asymmetric action encoder-decoder to inject environmental dynamics before action execution, achieving real-time closed-loop action correction and enhancing the system's adaptability in dynamic scenarios. In dynamic PushT simulations, DCDP improves adaptability by 19\% without retraining while requiring only 5\% additional computation. Its modular design enables plug-and-play integration, achieving both temporal coherence and real-time responsiveness in dynamic robotic scenarios, including real-world manipulation tasks. The project page is at: https://github.com/wupengyuan/dcdp

</details>


### [119] [From Transportation to Manipulation: Transforming Magnetic Levitation to Magnetic Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01982)
*Lara Bergmann,Noah Greis,Klaus Neumann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种低成本六自由度并联运动平台6D-Platform MagBot，它将两个磁悬浮移动器耦合为复合机器人平台，实现了毫米级定位精度和自主对接，扩展了工作空间、负载和功能灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁悬浮系统在工业自动化中主要应用于物料输送，但其在操作方面的潜力尚未充分开发。当前需要将运输和操作功能统一，以实现更灵活、高效和适应性强的制造解决方案。

Method: 设计并构建了6D-Platform MagBot，这是一种低成本六自由度并联运动平台，通过将两个磁悬浮移动器耦合形成复合机器人平台。系统支持通过对接站实现完全自主的拾取和放置，允许快速可重复的机器重构。

Result: 实验表明，6D-Platform MagBot实现了亚毫米级定位精度，相对于单个移动器，显著扩展了可达工作空间、有效载荷和功能灵活性。平台支持完全自主的拾取和放置操作。

Conclusion: 通过统一运输和操作功能，这项工作将磁悬浮技术推向磁悬浮机器人领域，为实现更敏捷、高效和适应性强的制造解决方案奠定了基础。

Abstract: Magnetic Levitation (MagLev) systems fundamentally increase the flexibility of in-machine material flow in industrial automation. Therefore, these systems enable dynamic throughput optimization, which is especially beneficial for high-mix low-volume manufacturing. Until now, MagLev installations have been used primarily for in-machine transport, while their potential for manipulation is largely unexplored. This paper introduces the 6D-Platform MagBot, a low-cost six degrees of freedom parallel kinematic that couples two movers into a composite robotic platform. Experiments show that the 6D-Platform MagBot achieves sub-millimeter positioning accuracy and supports fully autonomous pick up and drop off via a docking station, allowing rapid and repeatable reconfiguration of the machine. Relative to a single mover, the proposed platform substantially expands the reachable workspace, payload, and functional dexterity. By unifying transportation and manipulation, this work advances Magnetic Levitation towards Magnetic Robotics, enabling manufacturing solutions that are more agile, efficient, and adaptable.

</details>


### [120] [Learning Vision-Based Omnidirectional Navigation: A Teacher-Student Approach Using Monocular Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01999)
*Jan Finke,Wayne Paul Martis,Adrian Schmelter,Lars Erbach,Christian Jestel,Marvin Wiedemann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于视觉的移动机器人导航师生框架，用单目深度估计替代2D LiDAR，解决工业环境中3D障碍物检测问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业环境中可靠的障碍物规避需要3D场景理解，但广泛使用的2D LiDAR传感器只能感知环境的单个水平切片，会错过扫描平面上方或下方的关键障碍物

Method: 采用师生框架：教师策略使用PPO算法在NVIDIA Isaac Lab中训练，利用特权2D LiDAR观测学习鲁棒导航；学生策略通过蒸馏学习，仅依赖四个RGB摄像头通过微调的Depth Anything V2模型预测的单目深度图

Result: 仿真中学生策略成功率82-96.5%，始终优于标准2D LiDAR教师策略（50-89%）；真实实验中，MDE学生在导航具有复杂3D几何形状的障碍物（如悬垂结构和低矮物体）时优于2D LiDAR教师

Conclusion: 基于视觉的导航系统能够有效替代LiDAR，在复杂3D环境中提供更好的障碍物规避性能，且整个推理管道可在嵌入式设备上运行，无需外部计算

Abstract: Reliable obstacle avoidance in industrial settings demands 3D scene understanding, but widely used 2D LiDAR sensors perceive only a single horizontal slice of the environment, missing critical obstacles above or below the scan plane. We present a teacher-student framework for vision-based mobile robot navigation that eliminates the need for LiDAR sensors. A teacher policy trained via Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) in NVIDIA Isaac Lab leverages privileged 2D LiDAR observations that account for the full robot footprint to learn robust navigation. The learned behavior is distilled into a student policy that relies solely on monocular depth maps predicted by a fine-tuned Depth Anything V2 model from four RGB cameras. The complete inference pipeline, comprising monocular depth estimation (MDE), policy execution, and motor control, runs entirely onboard an NVIDIA Jetson Orin AGX mounted on a DJI RoboMaster platform, requiring no external computation for inference. In simulation, the student achieves success rates of 82-96.5%, consistently outperforming the standard 2D LiDAR teacher (50-89%). In real-world experiments, the MDE-based student outperforms the 2D LiDAR teacher when navigating around obstacles with complex 3D geometries, such as overhanging structures and low-profile objects, that fall outside the single scan plane of a 2D LiDAR.

</details>


### [121] [CHOP: Counterfactual Human Preference Labels Improve Obstacle Avoidance in Visuomotor Navigation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02004)
*Gershom Seneviratne,Jianyu An,Vaibhav Shende,Sahire Ellahy,Yaxita Amin,Kondapi Manasanjani,Samarth Chopra,Jonathan Deepak Kannan,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CHOP利用反事实人类偏好标签来对齐视觉运动导航策略，通过人类对替代轨迹的偏好标注来提升机器人的安全导航和障碍物避让能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉运动导航策略在复杂现实环境中存在安全导航和动态障碍物避让的困难，需要更好地与人类对安全性和避障的直觉认知对齐。

Method: 提出CHOP方法：针对每个视觉观察，收集机器人实际执行轨迹和反事实替代轨迹；人类标注者基于碰撞风险和路径效率提供成对偏好标签；利用聚合的偏好数据微调视觉运动导航策略。

Result: 在SCAND数据集上，CHOP微调的策略将近碰撞事件减少49.7%，与人类偏好轨迹的偏差降低45.0%，平均障碍物间距增加19.8%。在Ghost Robotics Vision60四足机器人上，目标成功率提高24.4%，最小障碍物间距增加6.8%，碰撞和干预事件减少45.7%，归一化路径完成度提升38.6%。

Conclusion: 反事实偏好监督能有效弥合大规模视觉运动策略与人类对齐、安全感知的具身导航之间的差距，显著提升导航安全性和人类偏好对齐度。

Abstract: Visuomotor navigation policies have shown strong perception-action coupling for embodied agents, yet they often struggle with safe navigation and dynamic obstacle avoidance in complex real-world environments. We introduce CHOP, a novel approach that leverages Counterfactual Human Preference Labels to align visuomotor navigation policies towards human intuition of safety and obstacle avoidance in navigation. In CHOP, for each visual observation, the robot's executed trajectory is included among a set of counterfactual navigation trajectories: alternative trajectories the robot could have followed under identical conditions. Human annotators provide pairwise preference labels over these trajectories based on anticipated outcomes such as collision risk and path efficiency. These aggregated preferences are then used to fine-tune visuomotor navigation policies, aligning their behavior with human preferences in navigation. Experiments on the SCAND dataset show that visuomotor navigation policies fine-tuned with CHOP reduce near-collision events by 49.7%, decrease deviation from human-preferred trajectories by 45.0%, and increase average obstacle clearance by 19.8% on average across multiple state-of-the-art models, compared to their pretrained baselines. These improvements transfer to real-world deployments on a Ghost Robotics Vision60 quadruped, where CHOP-aligned policies improve average goal success rates by 24.4%, increase minimum obstacle clearance by 6.8%, reduce collision and intervention events by 45.7%, and improve normalized path completion by 38.6% on average across navigation scenarios, compared to their pretrained baselines. Our results highlight the value of counterfactual preference supervision in bridging the gap between large-scale visuomotor policies and human-aligned, safety-aware embodied navigation.

</details>


### [122] [LAD-Drive: Bridging Language and Trajectory with Action-Aware Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02035)
*Fabian Schmidt,Karol Fedurko,Markus Enzweiler,Abhinav Valada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LAD-Drive是一个生成式自动驾驶框架，通过解耦高层意图和低层空间规划，使用概率元动作分布和动作感知扩散解码器，将MLLM的语义知识转化为连续轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在两个主要问题：1）单模态规划头限制了多模态驾驶行为的表示能力；2）生成方法通常基于one-hot编码的动作，丢弃了复杂场景中关键的导航不确定性。需要将MLLM的离散语义知识转化为连续轨迹。

Method: LAD-Drive采用结构化解耦方法：1）动作解码器推断概率元动作分布，建立显式信念状态；2）该分布与车辆运动状态融合，条件化动作感知扩散解码器；3）使用截断去噪过程将学习的运动锚点细化为安全、运动学可行的轨迹。

Result: 在LangAuto基准测试中，LAD-Drive实现了最先进的结果，比竞争基线在驾驶分数上提升高达59%，同时显著减少了路线偏差和碰撞。

Conclusion: LAD-Drive通过解耦意图和规划、保留导航不确定性，成功地将MLLM的语义知识转化为连续轨迹，为自动驾驶提供了有效的生成式框架。

Abstract: While multimodal large language models (MLLMs) provide advanced reasoning for autonomous driving, translating their discrete semantic knowledge into continuous trajectories remains a fundamental challenge. Existing methods often rely on unimodal planning heads that inherently limit their ability to represent multimodal driving behavior. Furthermore, most generative approaches frequently condition on one-hot encoded actions, discarding the nuanced navigational uncertainty critical for complex scenarios. To resolve these limitations, we introduce LAD-Drive, a generative framework that structurally disentangles high-level intention from low-level spatial planning. LAD-Drive employs an action decoder to infer a probabilistic meta-action distribution, establishing an explicit belief state that preserves the nuanced intent typically lost by one-hot encodings. This distribution, fused with the vehicle's kinematic state, conditions an action-aware diffusion decoder that utilizes a truncated denoising process to refine learned motion anchors into safe, kinematically feasible trajectories. Extensive evaluations on the LangAuto benchmark demonstrate that LAD-Drive achieves state-of-the-art results, outperforming competitive baselines by up to 59% in Driving Score while significantly reducing route deviations and collisions. We will publicly release the code and models on https://github.com/iis-esslingen/lad-drive.

</details>


### [123] [ACDC: Adaptive Curriculum Planning with Dynamic Contrastive Control for Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02104)
*Xuerui Wang,Guangyu Ren,Tianhong Dai,Bintao Hu,Shuangyao Huang,Wenzhang Zhang,Hengyan Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ACDC：一种结合自适应课程规划与动态对比控制的目标导向强化学习框架，通过多维课程调度和对比学习提升机器人操作任务的样本效率和成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有目标导向强化学习方法主要依赖经验优先级，导致在不同任务上性能受限。受人类学习行为启发，需要更全面的学习范式来引导智能体沿着精心设计的轨迹学习。

Method: 提出ACDC框架，包含两个核心组件：1) 自适应课程(AC)规划：在规划层面动态平衡多样性驱动的探索和质量驱动的利用，基于智能体成功率和训练进度调度学习课程；2) 动态对比(DC)控制：在控制层面通过范数约束对比学习实现课程计划，支持与当前课程重点对齐的幅度引导经验选择。

Result: 在具有挑战性的机器人操作任务上进行广泛实验，ACDC在样本效率和最终任务成功率方面均持续优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: ACDC通过整合多维自适应课程规划和动态对比控制，为目标导向强化学习提供了一个更全面的学习范式，显著提升了机器人操作任务的性能表现。

Abstract: Goal-conditioned reinforcement learning has shown considerable potential in robotic manipulation; however, existing approaches remain limited by their reliance on prioritizing collected experience, resulting in suboptimal performance across diverse tasks. Inspired by human learning behaviors, we propose a more comprehensive learning paradigm, ACDC, which integrates multidimensional Adaptive Curriculum (AC) Planning with Dynamic Contrastive (DC) Control to guide the agent along a well-designed learning trajectory. More specifically, at the planning level, the AC component schedules the learning curriculum by dynamically balancing diversity-driven exploration and quality-driven exploitation based on the agent's success rate and training progress. At the control level, the DC component implements the curriculum plan through norm-constrained contrastive learning, enabling magnitude-guided experience selection aligned with the current curriculum focus. Extensive experiments on challenging robotic manipulation tasks demonstrate that ACDC consistently outperforms the state-of-the-art baselines in both sample efficiency and final task success rate.

</details>


### [124] [Robometer: Scaling General-Purpose Robotic Reward Models via Trajectory Comparisons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02115)
*Anthony Liang,Yigit Korkmaz,Jiahui Zhang,Minyoung Hwang,Abrar Anwar,Sidhant Kaushik,Aditya Shah,Alex S. Huang,Luke Zettlemoyer,Dieter Fox,Yu Xiang,Anqi Li,Andreea Bobu,Abhishek Gupta,Stephen Tu,Erdem Biyik,Jesse Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Robometer是一个可扩展的机器人奖励建模框架，结合轨迹内进度监督和轨迹间偏好监督，通过双目标训练学习更通用的奖励函数，在多样化下游应用中提升机器人学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统通用机器人奖励模型通常基于专家演示预测绝对任务进度，仅提供局部、帧级监督。这种方法在专家演示上有效，但难以扩展到大规模机器人数据集，因为其中包含大量失败和次优轨迹，且分配密集进度标签存在模糊性。

Method: Robometer采用双目标训练：1) 帧级进度损失，在专家数据上锚定奖励幅度；2) 轨迹比较偏好损失，对同一任务的不同轨迹施加全局排序约束。该方法支持从真实和增强的失败轨迹中有效学习。为支持大规模训练，作者构建了RBM-1M数据集，包含超过100万条轨迹，涵盖多样化机器人本体和任务。

Result: 在基准测试和真实世界评估中，Robometer学习到的奖励函数比先前方法更具泛化性，在多样化下游应用中提升了机器人学习性能。

Conclusion: Robometer通过结合轨迹内进度监督和轨迹间偏好监督，解决了大规模机器人数据集中奖励建模的扩展性问题，学习到更通用的奖励函数，显著提升了机器人学习性能。

Abstract: General-purpose robot reward models are typically trained to predict absolute task progress from expert demonstrations, providing only local, frame-level supervision. While effective for expert demonstrations, this paradigm scales poorly to large-scale robotics datasets where failed and suboptimal trajectories are abundant and assigning dense progress labels is ambiguous. We introduce Robometer, a scalable reward modeling framework that combines intra-trajectory progress supervision with inter-trajectory preference supervision. Robometer is trained with a dual objective: a frame-level progress loss that anchors reward magnitude on expert data, and a trajectory-comparison preference loss that imposes global ordering constraints across trajectories of the same task, enabling effective learning from both real and augmented failed trajectories. To support this formulation at scale, we curate RBM-1M, a reward-learning dataset comprising over one million trajectories spanning diverse robot embodiments and tasks, including substantial suboptimal and failure data. Across benchmarks and real-world evaluations, Robometer learns more generalizable reward functions than prior methods and improves robot learning performance across a diverse set of downstream applications. Code, model weights, and videos at https://robometer.github.io/.

</details>


### [125] [Rethinking Camera Choice: An Empirical Study on Fisheye Camera Properties in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02139)
*Han Xue,Nan Min,Xiaotong Liu,Wendi Chen,Yuan Fang,Jun Lv,Cewu Lu,Chuan Wen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 鱼眼相机在机器人操作中的应用日益广泛，但其对策略学习的影响缺乏系统研究。本文首次通过实证研究分析了腕戴式鱼眼相机在模仿学习中的特性，重点关注空间定位、场景泛化和硬件泛化三个关键问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鱼眼相机因其超宽视场在机器人操作中得到快速应用，但缺乏对其下游策略学习影响的系统理解。本文旨在填补这一空白，为鱼眼数据集的大规模收集和有效使用提供实证指导。

Method: 通过在仿真和真实世界中进行大量实验，研究腕戴式鱼眼相机在模仿学习中的特性。重点关注三个研究问题：空间定位、场景泛化和硬件泛化。使用随机尺度增强（RSA）策略改善硬件泛化性能。

Result: 研究发现：(1) 宽视场显著增强空间定位，但这一优势高度依赖环境的视觉复杂度；(2) 鱼眼训练的策略在简单场景中容易过拟合，但在足够环境多样性训练下能实现优越的场景泛化；(3) 朴素跨相机迁移会导致失败，根本原因是尺度过拟合，通过RSA策略可改善硬件泛化性能。

Conclusion: 研究结果为机器人学习中鱼眼数据集的大规模收集和有效使用提供了具体可行的指导。鱼眼相机在适当训练条件下能提供显著优势，特别是在空间定位和场景泛化方面，但需要注意视觉复杂度和尺度过拟合问题。

Abstract: The adoption of fisheye cameras in robotic manipulation, driven by their exceptionally wide Field of View (FoV), is rapidly outpacing a systematic understanding of their downstream effects on policy learning. This paper presents the first comprehensive empirical study to bridge this gap, rigorously analyzing the properties of wrist-mounted fisheye cameras for imitation learning. Through extensive experiments in both simulation and the real world, we investigate three critical research questions: spatial localization, scene generalization, and hardware generalization. Our investigation reveals that: (1) The wide FoV significantly enhances spatial localization, but this benefit is critically contingent on the visual complexity of the environment. (2) Fisheye-trained policies, while prone to overfitting in simple scenes, unlock superior scene generalization when trained with sufficient environmental diversity. (3) While naive cross-camera transfer leads to failures, we identify the root cause as scale overfitting and demonstrate that hardware generalization performance can be improved with a simple Random Scale Augmentation (RSA) strategy. Collectively, our findings provide concrete, actionable guidance for the large-scale collection and effective use of fisheye datasets in robotic learning. More results and videos are available on https://robo-fisheye.github.io/

</details>


<div id='cs.SC'></div>

# cs.SC [[Back]](#toc)

### [126] [A Separation Method of the Positivity of A Quartic Polynomial](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00073)
*Taehun Kim,Jung Chan Lee,ByoungSeon Choi*

Main category: cs.SC

TL;DR: 提出基于费拉里解四次方程方法的分离技术，为四次多项式正定性提供新的充要条件，并应用于系数空间投影问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然四次多项式的正定性已有较多研究，但现有条件通常非常复杂，需要更简洁有效的判定方法

Method: 采用基于费拉里解四次方程技术的分离方法，推导四次多项式正定性的充要条件

Result: 获得了四次多项式正定性的新充要条件，并将结果应用于系数空间的投影问题

Conclusion: 通过费拉里技术的分离方法为四次多项式正定性提供了更简洁的判定条件，在系数空间投影等应用中具有实用价值

Abstract: Although the positivity of a quartic polynomial is a well-researched topic, existing conditions are often highly complex. Some necessary and sufficient conditions for the positivity of a quartic polynomial are presented through a separation method based on Ferrari's technique of solving a quartic equation. We apply the result to the problem of the projection of the coefficient space.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [Demand- and Priority-Aware Adaptive Congestion Control for Heterogeneous V2X Service Requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01134)
*Miguel Sepulcre,Javier Tortosa-Garcia,Javier Gozalvez*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出一种需求与优先级感知的自适应拥塞控制技术，用于处理V2X通信中异构且时变的业务需求


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有V2X拥塞控制机制主要关注车辆级别的信道负载稳定性和公平性，通常假设同质业务需求，无法有效处理日益增长的具有不同优先级和传输需求的V2X服务

Method: 提出需求与优先级感知的自适应拥塞控制技术，明确考虑异构和时变的V2X服务需求

Result: 该技术提高了V2X服务需求的满足度，同时保持了稳定的信道运行

Conclusion: 所提技术与当前V2X标准兼容，保持向后兼容性的同时提供了与正在进行的标准化活动一致的增强功能

Abstract: Vehicle-to-Everything (V2X) communications enable the exchange of information among vehicles to improve road safety and traffic efficiency. As V2X deployments progress, vehicles are expected to support an increasing number of V2X services, often characterized by different priorities and data transmission requirements. However, existing V2X congestion control mechanisms primarily focus on maintaining channel load stability and fairness at the vehicle level, typically assuming homogeneous traffic demands. This paper proposes a demand- and priority-aware adaptive congestion control technique that explicitly accounts for heterogeneous and time-varying V2X service requirements. The results demonstrate that the proposed technique improves the satisfaction of V2X service demands while maintaining stable channel operation. The proposed technique aligns with current V2X standards, preserving backward compatibility while providing enhancements consistent with ongoing standardization activities.

</details>


### [128] [Energy Efficient Traffic Scheduling For Optical LEO Satellite Downlinks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01334)
*Ethan Fettes,Pablo G. Madoery,Halim Yanikomeroglu,Gunes Karabulut Kurt,Abhishek Naik,Stéphane Martel*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 针对卫星通信中能量受限问题，提出静态和自适应方案来平衡传输成功率与能量效率，特别关注天气影响下的FSO链路优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着卫星数量激增和星链等巨型星座的出现，卫星通信面临空间-地面链路容量不足的挑战。虽然FSO通信能提供高数据速率，但对天气干扰敏感，导致能量利用效率低下。卫星能量受限的特性使得提高能量效率成为迫切需求。

Method: 开发了静态和自适应方案，包括阈值方案、启发式排序算法和基于强化学习的方案。这些方案旨在平衡传输成功率与能量效率，并在不同数据量和云层分布配置下进行评估，包括使用历史天气数据的案例研究。

Result: 静态方案在动态条件下传输成功率较低，而自适应技术能显著改善性能。但性能提升以增加复杂性和星上计算量为代价。

Conclusion: 自适应方案在动态环境下优于静态方案，但需要在性能提升与计算复杂度之间权衡。对于能量受限的卫星通信系统，特别是使用FSO链路时，开发智能的能量效率优化方案至关重要。

Abstract: In recent years, the number of satellites in orbit has increased rapidly, with megaconstellations like Starlink providing near-global, delay-sensitive communication services. However, not all satellite communication use cases have stringent delay requirements; services such as Earth observation (EO) and remote Internet of Things (IoT) fall into this category. These relaxed delay quality of service (QoS) objectives allow services to be delivered using sparse constellations, enabled by delay-tolerant networking protocols. In the context of rapidly growing data volumes that must be delivered through satellite networks, a key challenge is having sufficient \pgm{space-to-ground link capacity}. This has led to proposals for using free-space optical (FSO) communications, which offer high data rates. However, FSO communications are highly vulnerable to weather-related disruptions. This results in certain communication opportunities being energy inefficient. Given the energy-constrained nature of satellites, developing schemes to improve energy efficiency is highly desirable. In this work, both static and adaptive schemes were developed to balance maintaining the delivery ratio and maximizing energy efficiency. The proposed schemes fall into the following categories: threshold schemes, heuristic sorting algorithms, and reinforcement learning-based schemes. The schemes were evaluated under a variety of different data volumes and cloud cover distribution configurations \pgm{as well as a case study using historical weather data}. It was found that static schemes suffered from low delivery ratio performance under dynamic conditions when compared to adaptive techniques. However, this performance improvement came at the cost of increased complexity and onboard computations.

</details>


<div id='cs.LO'></div>

# cs.LO [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [DRAFT: A Formally Verified Constructive Proof of the Consistency of Peano Arithmetic Using Ordinal Assignments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00487)
*Aaron Bryce,Rajeev Gore'*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 对Gentzen的PA一致性证明进行形式化验证，基于Gödel重构版本，在Coq中完整实现并验证了切割消去论证的良基性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gentzen 1936年的皮亚诺算术一致性证明是数学基础中的重要结果，但原始证明在构造性环境中切割消去论证的良基性需要更严格的验证。该工作旨在通过定理证明器Coq对证明进行完全形式化验证。

Method: 基于Gödel对Gentzen证明的重构版本，进行修改并补充必要细节和修正，特别关注切割消去论证在构造性环境中的良基性证明。所有结果使用Coq定理证明器进行验证。

Result: 成功在Coq中实现了Gentzen一致性证明的完整形式化验证，提供了可验证的代码库，解决了构造性环境中切割消去良基性的证明问题。

Conclusion: 通过形式化验证方法，为Gentzen的经典结果提供了机器可验证的证明，增强了证明的可靠性，并为相关基础数学问题的形式化验证提供了范例。

Abstract: Gentzen's 1936 proof of the consistency of Peano Arithmetic was a significant result in the foundations of
  mathematics. We provide here a modified version of the proof, based on Gödel's reformulation, and including additional
  details and minor corrections which are necessary to definitively prove the well-foundedness of the cut-elimination argument in
  a constructive environment. All results have been verified using the Coq theorem prover.
  NOTE TO READERS 26 February 2026: this is a draft which we had intended to submit to the Journal of Automated Reasoning with no particular time-line in our minds as the work was completed as part of Aaron's honours project at ANU in 2023. For that reason, we have used the Springer style files. We are putting it on arxiv as there appears to be some interest in this work as indicated by a post to
  https://proofassistants.stackexchange.com/questions/6462/how-far-is-gentzens-consistency-proof-of-peano-arithmetic-from-being-formalized
  in early February 2026. The Coq code is available here: https://github.com/aarondroidbryce/Gentzen/tree/master

</details>


### [130] [Quantitative Monitoring of Signal First-Order Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00728)
*Marek Chalupa,Thomas A. Henzinger,N. Ege Saraç,Emily Yu*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 为信号一阶逻辑（SFO）提供首个基于鲁棒性的定量语义，开发在线监控算法，并实现首个公开可用的SFO在线定量监控原型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SFO虽然能表达丰富的实时规范，但当前只有布尔语义且缺乏工具支持，需要开发定量语义和在线监控方法。

Method: 1) 为SFO提供基于鲁棒性的定量语义；2) 识别SFO的过去时间片段；3) 开发将有限响应SFO公式转换为该片段的过去化过程；4) 为过去时间片段设计高效运行时监控算法。

Result: 实现了首个公开可用的SFO在线定量监控原型，在基准测试中展示了方法的实用性和有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作首次为SFO提供了定量语义和在线监控能力，扩展了实时规范表达范围，超越了信号时序逻辑等现有形式化方法。

Abstract: Runtime monitoring checks, during execution, whether a partial signal produced by a hybrid system satisfies its specification. Signal First-Order Logic (SFO) offers expressive real-time specifications over such signals, but currently comes only with Boolean semantics and has no tool support. We provide the first robustness-based quantitative semantics for SFO, enabling the expression and evaluation of rich real-time properties beyond the scope of existing formalisms such as Signal Temporal Logic. To enable online monitoring, we identify a past-time fragment of SFO and give a pastification procedure that transforms bounded-response SFO formulas into equisatisfiable formulas in this fragment. We then develop an efficient runtime monitoring algorithm for this past-time fragment and evaluate its performance on a set of benchmarks, demonstrating the practicality and effectiveness of our approach. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first publicly available prototype for online quantitative monitoring of full SFO.

</details>


### [131] [A Classifying Topos for the Spectrum of Equivalences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01056)
*Kenan Oggad*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文建立了进程代数中线性时间-分支时间谱与拓扑斯理论之间的统一框架，将行为等价性视为局部化，并通过格闭包定理揭示了谱的深层代数结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于统一两种看似不同的计算系统等价性理论：拓扑斯理论通过分类拓扑斯来定义等价性，而进程代数通过行为等价性谱（从迹等价到互模拟）来定义。论文旨在揭示这两种方法实际上是同一结构的两个方面。

Method: 方法包括：为每个标记转移系统构建几何理论𝕋_M及其分类拓扑斯ℰ[𝕋_M]；使用格罗滕迪克拓扑建立行为等价性层次J_bisim ⊊ J_sim ⊊ J_trace；通过能量拓扑扩展到13种命名等价性；利用格闭包定理计算预层Heyting蕴含；在Lean 4/Mathlib中进行形式化验证。

Result: 主要结果：1) 证明了互模拟严格粗于互模拟，互模拟严格粗于拓扑斯等价；2) 菱形Hennessy-Milner逻辑刻画了几何逻辑的互模拟不变片段；3) 发现了30个元素的格结构，包含17个未命名的混合等价性；4) 证明了谱是无限共框架的有限子偏序集，其运算（交、蕴含、减法）在进程代数中无法获得。

Conclusion: 结论表明行为抽象本质上是局部化，线性时间-分支时间谱与拓扑斯理论是同一结构的两个方面。几何闭包定理以构造性方式揭示了谱的深层代数结构，这些结构无法从纯进程代数角度获得，为计算系统等价性研究提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: What makes two computational systems equivalent? Topos theory answers with classifying toposes: a system's semantic content is encoded in the geometric theory it classifies, and two presentations are equivalent when their classifying toposes coincide. Process algebra answers with the linear time-branching time spectrum of van Glabbeek: a hierarchy of behavioral equivalences from trace equivalence to bisimilarity, each determined by which observations can distinguish processes. We show these are aspects of a single structure in which behavioral abstraction is localization.
  Each labeled transition system receives a geometric theory $\mathbb{T}_M$ whose classifying topos $\mathcal{E}[\mathbb{T}_M]$ determines its provable geometric sequents. Mutual simulation is strictly coarser than bisimulation, strictly coarser than topos equivalence; diamond-only Hennessy-Milner logic characterizes the bisimulation-invariant fragment of geometric logic -- a geometric van Benthem theorem. Grothendieck topologies yield $J_{\mathrm{bisim}} \subsetneq J_{\mathrm{sim}} \subsetneq J_{\mathrm{trace}}$, constructive for trace and bisimulation; a counterexample shows the observation-class approach inadequate for simulation, motivating Caramello's duality. Energy-topology extends this to all 13 named equivalences.
  Lattice closure yields 30 elements including 17 unnamed hybrids absent because the energy-game framework computes but does not close. $L_{30}$ is indecomposable with $S \to F = \mathrm{IF}$; a Geometric Closure Theorem computes presheaf Heyting implications at a single free extension. The hierarchy, bi-Heyting structure, and Closure Theorem are proved constructively with no known process-algebraic proof. The spectrum is a finite sub-poset of an infinite coframe whose operations (meets, implications, subtractions) yield structure inaccessible from process algebra. Formalized in Lean 4/Mathlib.

</details>


### [132] [Uniform Agent-interpolation of Distributed Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01146)
*Youan Su*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文通过结合Murai和Sano(2020)的矢列表演算与Bílková(2007)的方法，为带有分布式知识的认知逻辑K、KD和KT建立了均匀插值性质，并提供了纯语法的均匀插项公式构造算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均匀插值性质是Craig插值性质的强化，可理解为命题量词的可定义性。现有研究需要为带有分布式知识的认知逻辑建立均匀插值性质，并提供具体的构造算法。

Method: 结合Murai和Sano(2020)的矢列表演算与Bílková(2007)的方法，为认知逻辑K、KD和KT（带有分布式知识）开发证明系统，提出纯语法的均匀插项公式构造算法。

Result: 成功证明了认知逻辑K、KD和KT（带有分布式知识）具有均匀插值性质，并提供了确定均匀插项公式的纯语法算法。

Conclusion: 本文为带有分布式知识的认知逻辑建立了均匀插值性质，提供了具体的构造算法，扩展了均匀插值在认知逻辑领域的研究，特别考虑了命题变量和智能体符号在插项定义中的双重作用。

Abstract: Uniform interpolation property (UIP) is a strengthening of Craig interpolation property. It can be understood as the definability of propositional quantifiers. This paper develops the sequent calculi provided in Murai and Sano (2020), combining with the methods studied by B{\'ı}lkov{á} (2007) to show the uniform interpolation for epistemic logic $\mathbf{K}$, $\mathbf{KD}$ and $\mathbf{KT}$ with distributed knowledge. A purely syntactic algorithm is presented to determine a uniform interpolant formula. In the definition of an interpolant formula, not only propositional variables but also agent symbols are taken into consideration.

</details>


### [133] [Constructive and Predicative Locale Theory in Univalent Foundations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01308)
*Ayberk Tosun*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 在单型基础中构造性和谓词性地发展locale理论，特别关注谱locale和Stone locale，避免命题重设公理，通过大小假设规避非谓词性依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在单型基础中实现locale理论的构造性和谓词性发展，解决传统形式拓扑方法在谓词性开发中的局限性，直接处理框架并跟踪宇宙层次。

Method: 采用直接处理框架的方法，仔细跟踪宇宙层次，引入大小假设确保谓词性可行性；建立谱locale与分配格的范畴等价，使用补丁locale构造将Stone locale作为谱locale的核心反射子范畴；研究代数DCPO和Scott域的拓扑结构。

Result: 建立了大、局部小和小完备谱locale与分配格之间的范畴等价；证明了Stone locale是谱locale的核心反射子范畴；开发了Scott域的Scott locale并证明其为谱locale；建立了sharp元素与Scott locale补丁点之间的对应关系。

Conclusion: 在单型基础中成功实现了locale理论的谓词性发展，通过自然的大小假设规避了非谓词性依赖，为谱locale和Stone locale理论提供了系统框架，并建立了代数域与拓扑结构之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: We develop locale theory constructively and predicatively in univalent foundations (UF), with a particular focus on the theory of spectral and Stone locales. In the context of UF, predicativity refers specifically to the development of mathematics without the use of propositional resizing axioms. The traditional approach to the predicative development of point-free topology is to work with presentations of locales known as formal topologies. Here, we take a different approach: we work directly with frames, keeping careful track of the universes involved and adopting certain size assumptions to ensure that the theory is amenable to predicative development. Although it initially appears that many fundamental constructions of locale theory rely on impredicativity, we show that these can be circumvented under rather natural size assumptions.
  We first lay the groundwork for the predicative development of locale theory. We then orient our development towards a systematic investigation of the theory of spectral and Stone locales. We establish a categorical equivalence between large, locally small, and small-complete spectral locales and small distributive lattices. Moreover, we exhibit the category of Stone locales as a coreflective subcategory of spectral locales and spectral maps, using the construction known as the patch locale. Finally, we investigate the topology of algebraic DCPOs and Scott domains. We develop the Scott locale of a Scott domain, show that it forms a spectral locale, and then proceed to investigate its patch. Using this, we obtain a topological characterization of de Jong's notion of sharp element: we establish a correspondence between the sharp elements of a Scott domain and the points of the patch of its Scott locale.
  Our development is completely formalized and has been machine-checked using the Agda proof assistant.

</details>


### [134] [On the Metric Nature of (Differential) Logical Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01317)
*Ugo Dal Lago,Naohiko Hoshino,Paolo Pistone*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了微分逻辑关系的度量性质，引入了拟拟度量空间概念，建立了其与微分逻辑关系的范畴对应，并研究了微分预逻辑关系作为定量对应物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微分逻辑关系是衡量高阶程序之间距离的方法，其中函数程序之间的距离本身是函数，将输入误差与输出误差相关联。本文旨在澄清微分逻辑关系的度量性质，为程序距离提供更精细和上下文相关的信息。

Method: 引入拟拟度量空间概念，观察到拟拟度量空间的笛卡尔闭范畴反映了文献中微分逻辑关系的构造。利用笛卡尔闭结构推导出基本引理作为程序距离的组合推理原则。研究类型解释产生的拟拟度量空间，证明它们满足强传递性、不可区分性条件的变体以及弱对称性条件。最后引入微分预逻辑关系作为预逻辑关系框架的定量对应物。

Result: 建立了微分逻辑关系与拟拟度量空间范畴的对应关系，证明了类型解释产生的拟拟度量空间满足特定的度量性质。微分预逻辑关系的偏序集具有最精细的微分预逻辑关系（表现为形式定量等式理论），但缺乏最粗糙的微分预逻辑关系，这与类型λ演算中上下文等价作为最粗糙程序等价的情况形成对比。

Conclusion: 微分逻辑关系具有清晰的度量结构，可通过拟拟度量空间进行形式化。微分预逻辑关系为程序距离提供了定量框架，但其偏序结构缺乏最粗糙元素，表明在定量设置中程序距离的层次结构比经典等价关系更为复杂。

Abstract: Differential logical relations are methods to measure distances between higher-order programs where distances between functional programs are themselves \emph{functions}, relating errors in inputs with errors in outputs. This way, differential logical relations provide a more fine-grained and contextual information of program distances. This paper aims to clarify the metric nature of differential logical relations. We introduce the notion of quasi-quasi-metrics and observe that the cartesian closed category of quasi-quasi-metric spaces reflects the construction of differential logical relations in the literature. The cartesian closed structure induces a fundamental lemma, which can be seen as a compositional reasoning principle for program distances. Furthermore, we investigate the quasi-quasi-metric spaces arising from the interpretation of types, and we prove that they satisfy variants of the strong transitivity condition and indistancy condition, as well as a weak form of the symmetry condition. In the last part of this paper, we introduce a notion of differential prelogical relations arising as a quantitative counterpart of the framework of prelogical relations. Roughly speaking, differential prelogical relations are quasi-quasi-metrics on the collection of programs. The poset of differential prelogical relations has the finest differential prelogical relation presented as a formal quantitative equational theory, while the poset lacks a coarsest differential prelogical relation. The absence of a coarsest differential prelogical relation contrasts with the situations of typed lambda calculi, where the contextual equivalences serve as the coarsest program equivalences.

</details>


### [135] [NM-DEKL$^3_\infty$: A Three-Layer Non-Monotone Evolving Dependent Type Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01366)
*Peng Chen*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 提出了NM-DEKL$^3_\infty$类型系统，用于形式化动态环境中的演化知识，采用三层架构，证明了可靠性和等式完备性，并建立了到μ-演算的嵌入和严格表达能力包含关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要形式化动态环境中不断演化的知识，传统类型系统难以处理非单调的知识变化，因此需要设计新的依赖类型系统来建模这种动态知识演化过程。

Method: 设计了NM-DEKL$^3_\infty$依赖类型系统，采用三层架构：计算层、构造知识层和命题知识层。定义了语法和语义，建立了可靠性定理和等式完备性定理，构造了语法模型并证明其在模型范畴中的初始性，还给出了到μ-演算的嵌入。

Result: 成功建立了NM-DEKL$^3_\infty$类型系统，证明了系统的可靠性和等式完备性，构造了初始语法模型，展示了到μ-演算的嵌入，并证明了严格表达能力包含关系（包括非双模拟不变性质的表达能力）。

Conclusion: NM-DEKL$^3_\infty$是一个有效的形式化工具，能够建模动态环境中的知识演化，其三层架构提供了良好的分离关注点，系统的理论性质完备，表达能力强大，能够表达非双模拟不变的性质。

Abstract: We present a new dependent type system, NM-DEKL$^3_\infty$ (Non-Monotone Dependent Knowledge-Enhanced Logic), for formalising evolving knowledge in dynamic environments. The system uses a three-layer architecture separating a computational layer, a constructive knowledge layer, and a propositional knowledge layer. We define its syntax and semantics and establish Soundness and Equational Completeness; we construct a syntactic model and prove that it is initial in the category of models, from which equational completeness follows. We also give an embedding into the $μ$-calculus and a strict expressiveness inclusion (including the expressibility of non-bisimulation-invariant properties).

</details>


### [136] [Implementing Dependent Type Theory Inhabitation and Unification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01463)
*Chase Norman,Jeremy Avigad*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Canonical-min，一个用于依赖类型理论中可居性和统一性问题的求解器，在Lean中仅用185行代码实现，并提出了DTTBench基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 依赖类型理论是现代证明助手的基础，但可居性和统一性问题都是不可判定的，这些问题对定理证明和程序综合非常有用。需要开发有效的求解器来解决这些核心问题。

Method: 提出了Canonical-min求解器，采用新颖的依赖类型理论实现方法，并使用单子框架将类型检查器转换为高性能求解器。整个实现在Lean中仅用185行代码完成。

Result: 开发了Canonical-min求解器，该求解器对依赖类型理论中的可居性和统一性问题具有完备性和可靠性。同时创建了DTTBench基准测试套件，用于评估依赖类型理论中的类型可居性求解器性能。

Conclusion: Canonical-min展示了在依赖类型理论中实现可居性和统一性求解器的可行性，其简洁的实现证明了现代证明助手框架的有效性。DTTBench为未来研究提供了标准化的评估基准。

Abstract: Dependent type theory is the foundation of many modern proof assistants. Inhabitation and unification are undecidable problems that are useful for theorem proving and program synthesis. We introduce Canonical-min, a sound and complete solver for inhabitation and unification in dependent type theory, implemented in 185 lines of Lean code. This paper describes a novel implementation of dependent type theory and a monadic framework to transform the type checker into a performant solver. Finally, we introduce DTTBench, a benchmark for type inhabitation in dependent type theory.

</details>


### [137] [Generalizing Logic-based Explanations for Machine Learning Classifiers via Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01870)
*Francisco Mateus Rocha Filho,Ajalmar Rêgo da Rocha Neto,Thiago Alves Rocha*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 该论文提出两种新方法改进逻辑解释的覆盖范围：Onestep通过单步调整特征边界消除迭代开销，Twostep采用渐进方法显著提升解释覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习模型解释方法存在正确性无法保证或覆盖范围受限的问题。逻辑方法确保正确性但解释过于受限，而增量扩展方法面临计算成本与覆盖范围的权衡困境。

Method: 提出两种新方法：1) Onestep方法在先前增量扩展工作的基础上，为每个特征的每个边界生成单步解释，消除迭代过程开销；2) Twostep方法采用渐进方法，通过两步策略改进解释覆盖率。

Result: 实验结果显示，Twostep方法相比Onestep显著提高了解释覆盖率，在多个数据集上平均提升高达72.60%，同时也优于先前工作。

Conclusion: Twostep方法通过渐进策略有效解决了逻辑解释方法中覆盖范围与计算效率的权衡问题，为机器学习模型提供了更全面且正确的解释。

Abstract: Machine learning models support decision-making, yet the reasons behind their predictions are opaque. Clear and reliable explanations help users make informed decisions and avoid blindly trusting model outputs. However, many existing explanation methods fail to guarantee correctness. Logic-based approaches ensure correctness but often offer overly constrained explanations, limiting coverage. Recent work addresses this by incrementally expanding explanations while maintaining correctness. This process is performed separately for each feature, adjusting both its upper and lower bounds. However, this approach faces a trade-off: smaller increments incur high computational costs, whereas larger ones may lead to explanations covering fewer instances. To overcome this, we propose two novel methods. Onestep builds upon this prior work, generating explanations in a single step for each feature and each bound, eliminating the overhead of an iterative process. \textit{Twostep} takes a gradual approach, improving coverage. Experimental results show that Twostep significantly increases explanation coverage (by up to 72.60\% on average across datasets) compared to Onestep and, consequently, to prior work.

</details>


### [138] [Empirical Impact of Dimensionality on Random Geometric SAT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01892)
*Flora Rädiker*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 该论文分析了基于几何的SAT实例生成模型，研究了维度对可满足性阈值、求解器时间和不可满足性证明大小的影响，发现低维几何实例易于求解，而高维实例趋近于困难的均匀实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAT问题在理论上是NP完全的，但在实践中SAT求解器却能高效处理工业实例。为了弥合理论与实践的差距，需要研究工业实例的特性并建立能够模拟这些特性的生成模型。现有模型大多无法准确模拟工业实例的特性，因此需要分析有前景的几何模型。

Method: 分析Giráldez-Cru和Levy提出的基于几何的SAT模型，通过实证方法研究几何维度对SAT实例三个关键属性的影响：可满足性阈值的位置、求解器时间以及不可满足性证明的大小。补充理论研究，比较不同维度下的实例行为。

Result: 低维几何实例始终易于求解；随着维度增加，几何模型实例逐渐趋近于困难的均匀实例，表明该模型能够生成从易到难的全范围实例；低维实例的可满足性阈值出现在较低密度处；低维实例在可满足性阈值处没有求解时间硬度峰值，这与均匀实例不同；低维实例的证明大小与求解时间不相关。

Conclusion: 基于几何的SAT模型能够有效模拟工业实例的特性，特别是通过调整维度可以生成从易到难的不同难度实例。该模型为理解SAT实例的可解性提供了新视角，有助于弥合SAT问题理论与实践的差距。

Abstract: The Boolean Satisfiability Problem is perhaps one of the most well-known problems in theoretical computer science. On the one hand, it is proven to be NP-complete, which means that it is generally considered hard to solve. On the other hand, the SAT problem has found many practical applications, which yield so-called industrial instances, and SAT solvers can often efficiently find solutions to such instances.
  Closing this gap between theory and practice is a subject of current research. One approach is to identify properties of SAT instances that make them tractable. To aid in this, models for generating SAT instances have been proposed that mimic the properties of industrial instances. So far, attempts at creating such models were mostly unsuccessful, with instances being either too easy or too hard to solve, or missing important properties of industrial SAT instances.
  In this work, we analyse a promising SAT model introduced by Giráldez-Cru and Levy which is based on an underlying geometry. We empirically analyse the impact of this geometry's dimension on SAT instances with regard to three properties: the location of the satisfiability threshold, solver time, and size of proofs of unsatisfiability. Supplementing theoretical work, we find that low-dimensional geometric instances are throughout very tractable.
