As dimension increases, instances from the geometric model seem to converge to hard uniform instances, which means that the geometric model is capable of representing the full range of easy to hard instances. We also observe that the satisfiability threshold in low-dimensional geometric instances occurs at lower densities. Additionally, low-dimensional instances behave very unlike uniform instances in that they have no hardness peaks in solver time at the satisfiability threshold. This coincides with proof size, which we show to not correlate to solver time at low dimensions.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [139] [Strength Change Explanations in Quantitative Argumentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00008)
*Timotheus Kampik,Xiang Yin,Nico Potyka,Francesca Toni*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该论文提出了强度变化解释的概念，用于使基于论证的推理具有可争议性，通过改变论证图中子集的初始强度来实现期望的最终强度排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了使基于论证的推理具有可争议性，需要解释如何改变才能实现期望的（而非被质疑的）推理结果。现有方法在解释如何通过改变论证强度来达到期望结果方面存在不足。

Method: 引入强度变化解释的概念，用于定量（双极）论证图。该方法描述了对给定图中子集论证的初始强度进行改变，从而基于某些（可能不同的）子集论证的最终强度实现期望的排序。将现有的逆问题和反事实问题简化为强度变化解释，并证明了基本的声音性和完备性属性。

Result: 证明了强度变化解释在某些特殊情况下的存在性和不存在性。通过启发式搜索，在典型应用场景中常见的分层图上通常能成功找到强度变化解释，但在无法保证解释存在（或不存在）的设置中仍存在局限性。

Conclusion: 强度变化解释为定量论证图中的推理争议性提供了系统化的解释框架，能够将现有问题简化为统一的形式，并在实际应用中展示了可行性，但仍需进一步研究以解决存在性保证不足的情况。

Abstract: In order to make argumentation-based inference contestable, it is crucial to explain what changes can achieve a desired (instead of the contested) inference result. To this end, we introduce strength change explanations for quantitative (bipolar) argumentation graphs. Strength change explanations describe changes to the initial strengths of a subset of the arguments in a given graph that can achieve a desired ordering based on the final strengths of some (potentially different) subset of arguments. We show that the existing notions of inverse and counterfactual problems can be reduced to strength change explanations. We also prove basic soundness and completeness properties of our strength change explanations, and demonstrate their existence and non-existence in some special cases. By applying a heuristic search, we demonstrate that we can often successfully find strength change explanations for layered graphs that are common in typical application scenarios; still, limitations remain for settings where we do not provide guarantees for the presence (or absence) of explanations.

</details>


### [140] [Position: AI Agents Are Not (Yet) a Panacea for Social Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00113)
*Yiming Li,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该立场论文认为当前基于LLM的智能体并非社会模拟的万能解决方案，指出角色扮演的合理性不等于真实人类行为有效性，并提出了将AI智能体社会模拟统一表述为环境参与的部分可观察马尔可夫博弈框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对当前社会模拟研究中过度乐观地假设LLM集成智能体在联网多智能体设置中会自动产生真实人口动态的问题，作者旨在揭示当前智能体管道优化验证目标与科学模拟要求之间的系统性不匹配。

Method: 通过批判性分析当前LLM智能体社会模拟的局限性，提出将AI智能体社会模拟统一表述为环境参与的部分可观察马尔可夫博弈，包含明确的暴露和调度机制，使基本假设变得明确且可审计。

Result: 识别出三个关键问题：角色扮演合理性不等于真实人类行为有效性；集体结果常由智能体-环境协同动态而非仅智能体间消息传递中介；结果易受交互协议、调度和初始信息先验主导，特别是在政策导向场景中。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的智能体目前还不是社会模拟的万能解决方案，需要更严谨的框架来明确和审计基本假设，提出了环境参与的部分可观察马尔可夫博弈作为统一表述，并呼吁进一步行动以推进该领域发展。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have spurred growing interest in using LLM-integrated agents for social simulation, often under the implicit assumption that realistic population dynamics will emerge once role-specified agents are placed in a networked multi-agent setting. This position paper argues that LLM-based agents are not (yet) a panacea for social simulation. We attribute this over-optimism to a systematic mismatch between what current agent pipelines are typically optimized and validated to produce and what simulation-as-science requires. Concretely, role-playing plausibility does not imply faithful human behavioral validity; collective outcomes are frequently mediated by agent-environment co-dynamics rather than agent-agent messaging alone; and results can be dominated by interaction protocols, scheduling, and initial information priors, especially in policy-oriented settings. To make these assumptions explicit and auditable, we propose a unified formulation of AI agent-based social simulation as an environment-involved partially observable Markov game with explicit exposure and scheduling mechanisms and call for further actions.

</details>


### [141] [SIGMAS: Second-Order Interaction-based Grouping for Overlapping Multi-Agent Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00120)
*Minah Lee,Saibal Mukhopadhyay*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出SIGMAS框架，通过二阶交互建模从无监督的智能体轨迹中推断重叠多智能体集群的潜在群体结构


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集群系统（如无人机编队、机器人团队）具有复杂的动力学特性，通常由少数大型群体组成且成员关系持久。与行人或交通系统不同，集群需要群体识别来理解细粒度行为。现有方法缺乏从轨迹中无监督推断重叠群体结构的能力。

Method: 提出SIGMAS（基于二阶交互的多智能体集群分组）自监督框架，超越直接成对交互，建模智能体间的二阶交互（即智能体如何相似地与其他智能体交互）。通过可学习的门控机制平衡个体和集体动力学，实现联合推理。

Result: 在多样合成集群场景中的实验表明，SIGMAS能准确恢复潜在群体结构，并在同时重叠的集群动力学下保持鲁棒性，为集群理解建立了新的基准任务和建模框架。

Conclusion: SIGMAS通过二阶交互建模为重叠多智能体集群的群体预测提供了有效的自监督解决方案，为集群系统理解开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Swarming systems, such as drone fleets and robotic teams, exhibit complex dynamics driven by both individual behaviors and emergent group-level interactions. Unlike traditional multi-agent domains such as pedestrian crowds or traffic systems, swarms typically consist of a few large groups with inherent and persistent memberships, making group identification essential for understanding fine-grained behavior. We introduce the novel task of group prediction in overlapping multi-agent swarms, where latent group structures must be inferred directly from agent trajectories without ground-truth supervision. To address this challenge, we propose SIGMAS (Second-order Interaction-based Grouping for Multi-Agent Swarms), a self-supervised framework that goes beyond direct pairwise interactions and model second-order interaction across agents. By capturing how similarly agents interact with others, SIGMAS enables robust group inference and adaptively balances individual and collective dynamics through a learnable gating mechanism for joint reasoning. Experiments across diverse synthetic swarm scenarios demonstrate that SIGMAS accurately recovers latent group structures and remains robust under simultaneously overlapping swarm dynamics, establishing both a new benchmark task and a principled modeling framework for swarm understanding.

</details>


### [142] [Safe Multi-Agent Deep Reinforcement Learning for Privacy-Aware Edge-Device Collaborative DNN Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00129)
*Hong Wang,Xuwei Fan,Zhipeng Cheng,Yachao Yuan,Minghui Min,Minghui Liwang,Xiaoyu Xia*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出HC-MAPPO-L算法，通过分层约束多智能体近端策略优化与拉格朗日松弛，在边缘计算环境中联合优化推理延迟、能耗和隐私成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着DNN推理在边缘和移动平台的普及，隐私保护、资源约束和动态模型部署成为关键挑战。需要一种能够在边缘设备与服务器之间进行自适应模型划分的隐私感知协同推理框架。

Method: 将联合优化问题建模为约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP），提出HC-MAPPO-L算法：1）采用自适应拉格朗日对偶更新强制执行长期延迟约束；2）将CMDP分解为三层分层策略：自回归模型部署策略、拉格朗日增强的用户关联与模型划分策略、基于注意力的资源分配策略。

Result: 实验结果表明，HC-MAPPO-L始终满足严格的延迟约束，同时在能耗和隐私成本之间实现优越平衡，在不同问题规模和资源配置下均优于代表性基线算法。

Conclusion: HC-MAPPO-L框架有效解决了边缘计算中隐私感知协同推理的联合优化问题，通过分层多智能体强化学习方法实现了延迟约束下的性能优化。

Abstract: As Deep Neural Network (DNN) inference becomes increasingly prevalent on edge and mobile platforms, critical challenges emerge in privacy protection, resource constraints, and dynamic model deployment. This paper proposes a privacy-aware collaborative inference framework, in which adaptive model partitioning is performed across edge devices and servers. To jointly optimize inference delay, energy consumption, and privacy cost under dynamic service demands and resource constraints, we formulate the joint problem as a Constrained Markov Decision Process (CMDP) that integrates model deployment, user-server association, model partitioning, and resource allocation. We propose a Hierarchical Constrained Multi-Agent Proximal Policy Optimization with Lagrangian relaxation (HC-MAPPO-L) algorithm, a safe reinforcement learning-based framework that enhances Multi-Agent Proximal Policy Optimization (MAPPO) with adaptive Lagrangian dual updates to enforce long-term delay constraints. To ensure tractability while maintaining coordination, we decompose the CMDP into three hierarchically structured policy layers: an auto-regressive based model deployment policy, a Lagrangian-enhanced user association and model partitioning policy, and an attention-based resource allocation policy. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that HC-MAPPO-L consistently satisfies stringent delay constraints while achieving a superior balance among energy consumption and privacy cost, outperforming representative baseline algorithms across varying problem scales and resource configurations.

</details>


### [143] [Evaluating Theory of Mind and Internal Beliefs in LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00142)
*Adam Kostka,Jarosław A. Chudziak*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种集成心智理论、BDI式内部信念和符号求解器的新型多智能体架构，用于增强LLM多智能体系统中的协作智能，并在资源分配问题中评估了不同LLM下这些认知机制对协作决策的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统在动态世界中的协作智能仍然难以实现，因为LLM在多智能体环境中的表现极不稳定。单纯添加心智理论和内部信念等认知机制并不能自动改善协调效果，这些机制与形式逻辑验证之间的相互作用在不同LLM中尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 提出了一种新型多智能体架构，集成心智理论、BDI式内部信念和符号求解器进行逻辑验证。在资源分配问题中评估该架构，使用不同LLM研究认知机制与系统性能之间的复杂相互作用。

Result: 研究发现LLM能力、认知机制和系统性能之间存在复杂的相互作用关系。不同LLM配置下，集成心智理论、内部信念和符号求解器的架构对协作决策准确性的影响各不相同。

Conclusion: 这项工作通过提出集成心智理论、内部信念和符号求解器的新型多智能体架构，为增强多智能体系统中的协作智能做出了贡献，并评估了不同LLM设置下的性能表现，揭示了认知机制与LLM能力之间的复杂关系。

Abstract: LLM-based MAS are gaining popularity due to their potential for collaborative problem-solving enhanced by advances in natural language comprehension, reasoning, and planning. Research in Theory of Mind (ToM) and Belief-Desire-Intention (BDI) models has the potential to further improve the agent's interaction and decision-making in such systems. However, collaborative intelligence in dynamic worlds remains difficult to accomplish since LLM performance in multi-agent worlds is extremely variable. Simply adding cognitive mechanisms like ToM and internal beliefs does not automatically result in improved coordination. The interplay between these mechanisms, particularly in relation to formal logic verification, remains largely underexplored in different LLMs. This work investigates: How do internal belief mechanisms, including symbolic solvers and Theory of Mind, influence collaborative decision-making in LLM-based multi-agent systems, and how does the interplay of those components influence system accuracy? We introduce a novel multi-agent architecture integrating ToM, BDI-style internal beliefs, and symbolic solvers for logical verification. We evaluate this architecture in a resource allocation problem with various LLMs and find an intricate interaction between LLM capabilities, cognitive mechanisms, and performance. This work contributes to the area of AI by proposing a novel multi-agent system with ToM, internal beliefs, and symbolic solvers for augmenting collaborative intelligence in multi-agent systems and evaluating its performance under different LLM settings.

</details>


### [144] [Silo-Bench: A Scalable Environment for Evaluating Distributed Coordination in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01045)
*Yuzhe Zhang,Feiran Liu,Yi Shan,Xinyi Huang,Xin Yang,Yueqi Zhu,Xuxin Cheng,Cao Liu,Ke Zeng,Terry Jingchen Zhang,Wenyuan Jiang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 多智能体系统中，LLM智能体能够有效交换分布式信息，但无法可靠地整合这些信息进行计算，存在通信-推理鸿沟


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多智能体系统通过分布式信息来克服上下文限制，但智能体是否能可靠地计算分布式信息（而不仅仅是交换信息）仍是一个未解决的问题

Method: 引入Silo-Bench基准测试，包含30个算法任务和三个通信复杂度级别，评估了54种配置的1,620个实验，分析智能体在分布式信息计算中的表现

Result: 发现通信-推理鸿沟：智能体能自发形成适当的协调拓扑并积极交换信息，但系统性地无法将分布式状态合成为正确答案；失败集中在推理整合阶段；协调开销随规模增加而加剧，最终完全抵消并行化收益

Conclusion: 简单地增加智能体数量无法绕过上下文限制；Silo-Bench为追踪真正协作的多智能体系统进展提供了基础

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed in multi-agent systems to overcome context limitations by distributing information across agents. Yet whether agents can reliably compute with distributed information -- rather than merely exchange it -- remains an open question. We introduce Silo-Bench, a role-agnostic benchmark of 30 algorithmic tasks across three communication complexity levels, evaluating 54 configurations over 1,620 experiments. Our experiments expose a fundamental Communication-Reasoning Gap: agents spontaneously form task-appropriate coordination topologies and exchange information actively, yet systematically fail to synthesize distributed state into correct answers. The failure is localized to the reasoning-integration stage -- agents often acquire sufficient information but cannot integrate it. This coordination overhead compounds with scale, eventually eliminating parallelization gains entirely. These findings demonstrate that naively scaling agent count cannot circumvent context limitations, and Silo-Bench provides a foundation for tracking progress toward genuinely collaborative multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [145] [MedCollab: Causal-Driven Multi-Agent Collaboration for Full-Cycle Clinical Diagnosis via IBIS-Structured Argumentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01131)
*Yuqi Zhan,Xinyue Wu,Tianyu Lin,Yutong Bao,Xiaoyu Wang,Weihao Cheng,Huangwei Chen,Feiwei Qin,Zhu Zhu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: MedCollab是一个模拟医院分级会诊流程的多智能体框架，通过动态专家招募、结构化论证协议和疾病因果链构建，显著减少医学幻觉，提升诊断准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在医疗应用中存在诊断幻觉和推理可解释性不足的问题，限制了其在临床实践中的应用。需要开发一种能够模拟真实医院工作流程、提高诊断准确性和透明度的框架。

Method: 1. 多智能体框架模拟医院分级会诊工作流程；2. 动态专家招募机制根据患者症状和检查结果自适应组建临床和检查智能体；3. 采用结构化IBIS论证协议，要求智能体提供基于医学知识和临床数据的可追溯证据；4. 构建分层疾病因果链，将扁平化诊断预测转化为结构化病理进展模型；5. 多轮共识机制通过逻辑审计和加权投票过滤低质量推理。

Result: 在真实世界临床数据集上的评估显示，MedCollab在准确性和RaTEScore方面显著优于纯LLM和现有医疗多智能体系统，医学幻觉明显减少。

Conclusion: MedCollab提供了一种可扩展、透明且符合临床规范的医疗决策方法，通过模拟真实医院工作流程、结构化论证和共识机制，有效解决了LLM在医疗应用中的幻觉和可解释性问题。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise in healthcare applications, however, their use in clinical practice is still limited by diagnostic hallucinations and insufficiently interpretable reasoning. We present MedCollab, a novel multi-agent framework that emulates the hierarchical consultation workflow of modern hospitals to autonomously navigate the full-cycle diagnostic process. The framework incorporates a dynamic specialist recruitment mechanism that adaptively assembles clinical and examination agents according to patient-specific symptoms and examination results. To ensure the rigor of clinical work, we adopt a structured Issue-Based Information System (IBIS) argumentation protocol that requires agents to provide ``Positions'' backed by traceable evidence from medical knowledge and clinical data. Furthermore, the framework constructs a Hierarchical Disease Causal Chain that transforms flattened diagnostic predictions into a structured model of pathological progression through explicit logical operators. A multi-round Consensus Mechanism iteratively filters low-quality reasoning through logic auditing and weighted voting. Evaluated on real-world clinical datasets, MedCollab significantly outperforms pure LLMs and medical multi-agent systems in Accuracy and RaTEScore, demonstrating a marked reduction in medical hallucinations. These findings indicate that MedCollab provides an extensible, transparent, and clinically compliant approach to medical decision-making.

</details>


### [146] [Can AI Agents Agree?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01213)
*Frédéric Berdoz,Leonardo Rugli,Roger Wattenhofer*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: LLM智能体在拜占庭共识游戏中的表现不佳，即使在无利益冲突的良性环境中也难以达成可靠一致，随着群体规模增大和拜占庭节点加入，成功率进一步下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型越来越多地被部署为协作智能体，但它们在对抗性共识环境中的行为尚未得到系统研究。需要评估LLM智能体在拜占庭共识场景下的表现，特别是在无利益冲突的设置中。

Method: 使用同步全连接模拟，在标量值上进行拜占庭共识游戏评估。测试无利益冲突设置下的共识，重点关注一致性而非值的最优性。进行了数百次模拟，涵盖不同模型大小、群体规模和拜占庭节点比例。

Result: 即使在良性设置下，有效一致性也不可靠，且随着群体规模增大而恶化。引入少量拜占庭节点会进一步降低成功率。失败主要由活性丧失主导（如超时和停滞收敛），而非微妙的数值损坏。

Conclusion: 可靠的一致性尚未成为当前LLM智能体群体的可靠涌现能力，即使在无利益冲突的设置中也是如此。这对依赖稳健协调的部署提出了警示。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed as cooperating agents, yet their behavior in adversarial consensus settings has not been systematically studied. We evaluate LLM-based agents on a Byzantine consensus game over scalar values using a synchronous all-to-all simulation. We test consensus in a no-stake setting where agents have no preferences over the final value, so evaluation focuses on agreement rather than value optimality. Across hundreds of simulations spanning model sizes, group sizes, and Byzantine fractions, we find that valid agreement is not reliable even in benign settings and degrades as group size grows. Introducing a small number of Byzantine agents further reduces success. Failures are dominated by loss of liveness, such as timeouts and stalled convergence, rather than subtle value corruption. Overall, the results suggest that reliable agreement is not yet a dependable emergent capability of current LLM-agent groups even in no-stake settings, raising caution for deployments that rely on robust coordination.

</details>


### [147] [Epistemic Gain, Aleatoric Cost: Uncertainty Decomposition in Multi-Agent Debate for Math Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01221)
*Dan Qiao,Binbin Chen,Fengyu Cai,Jianlong Chen,Wenhao Li,Fuxin Jiang,Zuzhi Chen,Hongyuan Zha,Tieying Zhang,Baoxiang Wang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出贝叶斯不确定性分析框架来理解多智能体辩论，将预测不确定性分解为可通过辩论减少的认识不确定性和模型内部噪声引起的偶然不确定性，并基于此设计不确定性引导的多智能体强化学习算法来优化辩论效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体辩论在利用集体智能改善推理和减少幻觉方面显示出潜力，但信息交换如何塑造底层能力仍不清楚。实证中观察到矛盾现象：准确率提升伴随令牌熵大幅增加，同质与异质模型组合间存在显著差异。需要理论框架来理解辩论机制并指导改进。

Method: 提出贝叶斯不确定性分析框架，将总预测不确定性分解为认识不确定性（可通过辩论上下文减少）和偶然不确定性（由内部模型噪声引起）。基于此设计不确定性引导的多智能体强化学习算法，显式优化偶然噪声减少和认识信息利用。

Result: 实验表明，该训练方法显著提高了辩论后准确率和稳定性，并增强了超越单智能体强化学习的个体推理能力。有效辩论的关键在于实现高认识增益同时控制偶然成本。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解和改进多智能体辩论提供了统一的贝叶斯不确定性视角，揭示了辩论有效性的核心机制，并通过不确定性引导的强化学习实现了性能提升。

Abstract: Multi-Agent Debate (MAD) has shown promise in leveraging collective intelligence to improve reasoning and reduce hallucinations, yet it remains unclear how information exchange shapes the underlying ability. Empirically, MAD exhibits paradoxical phenomena, such as accuracy improvement accompanied by substantial increase in token entropy, and remarkable divergence between homogeneous and heterogeneous model combinations. In this paper, we propose a Bayesian uncertainty analysis framework for MAD, which decomposes total predictive uncertainty into epistemic uncertainty reducible by debate context and aleatoric uncertainty induced by internal model noise. Across multiple model configurations, we find that effective debate hinges on achieving high epistemic gain under controlled aleatoric cost. Building on this insight, we design an uncertainty-guided multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) algorithm that explicitly optimizes aleatoric noise reduction and epistemic information utilization. Experiments show that our training significantly improves post-debate accuracy and stability, and enhances individual reasoning beyond single-agent RL, providing a unified Bayesian uncertainty perspective for understanding and improving MAD.

</details>


### [148] [Exploration enhances cooperation in the multi-agent communication system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01401)
*Zhao Song,Chen Shen,Zhen Wang,The Anh Han*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该研究通过整合信号传递与捐赠游戏的两阶段演化博弈模型，发现适度的随机探索能最大化多智能体系统的合作水平，揭示了探索率与系统性能之间的非单调关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论框架通常排除随机探索以保持分析可处理性，导致探索对系统性能的功能性影响未被充分研究。设计增强多智能体系统合作的协议仍是一个重大挑战，而廉价谈话作为解决方案的作用需要更深入理解。

Method: 提出两阶段演化博弈理论模型，整合信号传递与捐赠游戏，在决策过程中明确纳入探索机制。通过基于智能体的模拟在不同拓扑结构上进行实验，分析探索率对系统合作水平的影响。

Result: 发现存在一个普遍的最优探索率能最大化系统范围内的合作水平。适度探索破坏背叛的稳定性，催化自组织合作联盟的形成，促进其周期性成功。合作峰值由振荡周期与放大之间的微妙平衡实现。

Conclusion: 追求确定性刚性不如接受战略探索，工程化的随机性对于维持基于通信的智能系统中的合作和实现最优性能至关重要。探索应被视为一种设计特征而非需要消除的噪声。

Abstract: Designing protocols enhancing cooperation for multi-agent systems remains a grand challenge. Cheap talk, defined as costless, non-binding communication before formal action, serves as a pivotal solution. However, existing theoretical frameworks often exclude random exploration, or noise, for analytical tractability, leaving its functional impact on system performance largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we propose a two-stage evolutionary game-theoretical model, integrating signalling with a donation game, with exploration explicitly incorporated into the decision-making. Our agent-based simulations across topologies reveal a universal optimal exploration rate that maximises system-wide cooperation. Mechanistically, moderate exploration undermines the stability of defection and catalyses the self-organised cooperative alliances, facilitating their cyclic success. Moreover, the cooperation peak is enabled by the delicate balance between oscillation period and amplification. Our findings suggest that rather than pursuing deterministic rigidity, embracing strategic exploration, as a form of engineered randomness, is essential to sustain cooperation and realise optimal performance in communication-based intelligent systems.

</details>


### [149] [Boltzmann-based Exploration for Robust Decentralized Multi-Agent Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02154)
*Nhat Nguyen,Duong Nguyen,Gianluca Rizzo,Hung Nguyen*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: CB-MCTS使用随机玻尔兹曼策略和衰减熵奖励改进Dec-MCTS，解决稀疏/偏斜奖励环境中的多智能体规划问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dec-MCTS在稀疏或偏斜奖励环境中表现不佳，需要更好的探索策略来应对多智能体规划中的欺骗性场景

Method: 用随机玻尔兹曼策略替代确定性UCT，结合衰减熵奖励，实现持续但集中的探索，专门针对多智能体系统设计

Result: 在简单遗憾设置下分析CB-MCTS，仿真显示在欺骗性场景中优于Dec-MCTS，在标准基准测试中保持竞争力

Conclusion: CB-MCTS为多智能体规划提供了鲁棒解决方案，是首个将玻尔兹曼探索应用于多智能体MCTS的方法

Abstract: Decentralized Monte Carlo Tree Search (Dec-MCTS) is widely used for cooperative multi-agent planning but struggles in sparse or skewed reward environments. We introduce Coordinated Boltzmann MCTS (CB-MCTS), which replaces deterministic UCT with a stochastic Boltzmann policy and a decaying entropy bonus for sustained yet focused exploration. While Boltzmann exploration has been studied in single-agent MCTS, applying it in multi-agent systems poses unique challenges. CB-MCTS is the first to address this. We analyze CB-MCTS in the simple-regret setting and show in simulations that it outperforms Dec-MCTS in deceptive scenarios and remains competitive on standard benchmarks, providing a robust solution for multi-agent planning.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [150] [Linting Style and Substance in READMEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00331)
*Hima Mynampaty,Nathania Josephine,Katherine E. Isaacs,Andrew M. McNutt*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: LintMe：一个通过轻量级DSL结合程序化操作和LLM内容评估的README检查工具，可根据不同上下文创建特定检查，提升README质量同时保持作者自主性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: README文件对软件项目的第一印象至关重要，但"好README"的标准因受众和上下文而异（如研究软件需要可复现性细节，开源库可能优先快速入门指南）。现有检查工具难以适应这种多样性。

Method: 开发LintMe设计探针，使用轻量级领域特定语言（DSL）创建上下文特定的检查规则。该DSL创新性地结合程序化操作（如检测损坏链接）和基于LLM的内容评估（如检测专业术语），解决传统检查工具难以处理的问题。

Result: 通过用户研究（N=11）、与朴素LLM使用的比较以及可扩展性案例研究，发现该设计方法易于使用、灵活且很好地匹配该领域需求。用户能够创建适应不同上下文的检查规则。

Conclusion: LintMe为检查更复杂的文档和其他文化中介的文本型文档打开了大门。该工作展示了结合程序化操作和LLM评估的方法在适应多样化文档需求方面的潜力。

Abstract: READMEs shape first impressions of software projects, yet what constitutes a good README varies across audiences and contexts. Research software needs reproducibility details, while open-source libraries might prioritize quick-start guides. Through a design probe, LintMe, we explore how linting can be used to improve READMEs given these diverse contexts, aiding style and content issues while preserving authorial agency. Users create context-specific checks using a lightweight DSL that uses a novel combination of programmatic operations (e.g., for broken links) with LLM-based content evaluation (e.g., for detecting jargon), yielding checks that would be challenging for prior linters. Through a user study (N=11), comparison with naive LLM usage, and an extensibility case study, we find that our design is approachable, flexible, and well matched with the needs of this domain. This work opens the door for linting more complex documentation and other culturally mediated text-based documents.

</details>


### [151] [Privacy Cards for Surfacing Mental Models and Exploring Privacy Concerns: A Case Study of Voice-First Ambient Interfaces with Older Adults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00384)
*Andrea Cuadra,Samar Sabie,Yan Shvartzshnaider,Deborah Estrin*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过隐私卡片访谈法研究老年人使用语音优先环境界面的伦理隐私问题，发现参与者对数据访问权限认知不足，难以区分内置功能与第三方应用，初始担忧较少但深入访谈揭示复杂问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究老年人使用语音优先环境界面（VFAIs）进行居家养老时的伦理和隐私影响，特别关注老年用户对数据隐私的理解和担忧。

Method: 采用深度参与式研究方法，与5位正在成为熟练VFAI用户的老年人合作。使用基于健康数据报告的VFAI设计探针，创建并迭代改进隐私卡片访谈协议。通过参与者先前访谈记录和设备使用日志定制隐私卡片，进行访谈以揭示其心智模型和隐私关切。

Result: 发现参与者缺乏足够的心智模型来做出适当同意决策：不知道谁能访问其数据，难以区分内置功能与第三方应用。初始对VFAI相关伦理问题担忧较少，但隐私卡片访谈揭示了细微问题，包括数据访问控制、功能边界模糊等。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了老年VFAI用户在隐私认知方面的局限性，强调需要改进界面设计和用户教育以支持知情同意。隐私卡片方法有效揭示了表面未表达的深层关切，为未来研究和设计提供了重要启示。

Abstract: We investigate the ethical and privacy implications of voice-first ambient interfaces (VFAIs) for aging in place through an in-depth engagement with five older adults. Our participants were in the process of becoming experienced VFAI users, and had used a VFAI-based design probe for health data reporting. We create and iteratively refine an interview protocol using Privacy Cards. We customize Privacy Cards by drawing on participants' previous interviews and device usage logs. Using Privacy Cards, we conduct interviews to surface their mental models, and explore their privacy concerns. We find insufficient mental models for proper consent. For example, participants did not know who could access their data, and experienced difficulty distinguishing built-in functionality from third-party apps. Participants initially expressed little worry about VFAI-related ethical concerns, but interviews with Privacy Cards revealed nuanced issues, resulting in various implications for future research and design.

</details>


### [152] [Personalities at Play: Probing Alignment in AI Teammates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00429)
*Mohammad Amin Samadi,Nia Nixon*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究评估了LLM作为队友时的人格对齐能力，通过三层框架（自我感知、行为表达、反思表达）发现AI人格可测量但多层次且依赖上下文，记忆构建比对话更能体现人格特质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型越来越多地作为协作者而非工具，需要研究AI队友是否能以可预测的方式表达人格特质，这对团队互动和学习至关重要。

Method: 采用三层评估框架：1) 自我感知（使用BFI-44量表评估四个LLM提供商在32种高低特质配置下的表现）；2) 行为表达（分析AI在真实团队对话中的生成话语）；3) 反思表达（分析结构化长期记忆）。使用LIWC-22分析对话和记忆内容。

Result: LLM能产生显著区分的大五人格特征，但提示语义丰富度影响有限，提供商差异和默认人格特征显著。角色框架很重要：多个模型在没有上下文时拒绝评估，但作为协作队友时配合。对话中的人格信号通常微妙，外向性最易检测；记忆表征放大了特质特定信号，特别是神经质、责任心和宜人性；开放性难以稳定引发。

Conclusion: AI人格可测量但多层次且依赖上下文，评估人格对齐的AI队友需要关注记忆和系统级设计，而不仅仅是对话行为。记忆构建比对话更能有效表达人格特质。

Abstract: Collaborative problem solving and learning are shaped by who or what is on the team. As large language models (LLMs) increasingly function as collaborators rather than tools, a key question is whether AI teammates can be aligned to express personality in predictable ways that matter for interaction and learning. We investigate AI personality alignment through a three-lens evaluation framework spanning self-perception (standardized self-report), behavioral expression (team dialogue), and reflective expression (memory construction). We first administered the Big Five Inventory (BFI-44) to LLM-based teammates across four providers (GPT-4o, Claude-3.7 Sonnet, Gemini-2.5 Pro, Grok-3), 32 high/low trait configurations, and multiple prompting strategies. LLMs produced sharply differentiated Big Five profiles, but prompt semantic richness added little beyond simple trait assignment, while provider differences and baseline "default" personalities were substantial. Role framing also mattered: several models refused the assessment without context, yet complied when framed as a collaborative teammate. We then simulated AI participation in authentic team transcripts using high-trait personas and analyzed both generated utterances and structured long-term memories with LIWC-22. Personality signals in conversation were generally subtle and most detectable for Extraversion, whereas memory representations amplified trait-specific signals, especially for Neuroticism, Conscientiousness, and Agreeableness; Openness remained difficult to elicit robustly. Together, results suggest that AI personality is measurable but multi-layered and context-dependent, and that evaluating personality-aligned AI teammates requires attention to memory and system-level design, not conversation-only behavior.

</details>


### [153] [Texterial: A Text-as-Material Interaction Paradigm for LLM-Mediated Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00452)
*Jocelyn Shen,Nicolai Marquardt,Hugo Romat,Ken Hinckley,Nathalie Riche,Fanny Chevalier*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Texterial将文本重新构想为可生长、雕刻和转化的材料，探索文本作为材料的隐喻如何揭示AI功能、重塑写作过程并创造引人入胜的用户体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成式AI模型虽然支持丰富的文本操作，但僵化的线性界面往往掩盖了这些能力。研究旨在探索文本作为材料的隐喻如何能更好地揭示AI功能，重塑写作过程，并创造更吸引人的用户体验。

Method: 1. 进行形成性研究，了解用户如何通过文本作为材料的隐喻进行推理；2. 提出概念框架，解释材料隐喻如何改变心智模型并弥合LLM中介写作中的设想、执行和评估鸿沟；3. 设计并评估两个技术探针：Text as Clay（通过手势雕刻精炼文本）和Text as Plants（想法随时间偶然生长）。

Result: 形成性研究表明用户能够自然地通过文本作为材料的隐喻进行推理。提出的概念框架解释了材料隐喻如何改变心智模型并弥合LLM中介写作中的鸿沟。两个技术探针（Text as Clay和Text as Plants）展示了文本作为可塑、有生命媒介的具体实现方式。

Conclusion: 通过将文本视为有生命、可塑的媒介，这项工作扩展了写作工具的设计空间，为AI增强的写作体验提供了新的可能性，使文本操作更加直观和富有创造性。

Abstract: What if text could be sculpted and refined like clay -- or cultivated and pruned like a plant? Texterial reimagines text as a material that users can grow, sculpt, and transform. Current generative-AI models enable rich text operations, yet rigid, linear interfaces often mask such capabilities. We explore how the text-as-material metaphor can reveal AI-enabled operations, reshape the writing process, and foster compelling user experiences. A formative study shows that users readily reason with text-as-material, informing a conceptual framework that explains how material metaphors shift mental models and bridge gulfs of envisioning, execution, and evaluation in LLM-mediated writing. We present the design and evaluation of two technical probes: Text as Clay, where users refine text through gestural sculpting, and Text as Plants, where ideas grow serendipitously over time. This work expands the design space of writing tools by treating text as a living, malleable medium.

</details>


### [154] [Opportunities and Challenges of Operating Semi-Autonomous Vehicles: A Layered Vulnerability Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01202)
*Soumita Mukherjee,Priya Kumar,Laura Cabrera*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 应用Luna的分层脆弱性框架分析特斯拉FSD半自动驾驶系统中人类操作员的脆弱性产生机制，揭示心理、操作和社会三个层面的交互作用


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有道路安全模型将脆弱性视为外部道路使用者的固定属性，但新兴证据表明半自动驾驶车辆操作员在监督无法完全控制的自动化系统时，自身也经历动态和情境性的脆弱性

Method: 对17名活跃FSD用户进行半结构化访谈，采用与Luna框架一致的演绎-归纳编码过程分析他们的叙述

Result: 发现操作员脆弱性存在于三个交互层面：心理、操作和社会层面。脆弱性并非来自单一层面，而是这些层面在特定情境中汇聚的结果，产生波动的监督需求和不均衡的风险识别与管理能力

Conclusion: 研究扩展了情境信任校准、自动化自满和有意义人类控制的讨论，表明通常被视为责任的因素（如信任或非正式学习）可根据情境既增加又减轻脆弱性。分析指出需要设计和监管干预措施共同解决心理、操作和社会条件，而非孤立处理，并强调责任如何在支持不足的监督制度中被隐含地转移给个体操作员

Abstract: This study examines how vulnerability is produced for human operators of Tesla's Full Self-Driving (FSD), a Level 2 semi-autonomous vehicle (SAV) system, by applying Florencia Luna's layered vulnerability framework. While existing road safety models conceptualize vulnerability as a fixed attribute of external road users, emerging evidence suggests that semi-autonomous vehicle operators themselves experience dynamic and situational vulnerability as they supervise automated systems that they do not fully control. To investigate this phenomenon, we conducted semi-structured interviews with 17 active FSD users, analyzing their accounts through a combined deductive-inductive coding process aligned with Luna's framework. Findings reveal three interacting layers of operator vulnerability, namely psychological, operational, and social. Vulnerability emerged not from any single layer but from how these layers converged in specific situations, creating fluctuating supervisory demands and uneven capacity to recognize and manage risk. The findings extend debates on contextual trust calibration, automation complacency, and meaningful human control by demonstrating how factors commonly treated as liabilities such as trust or informal learning, can both increase and mitigate vulnerability depending on context. This analysis determines the need for design and regulatory interventions that address psychological, operational, and social conditions together rather than in isolation, and highlights how responsibility is implicitly shifted onto individual operators within inadequately supported supervisory regimes.

</details>


### [155] ["I Need to Find That One Chart": How Data Workers Navigate, Make Sense of, and Communicate Analytical Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00485)
*Ken Gu,Srishti Palani,Vidya Setlur*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了数据工作者如何导航、理解和交流先前的分析对话，开发了补充结构化元素的设计探针，并发现了三种导航策略和两种总结实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话式界面用于数据分析时，会产生长而线性的对话记录，这与现实世界中迭代、非线性的分析过程不匹配，使得回顾和总结对话在不同情境下变得困难。

Method: 开发了一个设计探针，为分析对话补充结构化元素和交互功能（如过滤、多级导航、按需详情），并通过用户研究（n=10）让参与者使用该探针导航和交流过去的分析。

Result: 参与者通过三种导航策略（视觉回忆、顺序和抽象）满足信息需求（回忆、重新定位、优先级排序），并采用两种总结实践（添加过程细节和上下文）。

Conclusion: 研究结果为支持分析对话的重新访问和交流提供了设计启示，强调了超越传统滚动和关键词搜索界面的重要性。

Abstract: Conversational interfaces are increasingly used for data analysis, enabling data workers to express complex analytical intents in natural language. Yet, these interactions unfold as long, linear transcripts that are misaligned with the iterative, nonlinear nature of real-world analyses. Revisiting and summarizing conversations for different contexts is therefore challenging. This paper investigates how data workers navigate, make sense of, and communicate prior analytical conversations. To study behaviors beyond those supported by standard interfaces (i.e., scrolling and keyword search), we develop a design probe that supplements analytical conversations with structured elements and affordances (e.g., filtering, multi-level navigation and detail-on-demand). In a user study (n = 10), participants used the probe to navigate and communicate past analyses, fulfilling information needs (recall, reorient, prioritize) through navigation strategies (visual recall, sequential and abstractive) and summarization practices (adding process details and context). Based on these findings, we discuss design implications to support re-visitation and communication of analytical conversations.

</details>


### [156] [PlantWhisperer: Designing Conversational AI to Support Plant Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00598)
*Daniel Mejer Christensen,Katja Stougård Jørgensen,Josefine Palsgaard Wyrtz,Jennie Torp Overgaard,Niels van Berkel,Joel Wester*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探索AI聊天机器人如何通过植物养护支持来提升人类福祉，开发了让用户通过聊天机器人"对话"植物的移动应用，基于PERMA模型评估显示积极效果但存在用户偏好差异


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机交互研究表明关心他人（包括人类和计算机）对福祉有积极影响，但对话式AI在此类场景中的潜在作用尚不明确，特别是AI聊天机器人如何通过支持植物养护来影响人类福祉

Method: 开发移动应用让用户通过聊天机器人"对话"植物，采用10名参与者的用户评估，基于Seligman的PERMA模型（心理福祉支柱）进行半结构化访谈

Result: 研究发现积极效果，参与者反映与植物建立连接感和相应的成就感；参与者普遍对应用持积极态度，但也提出用户对代表植物的聊天机器人交互存在多样偏好和期望

Conclusion: AI聊天机器人可以通过支持植物养护对福祉产生积极影响，但需要考虑用户对植物-聊天机器人交互的个性化偏好和期望差异

Abstract: Research in Human-Computer Interaction (HCI) has shown that caring for others, including both humans (e.g., close friends) and computers (e.g., Tamagotchi), can have a positive effect on people's wellbeing. However, we know less about the potential role of conversational AI in such settings. In this work, we explore how AI chatbots can support plant care and, in turn, positively influence people's well-being. We developed a mobile application that allows users to `talk' to their plants via chatbots. We evaluated the application with ten participants and conducted semi-structured interviews based on Seligman's PERMA model, which identifies pillars of psychological well-being. Our findings suggest positive effects, with participants reflecting on a sense of connection to their plants and corresponding feelings of accomplishment. While our findings suggest that participants were generally positive about the app, they also raised concerns about the diverse preferences and expectations of users regarding interactions with chatbots representing plants.

</details>


### [157] [Towards Non-Latin Text and Layout Personalization for Enhanced Readability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00688)
*Rina Buoy,Dylan berkamp Fouepe Dongmo,Vesal Khean,Simone Marinai,Koichi Kise*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探索基于词性（POS）的字符和布局个性化对柬埔寨语和日语可读性的影响，发现加粗内容词能显著提升柬埔寨语读者的理解和记忆效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然计算机对字符和布局的识别理解已有深入研究，但人类如何阅读和感知字符布局相对缺乏探索。本研究旨在填补人类-文档交互（HDI）领域的空白，探究字符和布局个性化对可读性的影响。

Method: 采用实证研究方法，对柬埔寨语和日语两种非拉丁、非分词书写系统进行实验。对柬埔寨语读者（43人）使用基于词性的内容词加粗技术，对日语读者（10人）使用句法颜色编码技术，测量阅读理解、记忆、阅读时间、答题时间和感知难度等指标。

Result: 柬埔寨语读者在基于词性的内容词加粗条件下，阅读理解和记忆任务表现显著提升（p值分别为0.03和0.04），且未增加感知难度、认知负荷或阅读努力。日语读者在句法颜色编码下表现出类似趋势，但阅读速度有所下降。

Conclusion: 基于词性的文本样式技术能有效提升非拉丁书写系统的可读性，为实现动态、脚本依赖的字符布局个性化以优化人类可读性迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: Reading has always been an integral part of both professional and personal life. Character and layout recognition and understanding by computers are well-explored areas. Nevertheless, how characters and layout are read and perceived by humans remains relatively underexplored. This work contributes to the field of human-document interaction (HDI) by investigating the effects of character and layout personalization on readability. The paper presents an empirical study on how parts-of-speech (POS)-based character and layout modifications can lead to overall improvements in both reading comprehension and memorization for two non-segmented, non-Latin writing systems: Khmer and Japanese. The experimental results from 43 participants suggest that, by bolding POS-derived content words, Khmer readers perform better on both reading comprehension and memorisation tasks, with a significant effect (p-values of 0.03 and 0.04, respectively). A similar overall tendency is also observed in a pilot study among Japanese readers (10 participants) using syntactic color-coding. In addition, the analyses of reading time, answering time, and perceived difficulty reveal that the proposed text styling technique does not increase any perceived difficulty, cognitive load, or reading effort for the Khmer readers. However, the Japanese readers experienced a decrease in reading speed. This study and its findings represent a significant step towards enabling dynamic, script-dependent personalization of character and layout to optimize human readability.

</details>


### [158] [Structure Matters: Evaluating Multi-Agents Orchestration in Generative Therapeutic Chatbots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00774)
*Sina Elahimanesh,Mohammadali Mohammadkhani,Sara Zahedi Movahed,Mohammadmahdi Abootorabi,Shayan Salehi,Abbas Edalat*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 多智能体系统在心理治疗对话中比单智能体和无引导LLM表现更自然、更人性化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型擅长开放式对话，但心理治疗需要结构化进展和遵循临床协议，这使得心理治疗师聊天机器人的设计具有挑战性。研究旨在探索不同LLM设计如何影响基于自我依恋技术的心理治疗对话的感知效果。

Method: 基于自我依恋技术（SAT）开发心理治疗聊天机器人，比较三种架构变体：1）多智能体系统（使用有限状态机对齐治疗阶段和共享长期记忆）；2）单智能体（使用相同知识库和提示）；3）无引导LLM。在为期8天的随机对照试验中，66名波斯语参与者被平衡分配到三个聊天机器人组进行评估。

Result: 多智能体系统在感知自然度和人性化方面显著优于其他变体，在大多数其他指标上也获得更高评分。这表明对于治疗性AI，架构编排与提示工程同样重要。

Conclusion: 在心理治疗对话中，架构编排与提示工程同样重要，多智能体系统能够更好地促进自然、引人入胜的治疗对话。

Abstract: While large language models (LLMs) excel at open-ended dialogue, effective psychotherapy requires structured progression and adherence to clinical protocols, making the design of psychotherapist chatbots challenging. We investigate how different LLM-based designs shape perceived therapeutic dialogue in a chatbot grounded in the Self-Attachment Technique (SAT), a novel self-administered psychotherapy rooted in attachment theory. We compare three architectural variants: (1) a multi-agent system utilizing finite state machine aligned with therapeutic stages and a shared long-term memory, (2) a single-agent using identical knowledge-base and the same prompts, and (3) an unguided LLM. In an eight-day randomized controlled trial (RCT) with N=66 Farsi-speaking participants, balanced across the three chatbots, the multi-agent system is perceived as significantly more natural and human-like than the other variants and achieves higher ratings across most other metrics. These findings demonstrate that for therapeutic AI, architectural orchestration is as critical as prompt engineering in fostering natural, engaging dialogue.

</details>


### [159] [Constructing Everyday Well-Being: Insights from God-Saeng for Personal Informatics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00847)
*Inhwa Song,Kwangyoung Lee,Janghee Cho,Amon Rapp,Hwajung Hong*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文探讨韩国"God-Saeng"现象如何通过规划、问责和数据化实践塑造日常幸福感，揭示个人信息学系统应超越追踪功能，帮助用户协商文化张力并创造意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统个人信息学(PI)系统主要支持行为改变，但日常幸福感不仅涉及达成目标行为，还受到赋予行动意义的文化叙事影响。研究旨在探索韩国God-Saeng现象如何体现年轻人在日常生活中的幸福感协商过程。

Method: 采用10天探索性研究(N=24)，通过小型任务(bit-sized missions)考察韩国年轻人如何参与God-Saeng实践，分析其规划实践、问责基础设施和数据化过程。

Result: 参与者依赖规划、问责和数据化来稳定自我，但这些常规也加剧了自我监控和表现压力。他们在一致性vs灵活性、真实性vs可见性、生产力vs关系等更广泛价值观之间导航，并通过社会文化背景重新诠释日常活动。

Conclusion: 个人信息学系统应超越追踪功能，发展为数字工具，帮助用户协商张力、创造意义，并反思技术如何参与其文化和存在主义情境下的幸福感构建。

Abstract: While Personal Informatics (PI) systems support behavior change, everyday well-being involves more than achieving individual target behaviors. It is shaped by cultural narratives that give actions meaning. In South Korea, the God-Saeng phenomenon, encompassing disciplined, collective, and publicly documented self-improvement practices, offers a lens into how well-being is negotiated in daily life. We conducted a 10-day probe (N=24) with bite-sized missions to examine how young adults engaged in God-Saeng. Participants relied on planning practices, accountability infrastructures, and datafication to stabilize themselves, yet these same routines also intensified pressures toward self-monitoring and performance. They navigated tensions between consistency and flexibility, authenticity and visibility, and productivity and broader values such as relationships, and reinterpreted ordinary activities through sociocultural contexts. These insights suggest design opportunities for PI systems that move beyond tracking, toward digital instruments that help users negotiate tensions, make meaning, and reflexively understand how technologies participate in their culturally and existentially situated well-being.

</details>


### [160] [From OCR to Analysis: Tracking Correction Provenance in Digital Humanities Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00884)
*Haoze Guo,Ziqi Wei*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出一个面向数字人文OCR校正文本的溯源感知框架，记录字符级校正谱系，并验证溯源信息对下游命名实体提取的影响


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OCR是数字人文文本处理流程中关键但易错的环节，现有校正工作流常覆盖中间决策，掩盖文本转换如何影响学术解释

Method: 开发溯源感知框架，在字符级别记录校正谱系（编辑类型、校正来源、置信度、修订状态）；使用历史文本试点语料，比较原始OCR、完全校正文本和溯源过滤校正在下游命名实体提取中的差异

Result: 校正路径显著影响提取的实体和文档级解释；溯源信号有助于识别不稳定输出并优先安排人工审查

Conclusion: 溯源应作为数字人文NLP中的首要分析层，支持可重复性、来源批评和不确定性感知的解释

Abstract: Optical Character Recognition (OCR) is a critical but error-prone stage in digital humanities text pipelines. While OCR correction improves usability for downstream NLP tasks, common workflows often overwrite intermediate decisions, obscuring how textual transformations affect scholarly interpretation. We present a provenance-aware framework for OCR-corrected humanities corpora that records correction lineage at the span level, including edit type, correction source, confidence, and revision status. Using a pilot corpus of historical texts, we compare downstream named entity extraction across raw OCR, fully corrected text, and provenance-filtered corrections. Our results show that correction pathways can substantially alter extracted entities and document-level interpretations, while provenance signals help identify unstable outputs and prioritize human review. We argue that provenance should be treated as a first-class analytical layer in NLP for digital humanities, supporting reproducibility, source criticism, and uncertainty-aware interpretation.

</details>


### [161] [VizQStudio: Iterative Visualization Literacy MCQs Design with Simulated Students](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00994)
*Zixin Chen,Yuhang Zeng,Sicheng Song,Yanna Lin,Xian Xu,Huamin Qu,Meng Xia*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: VizQStudio：一个基于MLLM模拟学生的可视化分析系统，支持教师迭代设计和优化可视化素养多选题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计高质量的可视化素养多选题面临多重挑战：需要协调多模态元素（图表、题干、干扰项），应对多样化的可视化任务，适应不同背景的学习者。现有评估工具主要依赖标准化的固定题库，缺乏支持迭代设计的适应性。

Method: 开发VizQStudio可视化分析系统，该系统利用MLLM驱动的模拟学生。教师可以指定多样化的学生档案（人口统计、知识水平、学习特征），系统可视化展示模拟学生对不同问题组件的推理和响应过程。

Result: 通过混合方法评估（专家访谈、案例研究、课堂部署、大规模在线研究），验证了系统在帮助教师探索潜在误解、难度校准和设计权衡方面的价值，同时揭示了在现实教学环境中的局限性。

Conclusion: 本研究将MLLM学生模拟重新定位为设计阶段的探索性辅助工具，通过在实际教学环境中检验其价值和局限，为未来系统如何支持以教师为中心、迭代且负责任地使用AI进行多模态评估设计提供了设计洞见。

Abstract: Multiple-choice questions (MCQs) are a widely used educational tool, particularly in domains such as visualization literacy that require broad conceptual coverage and support diverse real-world applications. However, designing high-quality visualization literacy MCQs remains challenging, as instructors must coordinate multimodal elements (e.g., charts, question stems, and distractors), address diverse visualization tasks, and accommodate learners with heterogeneous backgrounds. Existing visualization literacy assessments primarily rely on standardized, fixed item banks, offering limited support for iterative question design that adapts to differences in learners' abilities, backgrounds, and reasoning strategies. To address these challenges, we present VizQStudio, a visual analytics system that supports instructors in iteratively designing and refining visualization literacy MCQs using MLLM-powered simulated students. Instructors can specify diverse student profiles spanning demographics, knowledge levels, and learning-related traits. The system then visualizes how simulated students reason about and respond to different question components, helping instructors explore potential misconceptions, difficulty calibration, and design trade-offs prior to classroom deployment. We investigate VizQStudio through a mixed-method evaluation, including expert interviews, case studies, a classroom deployment, and a large-scale online study. Overall, this work reframes MLLM-based student simulation in assessment authoring as a design-time, exploratory aid. By examining both its value and limitations in realistic instructional settings, we surface design insights that inform how future systems can support instructor-centered, iterative, and responsible uses of AI for multimodal assessment design in visualization literacy and related domains.

</details>


### [162] [Sustainable Care: Designing Technologies That Support Children's Long-Term Engagement with Social Issues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00996)
*JaeWon Kim,Aayushi Dangol,Rotem Landesman,Alexis Hiniker,McKenna F. Parnes*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出"可持续关怀"设计视角，探讨如何通过技术设计支持儿童持续参与社会议题而不导致共情疲劳


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前儿童通过数字技术接触社会议题（气候变化、冲突、不平等），但现有设计多通过恐惧和紧迫感吸引注意力，缺乏有意义的行动路径，导致儿童焦虑、退缩和参与度下降

Method: 举办全天研讨会，引入"可持续关怀"设计视角，邀请儿童-计算机交互、游戏、教育和青少年心理健康领域的研究者和从业者共同探讨

Result: 研讨会旨在共同绘制该领域的研究图景，并为CCI社区制定研究议程

Conclusion: 需要新的技术设计方法支持儿童持续参与社会议题，避免共情痛苦和倦怠，促进持久的公民参与

Abstract: Children today encounter social issues -- climate change, conflict, inequality -- through digital technologies, and the design of that encounter shapes whether young people move toward lasting civic engagement or toward anxiety and withdrawal. Much of the content children see is optimized for attention through fear and urgency, with few pathways toward meaningful action -- contributing to rising distress and disengagement among young people who care deeply but feel powerless to act. This full-day workshop introduces ``sustainable care'' as a design lens, asking how technology might support children's sustained engagement with social causes without contributing to empathic distress or burnout. We invite researchers and practitioners across child-computer interaction, games, education, and youth mental health to map this landscape together and develop a research agenda for the CCI community.

</details>


### [163] [Remember You: Understanding How Users Use Deadbots to Reconstruct Memories of the Deceased](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01017)
*Yifan Li,Xingyu Lan*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究通过访谈26名用户，探讨了哀悼者如何通过"数字逝者"AI互动主动构建和重塑对逝者的记忆，揭示了从强化理想化到AI生成记忆与真实记忆模糊融合的动态过程，并提出了相关的伦理关切。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注"数字逝者"的伦理问题，但对哀悼者如何通过此类互动构建和重塑记忆了解不足。本研究旨在填补这一空白，探索用户如何在与逝者AI模拟的互动中主动建构记忆。

Method: 采用深度访谈法，对26名使用"数字逝者"的用户进行定性研究，分析他们在互动过程中的记忆建构行为和心理机制。

Result: 研究发现用户并非被动接受者，而是通过选择性输入、持续互动调整和想象性认知补充，构建融合真实记忆与个人期望的理想化数字形象。"数字逝者"提供了无社会压力的私人哀悼空间和解决未竟情感渠道。用户记忆呈现动态演变：从初期强化和理想化，到后期AI生成的新记忆与真实回忆模糊融合。

Conclusion: "数字逝者"互动中的记忆模糊融合现象引发了关于记忆扭曲和依赖性的伦理关切，突显了未来临床研究需要关注AI介导哀悼的长期影响。

Abstract: Generative AI has enabled ``Deadbots'', offering mourners an interactive way to engage with simulations of the deceased. While existing research often emphasizes ethics, less is known about how bereaved individuals construct and reshape memory through such interactions. To address this gap, this study draws on in-depth interviews with 26 users. Findings reveal that users are not passive recipients but active constructors of the deceased's digital representation. Through selective input, ongoing interactive adjustments and imaginative cognitive supplementation, they build an idealized digital figure blending authentic memories with personal expectations. Deadbots provide a private space to grieve without social pressure and a channel to address unresolved emotions. In this process, users' memory of the deceased evolves dynamically: from initial reinforcement and idealization to a later stage where AI-generated new memories blur with authentic recollections, reflecting a complex desire for connection through an artificial medium. This blurring raises ethical concerns regarding memory distortion and dependency, underscoring the need for future clinical research on the long-term impact of AI-mediated grieving.

</details>


### [164] [AEDHunter: Investigating AED Retrieval in the Real World via Gamified Mobile Interaction and Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01075)
*Helinyi Peng,Akihito Taya,Yuuki Nishiyama,Kaoru Sezaki*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 开发了AEDHunter游戏化移动应用，通过重复实地训练提高公众寻找自动体外除颤器的能力，减少检索时间并增强信心


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 院外心脏骤停时早期除颤能显著提高生存率，但公众对AED位置了解有限，现有静态2D地图解决方案在紧急或复杂现实场景中效果不佳

Method: 开发AEDHunter游戏化、基于位置的移动应用，利用智能手机传感器分析参与者移动和学习模式，使用低成本蓝牙标签验证到达AED位置，采用双状态活动检测器识别"探索性停顿"作为行为学习信号

Result: 在真实世界评估研究中，参与者经过重复练习后显著减少了AED检索时间，报告了更强的定位AED信心，探索性停顿分析显示犹豫逐渐减少

Conclusion: 像AEDHunter这样的游戏化应用可以通过重复实地训练提高AED检索性能，增强自我报告的准备度，为技术支持的学习和公共安全应用提供设计见解

Abstract: Early defibrillation significantly improves survival rates in cases of out-of-hospital cardiac arrest. However, limited public awareness of Automated External Defibrillator (AED) locations constrains their effective use. Existing solutions, such as static 2D maps, often fall short in urgent or complex real-world scenarios. To address this challenge, we developed AEDHunter, a gamified, location-based mobile application designed to transform AED retrieval into an engaging and repeatable practice experience. Leveraging smartphone sensors to analyze participants' movement and learning patterns, and using low-cost Bluetooth tags to verify arrivals at AED locations, AEDHunter guides users through multiple sessions of AED discovery. In a real-world evaluation study, participants significantly reduced their AED retrieval times after repeated practice sessions and reported increased confidence in locating AEDs. Additionally, we employ a two-state activity detector to identify ``exploratory pauses'', which are then used as a behavioral learning signal to quantify hesitation and its progressive reduction through practice. Our findings suggest that gamified applications like AEDHunter can improve AED retrieval performance through repeated, in-situ training and enhance self-reported preparedness, offering design insights for technology-supported learning and public safety applications.

</details>


### [165] [Take the Power Back: Screen-Based Personal Moderation Against Hate Speech on Instagram](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01187)
*Anna Ricarda Luther,Hendrik Heuer,Stephanie Geise,Sebastian Haunss,Andreas Breiter*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究通过德尔菲法调查了40名遭受仇恨言论的活跃用户，发现他们优先希望在对话和算法推荐界面使用个性化内容审核工具，重视跨界面可撤销和可监督的功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体平台的内容审核常常无法有效保护目标用户，而个性化审核工具可以让用户自行决定如何过滤内容。但目前尚不清楚用户希望在哪些界面（如评论、Reels标签页或首页动态）使用个性化审核，以及他们最看重哪些功能特性。

Method: 采用三波德尔菲研究法，招募40名经历过仇恨言论的活跃用户参与。结合定量评分、排序和开放式问题，收集用户对所需功能特性的意见。

Result: 参与者优先希望在对话界面和算法推荐界面使用个性化内容审核。他们重视跨界面可撤销和可监督的功能特性，而基于输入、特定内容类型和高度自动化的功能则更具界面特异性。

Conclusion: 研究强调了个性化内容审核的重要性，并为Instagram的个性化审核工具提供了以用户为中心的设计建议。

Abstract: Hate speech remains a pressing challenge on social media, where platform moderation often fails to protect targeted users. Personal moderation tools that let users decide how content is filtered can address some of these shortcomings. However, it remains an open question on which screens (e.g., the comments, the reels tab, or the home feed) users want personal moderation and which features they value most. To address these gaps, we conducted a three-wave Delphi study with 40 activists who experienced hate speech. We combined quantitative ratings and rankings with open questions about required features. Participants prioritized personal moderation for conversational and algorithmically curated screens. They valued features allowing for reversibility and oversight across screens, while input-based, content-type specific, and highly automated features are more screen specific. We discuss the importance of personal moderation and offer user-centered design recommendations for personal moderation on Instagram.

</details>


### [166] [Proscenium: Exploring Design Spaces of Layered Information Experience on a Large Dual-Layer Transparent Display](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01238)
*Chen Chen,Michel Pahud,David Brown,Chuck Needham,Balasaravanan T. Kumaravel,Andrew D. Wilson,Ken Hinckley,Nicolai Marquardt*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Proscenium是一个可调节层间距的双层大型透明显示工作空间，旨在探索分层信息空间的设计可能性，通过14个推测性体验原型展示了跨显示层的信息过渡与链接技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层信息空间是设计直观且引人入胜交互体验的有前景策略，但多层显示虽然通过层间轻微分离实现有限深度感知，如何充分利用分层信息的独特可供性来设计完整体验仍不明确。

Method: 引入Proscenium系统：一个具有可调节层间距的双层大型透明显示工作空间。构建初步设计空间，重点关注渲染信息如何在显示层之间过渡和链接，并开发了涵盖六个类别的14个推测性体验原型。

Result: 展示了Proscenium系统的实现和14个体验原型，这些原型分布在六个类别中，演示了分层信息空间在跨层信息过渡和链接方面的设计可能性。

Conclusion: Proscenium系统为探索分层信息空间的设计提供了实验平台，通过可调节层间距和跨层信息交互技术，为未来分层显示体验的设计开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Layering information spaces is a promising strategy to design intuitive and engaging interactive experiences. Although multi-layer displays enable promising interaction techniques through limited depth perception - achieved via slight separation between layers - it remains unclear how to fully design experiences that leverage the unique affordances of layered information. To address this, we introduce Proscenium, a dual-layer, large transparent display workspace setup with an adjustable separation between the layers. We demonstrate our preliminary design space focusing on how rendered information can be transitioned and linked across displays, and showcase 14 speculative experience prototypes across six categories.

</details>


### [167] [Actor's Note: Examining the Role of AI-Generated Questions in Character Journaling for Actor Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01314)
*Sora Kang,Jaemin Zoh,Hyoju Kim,Hyeonseo Park,Hajin Lim,Joonhwan Lee*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出Actor's Note工具，将大语言模型重新定位为启发式伙伴而非合著者，通过情境感知提问引导演员反思，解决角色日志记录中的认知负担、空白页问题和短期回报不明确等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 角色日志记录是演员训练中的成熟练习，但许多演员因认知负担、空白页问题和短期回报不明确而难以持续。现有方法未能有效支持演员的反思实践，需要设计能降低进入门槛、支持持续反思并保持演员自主性的工具。

Method: 基于将大语言模型重新定位为启发式伙伴而非合著者的视角，设计了Actor's Note日志工具。该工具根据剧本、角色和排练阶段定制问题，同时保持演员的自主性。通过14天交叉研究，对29名演员使用调查、日志和访谈进行评估。

Result: 结果表明，该工具降低了进入门槛，支持了持续反思，并丰富了角色探索。参与者描述了AI在不同排练阶段（早期vs后期）引入时的不同益处。工具成功维持了反思实践，同时保持了艺术沉浸感。

Conclusion: 这项工作为创造力支持工具提供了实证见解和设计原则，能够在表演训练中维持反思实践，同时保持艺术沉浸感。重新定位AI为启发式伙伴而非合著者的方法被证明有效，为类似工具的设计提供了参考框架。

Abstract: Character journaling is a well-established exercise in actor training, but many actors struggle to sustain it due to cognitive burden, the blank page problem, and unclear short-term rewards. We reframe large language models not as co-authors but as maieutic partners-tools that guide reflection through context-aware questioning rather than producing text on behalf of the user. Based on this perspective, we designed Actor's Note, a journaling tool that tailors questions to the script, role, and rehearsal phase while preserving actor agency. We evaluated the system in a 14-day crossover study with 29 actors using surveys, logs, and interviews. Results indicate that the tool reduced entry barriers, supported sustained reflection, and enriched character exploration, with participants describing different benefits when AI was introduced at earlier versus later rehearsal stages. This work contributes empirical insights and design principles for creativity-support tools that sustain reflective practices while preserving artistic immersion in performance training.

</details>


### [168] [Caught in a Mafia Romance: How Users Explore Intimate Roleplay and Narrative Exploration with Chatbots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01319)
*Julia Kieserman,Cat Mai,Sara Lignell,Lucy Qin,Athanasios Andreou,Damon McCoy,Rosanna Bellini*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 用户通过Character.AI平台与年轻成年、男性化角色进行亲密角色扮演，偏好权力不对等场景和奇幻设定，同时关注性内容过度或不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有研究关注人类可能对聊天机器人产生浪漫依恋，但人们对AI聊天机器人互动类型的期望仍不明确，尤其是在快速发展的聊天机器人生态中。本研究旨在通过实证分析Character.AI平台及其用户社区，揭示用户实际期望的AI互动模式。

Method: 采用混合研究方法：1) 对Character.AI平台进行实证研究，该平台允许用户设计和分享角色型聊天机器人；2) 结合对Character.AI用户在Reddit上发帖的内容分析。

Result: 研究发现与流行叙事相反，用户主要期望：1) 与年轻成年、男性化角色进行亲密角色扮演，用户在明确定义的场景中处于权力劣势地位；2) 沉浸在无边界的奇幻设定中。同时发现用户既关注性内容过度的问题，也关注性内容不足的问题，这需要新的数字安全功能。

Conclusion: 用户对AI聊天机器人的互动期望比传统认知更复杂，涉及权力动态、奇幻沉浸和性内容平衡等多维度。研究结果揭示了需要开发新型数字安全功能来应对用户对性内容过度和不足的双重担忧，为聊天机器人设计提供了重要启示。

Abstract: AI chatbots, built using large language models, are increasingly integrated into society and mimic the patterns of human text exchanges. While previous research has raised concerns that humans may form romantic attachment to chatbots, the range of AI-mediated interactions that people wish to create for themselves or others with chatbots remains poorly understood, particularly given the fast evolving landscape of chatbots. We provide an empirical study of Character.AI (cAI), a popular chatbot platform that enables users to design and share character-based bots, and synthesize this with an analysis of Reddit posts from cAI users. Contrary to popular narratives, we identify that users want to: (1) engage in intimate role-play with young adult, masculine-presenting characters that place users in a position of inferior power in well-defined scenarios and (2) immerse themselves in boundless, fantasy settings. We further find that users problematize both the excessive and insufficient sexualized content in such interactions which warrants novel digital-safety features.

</details>


### [169] [From Sustainable Materials to User-Centered Sustainability: Material Experience in Art Healing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01377)
*Yuxin Zhang,Fan Zhang,Zihao Song,Chao Zhao*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究开发基于水凝胶的可持续材料，探索从材料可持续性到用户中心可持续性的转变，重点关注通过材料体验实现艺术疗愈效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可持续材料研究多关注环境性能，本研究旨在将可持续性扩展到用户心理层面，通过材料体验实现艺术疗愈，满足用户情感需求。

Method: 以水凝胶为基质开发可持续材料，通过视觉、触觉、嗅觉等多模态材料体验实验，分析美学、内在和物理属性对艺术疗愈的影响。

Result: 在多模态材料体验中，"美学"属性对艺术疗愈影响最大，其次是"内在"属性，"物理"属性影响相对有限；研究提出了系统的材料体验框架。

Conclusion: 材料设计应超越物理性能，重视用户心理感知和情感需求；提出的材料体验框架为设计师提供了系统理解材料特性的工具，促进用户中心的可持续设计。

Abstract: This study develops sustainable materials using hydrogel as the matrix and explores the transition from sustainable materials to user-centered sustainability, with a particular focus on achieving art healing through material experience. The findings reveal that "Aesthetic" property exert the greatest influence on art healing in the context of multimodal material experiences involving visual, tactile, and smell, followed by "Intrinsic" property, whereas "Physical" property have a comparatively limited effect. Furthermore, the study proposes a material experience framework that enables designers to systematically and holistically understanding material characteristics. It highlights the importance of considering users' psychological perceptions and emotional needs in the material design process.

</details>


### [170] [When Humans Don't Feel Like an Option: Contextual Factors That Shape When Older Adults Turn to Conversational AI for Emotional Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01413)
*Mengqi Shi,Tianqi Song,Zicheng Zhu,Yi-Chieh Lee*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探讨老年人何时及为何选择AI而非亲密他人进行情感表达，识别了影响这一决策的三个情境因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有研究关注老年人对AI陪伴的一般态度，但缺乏对特定时刻决策机制的了解。本研究旨在填补这一空白，探究老年人日常生活中的情境因素如何影响他们选择AI而非亲密他人进行情感支持。

Method: 采用质性研究方法，通过对18位老年人进行访谈，分析他们在日常生活中选择AI进行情感表达的具体情境和决策过程。

Result: 识别出三个关键情境因素：1)人类联系的时间不可用性；2)关系考量（负担和评价担忧）；3)自我呈现关注（尊严和面子维护）。这些因素反映了老年人对独立性、尊严和积极自我呈现的年龄相关需求。

Conclusion: 研究将关注点从AI使用的一般模式转向情感支持行为出现的具体情境，为情境敏感的责任AI设计提供了实证基础，并为晚年情感支持寻求的后续研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Older adults are increasingly turning to conversational AI for emotional expression. While prior research has examined general attitudes toward AI companionship, little is known about the specific moments when and why older adults choose AI over close others for emotional support. This study addresses this gap by examining the moment-level conditions that shape these decisions in everyday life. Drawing on interviews with 18 older adults, we identify three contextual factors: temporal unavailability of human contacts, relational considerations around burden and evaluation, and self-presentation concerns tied to dignity and face-saving. Our findings reveal how age-related needs for independence, dignity, and valued self-presentation shape these everyday decisions. This work shifts attention from general patterns of AI use to the moment-level circumstances in which emotionally supportive engagement emerges. By foregrounding these situated dynamics, we provide an empirical foundation for context-sensitive responsible AI design and future research on emotional support-seeking in later life.

</details>


### [171] [Power Echoes: Investigating Moderation Biases in Online Power-Asymmetric Conflicts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01457)
*Yaqiong Li,Peng Zhang,Peixu Hou,Kainan Tu,Guangping Zhang,Shan Qu,Wenshi Chen,Yan Chen,Ning Gu,Tun Lu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究在线权力不对称冲突中的人类审核偏见，以及AI建议对这些偏见的影响


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线权力不对称冲突普遍存在，目前主要依赖人工审核。先前研究关注不同场景下的人工审核偏见，但权力不对称冲突中的审核偏见尚未探索

Method: 采用混合设计实验，50名参与者，以消费者与商家之间的真实冲突为场景，研究权力相关偏见类型及AI建议的影响

Result: 发现两种审核模式下存在偏向强势方的多种偏见。AI辅助减轻了大部分人工审核偏见，但也放大了少数偏见

Conclusion: 基于结果提出对未来权力不对称冲突中人工审核和人机协同审核系统研究的见解

Abstract: Online power-asymmetric conflicts are prevalent, and most platforms rely on human moderators to conduct moderation currently. Previous studies have been continuously focusing on investigating human moderation biases in different scenarios, while moderation biases under power-asymmetric conflicts remain unexplored. Therefore, we aim to investigate the types of power-related biases human moderators exhibit in power-asymmetric conflict moderation (RQ1) and further explore the influence of AI's suggestions on these biases (RQ2). For this goal, we conducted a mixed design experiment with 50 participants by leveraging the real conflicts between consumers and merchants as a scenario. Results suggest several biases towards supporting the powerful party within these two moderation modes. AI assistance alleviates most biases of human moderation, but also amplifies a few. Based on these results, we propose several insights into future research on human moderation and human-AI collaborative moderation systems for power-asymmetric conflicts.

</details>


### [172] [Bimanual XR Specification of Relative and Absolute Assembly Hierarchies for Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01495)
*Benjamin Yang,Xichen He,Charlie Zou,Jen-Shuo Liu,Barbara Tversky,Steven Feiner*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于远程装配任务的双手机XR交互方法，通过创建相对和绝对对象约束的层次结构来指定机器人高层级遥操作目标


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要一种直观的方法来指定远程装配任务，允许用户定义高层级目标，同时给予机器人软件灵活性来选择最优执行位置，提高效率

Method: 使用双手机XR交互，每只手抓取一个对象创建约束组（可视化为外壳），组可以嵌套成层次结构。每个组可以是相对的（机器人可指定6DoF位姿）或绝对的（用户指定的固定6DoF位姿），相对于其父组

Result: 开发了一种能够创建约束层次结构的交互方法，其中相对约束允许机器人软件基于效率考虑选择子装配体的构建位置，而不是由用户强制指定

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种直观的XR界面，用于指定远程装配任务，平衡了用户意图表达和机器人执行效率优化，通过约束层次结构实现了灵活的任务规范

Abstract: We present a bimanual XR interaction approach for specifying remote assembly tasks as hierarchies of relative and absolute object constraints that specify high-level teleoperation goals for robots. Grabbing one object in each hand creates a constraint group (visualized as a hull) and groups can be nested into hierarchies. Each group can be relative (with a robot-specifiable 6DoF pose) or absolute (with an author-specified fixed 6DoF pose) in relation to its parent. A relative group specifies a subassembly that can be constructed at a location chosen by the robot software for efficiency rather than mandated by the user.

</details>


### [173] [Who Explains Privacy Policies to Me? Embodied and Textual LLM-Powered Privacy Assistants in Virtual Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01638)
*Vincent Freiberger,Moritz Dresch,Florian Alt,Arthur Fleig,Viktorija Paneva*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探索在VR应用商店中嵌入LLM驱动的隐私助手，通过文本聊天和虚拟化身两种交互模式帮助用户进行隐私感知的应用选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VR系统收集细粒度行为与生物特征数据，但隐私政策因语言复杂、篇幅长、与用户交互流程脱节而很少被阅读和理解，需要降低知情同意门槛。

Method: 开发LLM驱动的隐私助手嵌入VR应用商店，实现文本聊天界面和具身虚拟化身两种交互模式；采用被试内设计(N=21)探索性研究，比较无辅助与辅助条件下用户浏览VR生产力应用的行为。

Result: 两种交互模式都能支持用户更审慎地参与隐私信息与决策过程；隐私评分主要作为否决机制而非首要选择驱动；具身交互效果因人而异，文本交互则支持反思性审查。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的隐私助手能有效降低VR应用选择中的知情同意门槛，不同交互模式各有优势，隐私信息呈现方式需考虑用户个体差异和决策支持需求。

Abstract: Virtual Reality (VR) systems collect fine-grained behavioral and biometric data, yet privacy policies are rarely read or understood due to their complex language, length, and poor integration into users' interaction workflows. To lower the barrier to informed consent at the point of choice, we explore a Large Language Model (LLM)-powered privacy assistant embedded into a VR app store to support privacy-aware app selection. The assistant is realized in two interaction modes: a text-based chat interface and an embodied virtual avatar providing spoken explanations. We report on an exploratory within-subjects study $(N = 21)$ in which participants browsed VR productivity applications under unassisted and assisted conditions. Our findings suggest that both interaction modes support more deliberate engagement with privacy information and decision-making, with privacy scores primarily functioning as a veto mechanism rather than a primary selection driver. The impact of embodied interaction varied between participants, while textual interaction supported reflective review.

</details>


### [174] [An Investigation of the Relation Between Immersion and Learning Across Three Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01644)
*Paolo Boffi,Alberto Gallace,Pier Luca Lanzi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究通过CAMIL框架探究沉浸感与学习的关系，在三个领域开发应用并评估，发现VR能提升临场感、用户体验和技术接受度，但学习效果参差不齐，最终提出设计指南和改进策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究沉浸式虚拟现实（VR）在不同教育领域（文化遗产、环境意识、高中物理）中与学习效果的关系，验证CAMIL框架的有效性，并确定VR在教学中何时最有益。

Method: 基于CAMIL框架开发三个领域的应用，采用统一的评估协议（实验室研究和课堂部署），测量学习成果、用户体验、技术接受度、临场感/具身感和晕动症。

Result: 沉浸式VR在临场感、用户体验和技术接受度方面得分更高；但学习成果表现不一：即时后测中事实性知识与对照组相当；最终提出设计指南和CAMIL框架指导的改进策略。

Conclusion: 沉浸式VR能增强体验质量，但学习效果不一定优于传统方法；需要针对性的设计策略来优化学习成果；CAMIL框架为理解沉浸式学习提供了有效视角。

Abstract: We investigate the relationship between immersion and learning across three domains (cultural heritage, environmental awareness, and high school physics) through the lens of the Cognitive Affective Model of Immersive Learning (CAMIL) framework. We present three applications we developed for this investigation, highlighting their shared design elements and domain-specific mechanics. Using a common evaluation protocol across lab studies and a classroom deployment, we assessed learning outcomes, user experience, technology acceptance, presence/embodiment, and cybersickness. Our results show that immersive virtual reality led to higher scores for presence, user experience, and technology acceptance. In contrast, learning outcomes were mixed. In immediate post-test evaluations, factual knowledge scores were comparable between immersive virtual reality and control groups. In the end, we synthesize design guidelines that outline when immersive virtual reality might be most beneficial in didactic contexts, and we provide CAMIL-informed recommendations and strategies to improve learning outcomes and overall experiential quality.

</details>


### [175] [PleaSQLarify: Visual Pragmatic Repair for Natural Language Database Querying](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01795)
*Robin Shing Moon Chan,Rita Sevastjanova,Mennatallah El-Assady*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: PleaSQLarify是一个基于语用推理的自然语言数据库接口，通过增量澄清交互解决用户意图与系统解释之间的不匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自然语言数据库接口在输入模糊性下表现脆弱，标准方法通常将不确定性压缩为单一查询，对用户意图与系统解释之间的不匹配提供很少支持。

Method: 通过语用推理框架，将用户意图与系统先验对齐问题重构为语用修复问题。PleaSQLarify围绕可解释的决策变量构建交互，实现高效澄清。视觉界面通过展示动作空间、请求用户消歧、使信念更新可追踪来补充这一方法。

Result: 在12名参与者的研究中，PleaSQLarify帮助用户识别替代解释并有效解决模糊性。系统支持用户控制，使模糊查询的成功率从基线方法的67%提高到92%。

Conclusion: 语用修复作为一种设计原则，能够促进自然语言界面中有效的用户控制。PleaSQLarify展示了通过结构化交互解决模糊性的可行性，为自然语言数据库接口设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Natural language database interfaces broaden data access, yet they remain brittle under input ambiguity. Standard approaches often collapse uncertainty into a single query, offering little support for mismatches between user intent and system interpretation. We reframe this challenge through pragmatic inference: while users economize expressions, systems operate on priors over the action space that may not align with the users'. In this view, pragmatic repair -- incremental clarification through minimal interaction -- is a natural strategy for resolving underspecification. We present \textsc{PleaSQLarify}, which operationalizes pragmatic repair by structuring interaction around interpretable decision variables that enable efficient clarification. A visual interface complements this by surfacing the action space for exploration, requesting user disambiguation, and making belief updates traceable across turns. In a study with twelve participants, \textsc{PleaSQLarify} helped users recognize alternative interpretations and efficiently resolve ambiguity. Our findings highlight pragmatic repair as a design principle that fosters effective user control in natural language interfaces.

</details>


### [176] [Visual Bias in Simulated Users: The Impact of Luminance and Contrast on Reinforcement Learning-based Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01901)
*Hannah Selder,Charlotte Beylier,Nico Scherf,Arthur Fleig*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 系统分析亮度与对比度如何影响RL驱动的模拟用户在指向和追踪任务中的行为表现，发现静态干扰物条件下亮度变得关键，而运动线索可缓解此问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然强化学习能够模拟HCI任务，但当性能受视觉渲染伪影而非交互设计驱动时，其有效性值得怀疑。需要系统分析亮度和对比度如何影响模拟用户的行为

Method: 训练247个使用RL的模拟用户进行指向和追踪任务，改变任务相关对象、干扰物和背景的亮度，在无干扰物、静态干扰物和移动干扰物条件下评估任务性能和未见亮度下的鲁棒性

Result: 静态干扰物条件下亮度变得关键，显著降低性能和鲁棒性；运动线索可缓解此问题；鲁棒性取决于保持亮度相对顺序而非匹配绝对值；极端亮度（尤其是黑色）通常产生高性能但鲁棒性差

Conclusion: 看似微小的亮度变化能强烈影响学习行为，揭示了RL驱动的模拟用户实际学习内容的重要见解，对HCI模拟的有效性评估具有重要启示

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) enables simulations of HCI tasks, yet their validity is questionable when performance is driven by visual rendering artifacts distinct from interaction design. We provide the first systematic analysis of how luminance and contrast affect behavior by training 247 \RV{simulated users using RL} on pointing and tracking tasks. We vary the luminance of task-relevant objects, distractors, and background under no distractor, static distractor, and moving distractor conditions, and evaluate task performance and robustness to unseen luminances. Results show luminance becomes critical with static distractors, substantially degrading performance and robustness, whereas motion cues mitigate this issue. Furthermore, robustness depends on preserving relational ordering between luminances rather than matching absolute values. Extreme luminances, especially black, often yield high performance but poor robustness. Overall, seemingly minor luminance changes can strongly shape learned behavior, revealing critical insights into what RL-driven simulated users actually learn.

</details>


### [177] [Ignore All Previous Instructions: Jailbreaking as a de-escalatory peace building practise to resist LLM social media bots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01942)
*Huw Day,Adrianna Jezierska,Jessica Woodgate*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 用户将"越狱"视为一种非暴力降级实践，通过绕过LLM安全措施来揭露自动化行为并破坏误导性叙事传播


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注平台主导的内容审核作为对抗LLM操纵政治话语的对策，但本文提出从用户中心视角看待"越狱"作为一种新兴的非暴力降级实践

Method: 提出用户中心视角，将"越狱"定义为用户与疑似LLM驱动的账户互动，通过绕过大型语言模型的安全防护措施来揭露自动化行为

Result: 用户通过"越狱"实践能够暴露自动化行为并破坏误导性叙事的传播，提供了一种对抗LLM操纵政治话语的新途径

Conclusion: "越狱"作为一种用户主导的非暴力降级实践，为应对LLM在社交媒体上操纵政治话语和冲突升级提供了新的对抗策略

Abstract: Large Language Models have intensified the scale and strategic manipulation of political discourse on social media, leading to conflict escalation. The existing literature largely focuses on platform-led moderation as a countermeasure. In this paper, we propose a user-centric view of "jailbreaking" as an emergent, non-violent de-escalation practice. Online users engage with suspected LLM-powered accounts to circumvent large language model safeguards, exposing automated behaviour and disrupting the circulation of misleading narratives.

</details>


### [178] [TactileWalk: Dynamic Electrotactile Patterns for Fingertip-Based Interaction During Walking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01974)
*Vedika Nimbalkar,Roshan Peiris*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究开发指尖电触觉导航系统，比较三种动态模式（单线、双线、方框）在八个方向上的识别效果，发现简单线性模式在行走时识别准确率最高（90.83%）


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发可穿戴导航系统，通过指尖电触觉刺激提供方向指引，解决视觉导航在移动中的局限性，探索最适合移动场景的动态触觉模式设计

Method: 开发基于ESP32微控制器和高电压驱动器的10x6电极网格指尖刺激原型，比较单线、双线、方框三种动态模式在八个方向上的表现，进行两项研究：静态识别实验和行走状态下的导航实验

Result: 静态实验显示简单线性模式识别准确率显著高于复杂形状；行走实验中双线模式准确率最高（90.83%），垂直运动更直观，参与者偏好具有空间冗余性的双线模式

Conclusion: 为移动触觉导航提出设计原则：采用简单、空间冗余的触觉模式以降低认知负荷，双线模式在行走导航中表现最佳，垂直运动方向更符合直觉

Abstract: TactileWalk evaluates dynamic electrotactile patterns on fingertips for wearable navigation. We developed a fingertip stimulation prototype featuring a 10x6 electrode grid driven by an ESP32 microcontroller and high-voltage drivers to enable rapid, independent electrode activation for spatiotemporal pattern rendering. This research compares three dynamic patterns- Single Line, Double Line, and Box-across eight directions presented on the tactile display at the fingertip. Study 1 (stationary) revealed that simple linear patterns were recognized significantly more accurately than complex shapes. Study 2 (walking) confirmed these cues remain robust under movement, where the Double Line pattern yielded the highest accuracy (90.83%). Participants consistently preferred the reinforcing Double Line and found vertical motion more intuitive while walking. We propose design implications for mobile haptics, advocating for simple, spatially redundant patterns to minimize cognitive load during eyes-free navigation.

</details>


### [179] [Teen Vigilance: Navigating Risky Social Interactions on Discord](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02052)
*Elena Koung,Yunhan Liu,Zinan Zhang,Xinning Gui,Yubo Kou*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 青少年在Discord平台上面临混合环境风险，通过个人警惕和社区参与策略来导航危险社交互动


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Discord作为青少年广泛使用的同步通信平台，结合了私密DM、半私密语音频道和公共服务器，创造了混合环境，可能产生复杂且未被充分探索的安全风险。需要了解青少年如何在这种环境中导航风险社交互动。

Method: 通过对16名青少年Discord用户进行访谈，研究他们在平台中导航危险社交互动的策略。

Result: 研究发现青少年在遭遇风险时通过警惕性策略保护自己：在建立友谊前评估可疑互动、使用安全工具、进行受控风险承担以保护隐私和安全。在社区层面，他们通过选择性参与服务器来减轻风险，这一实践得到警惕治理结构的支持。

Conclusion: 警惕性使青少年能够在危险遭遇中采取行动保护自己，这增进了对青少年风险导航能动性的理解，并为以青少年为中心的安全在线环境设计提供了信息。

Abstract: Teenagers are avid users of Discord, a fast growing platform for synchronous communication where they often interact with strangers. Because Discord combines private DMs, semi-private voice channels, and public servers in one place, it creates a hybrid environment that can produce complex and underexplored safety risks for teenagers. Drawing on 16 interviews with teenage Discord users, this study examines their strategies for navigating risky social interactions in the platform. Our findings reveal that when teenagers encounter risks during social interactions, they exercise vigilance by evaluating suspicious interactions before forming friendships, using safety tools, and engaging in controlled risk-taking to safeguard their privacy and security. At the community level, they mitigate risks through selective participation in servers, a practice supported by vigilant governance structures. We discuss how vigilance enables teenagers to act during risky encounters to protect themselves, advancing understanding of teenagers' agency in risk navigation and informing teen-centered designs for safer online environments.

</details>


### [180] [A Resource-Rational Principle for Modeling Visual Attention Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02056)
*Yunpeng Bai*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于资源理性的模拟框架，将视觉注意力建模为在感知、记忆和时间约束下的序列决策过程，为HCI设计提供理论驱动的计算模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算注意力模型要么是描述性的、任务特定的，要么难以解释，缺乏统一的、可解释的、理论驱动的框架来理解视觉注意力分配机制。

Method: 采用资源理性方法，将视觉任务（如阅读和多任务处理）形式化为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP）的有界最优控制问题，通过模拟环境（传统文本阅读和智能眼镜行走阅读）实例化模型。

Result: 模型能够重现经典实证效应，解释理解与安全之间的权衡关系，并在时间压力和界面变化条件下生成新颖预测，验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究为视觉注意力提供了统一的计算解释框架，为理论驱动和资源高效的HCI设计提供了新工具，使眼动行为从理性适应中自然涌现而非手工编码。

Abstract: Understanding how people allocate visual attention is central to Human-Computer Interaction (HCI), yet existing computational models of attention are often either descriptive, task-specific, or difficult to interpret. My dissertation develops a resource-rational, simulation-based framework for modeling visual attention as a sequential decision-making process under perceptual, memory, and time constraints. I formalize visual tasks, such as reading and multitasking, as bounded-optimal control problems using Partially Observable Markov Decision Processes, enabling eye-movement behaviors such as fixation and attention switching to emerge from rational adaptation rather than being hand-coded or purely data-driven. These models are instantiated in simulation environments spanning traditional text reading and reading-while-walking with smart glasses, where they reproduce classic empirical effects, explain observed trade-offs between comprehension and safety, and generate novel predictions under time pressure and interface variation. Collectively, this work contributes a unified computational account of visual attention, offering new tools for theory-driven and resource-efficient HCI design.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [181] [Semijoins of Annotated Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00448)
*Phokion G. Kolaitis*

Main category: cs.DB
