TL;DR: 论文研究了带注释关系（annotated relations）的半连接操作理论，证明了对于具有内一致性属性的正交换幺半群，模式是无环的当且仅当在该幺半群上存在全约简器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统关系代数中，半连接操作在查询处理中具有基础性作用，且无环模式可通过全约简器（一系列半连接操作）来表征。近年来，无环性研究已扩展到带注释的关系（注释值来自正交换幺半群），但尚不清楚这种基于全约简器的无环性表征是否适用于带注释关系。

Method: 首先在幺半群上引入半连接函数的概念，并刻画存在半连接函数的正交换幺半群。然后引入幺半群上模式的"全约简器"概念，证明对于具有内一致性属性的正交换幺半群，模式是无环的当且仅当在该幺半群上存在全约简器。

Result: 建立了带注释关系的半连接理论，证明了对于具有内一致性属性的正交换幺半群，无环模式与存在全约简器之间的等价关系成立，将经典结果推广到带注释关系的情形。

Conclusion: 成功将传统关系代数中基于半连接的无环性表征扩展到带注释关系，为带注释数据库的查询处理和模式分析提供了理论基础，填补了该领域的重要理论空白。

Abstract: The semijoin operation is a fundamental operation of relational algebra that has been extensively used in query processing. Furthermore, semijoins have been used to formulate desirable properties of acyclic schemas; in particular, a schema is acyclic if and only if it has a full reducer, i.e., a sequence of semijoins that converts a given collection of relations to a globally consistent collection of relations. In recent years, the study of acyclicity has been extended to annotated relations, where the annotations are values from some positive commutative monoid. So far, however, it has not been known if the characterization of acyclicity in terms of full reducers extends to annotated relations. Here, we develop a theory of semijoins of annotated relations. To this effect, we first introduce the notion of a semijoin function on a monoid and then characterize the positive commutative monoids for which a semijoin function exists. After this, we introduce the notion of a full reducer for a schema on a monoid and show that the following is true for every positive commutative monoid that has the inner consistency property: a schema is acyclic if and only if it has a full reducer on that monoid.

</details>


### [182] [COLE$^+$: Towards Practical Column-based Learned Storage for Blockchain Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00509)
*Ce Zhang,Cheng Xu,Haibo Hu,Jianliang Xu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: COLE⁺是一个增强的区块链存储解决方案，通过支持链重组和状态修剪来改进现有的COLE系统，使用内存树结构和可修剪版本树来减少存储开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链的透明性和完整性带来了显著的存储挑战（如以太坊节点需要约23TB存储，年增长4TB）。现有解决方案COLE虽然通过列式设计和学习索引减少了存储大小并提高了吞吐量，但存在两个主要限制：1）不支持区块链分叉时的链重组；2）无法进行状态修剪以最小化存储开销。

Method: COLE⁺提出两种核心创新：1）用于处理链重组的支持回滚的内存树结构，利用内容定义分块（CDC）为每个区块维护一致的哈希摘要；2）用于磁盘存储的新型两级Merkle哈希树结构（称为可修剪版本树），支持高效的状态修剪。

Result: 理论和实证分析均表明COLE⁺的有效性，证明其在实际区块链系统中具有应用潜力。具体而言，该系统解决了COLE的局限性，同时保持了存储效率优势。

Conclusion: COLE⁺是一个实用的增强存储解决方案，通过创新的内存和磁盘数据结构成功解决了链重组和状态修剪问题，为现实世界区块链系统的存储挑战提供了有效应对方案。

Abstract: Blockchain provides a decentralized and tamper-resistant ledger for securely recording transactions across a network of untrusted nodes. While its transparency and integrity are beneficial, the substantial storage requirements for maintaining a complete transaction history present significant challenges. For example, Ethereum nodes require around 23TB of storage, with an annual growth rate of 4TB. Prior studies have employed various strategies to mitigate the storage challenges. Notably, COLE significantly reduces storage size and improves throughput by adopting a column-based design that incorporates a learned index, effectively eliminating data duplication in the storage layer. However, this approach has limitations in supporting chain reorganization during blockchain forks and state pruning to minimize storage overhead. In this paper, we propose COLE$^+$, an enhanced storage solution designed to address these limitations. COLE$^+$ incorporates a novel rewind-supported in-memory tree structure for handling chain reorganization, leveraging content-defined chunking (CDC) to maintain a consistent hash digest for each block. For on-disk storage, a new two-level Merkle Hash Tree (MHT) structure, called prunable version tree, is developed to facilitate efficient state pruning. Both theoretical and empirical analyses show the effectiveness of COLE$^+$ and its potential for practical application in real-world blockchain systems.

</details>


### [183] [A Tree-Structured Two-Phase Commit Framework for OceanBase: Optimizing Scalability and Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00866)
*Quanqing Xu,Chen Qian,Chuanhui Yang,Fanyu Kong,Guixiang Liu,Fusheng Han,Zixiang Zhai*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种面向OceanBase的树形2PC框架，通过日志流作为原子参与者、动态树形拓扑和未知状态处理，显著降低了分布式事务的协调开销和延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统两阶段提交协议在分布式数据库中面临高协调开销、高延迟和动态分区转移恢复复杂的问题。需要一种能减少协调成本、处理动态拓扑变化并保证一致性的新方案。

Method: 提出三个创新：1) 将日志流作为原子参与者替代分区级协调；2) 设计基于协调器根DAG拓扑的树形2PC协议，通过递归构建提交树处理分区迁移；3) 引入prepare-unknown和trans-unknown状态防止参与者丢失上下文时的违反一致性。

Result: 实验评估显示性能接近单机事务，显著降低了延迟和带宽消耗。例如，当N=100时协调开销减少99%，验证了框架在现代分布式数据库中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该树形2PC框架通过创新的日志流参与者、动态树形拓扑和未知状态机制，有效解决了分布式事务的协调开销、动态分区迁移和一致性保证问题，为现代分布式数据库提供了高性能的事务处理方案。

Abstract: Modern distributed databases face challenges in achieving transactional consistency across distributed partitions. Traditional two-phase commit (2PC) protocols incur high coordination overhead and latency, and require complex recovery for dynamic partition transfers. This paper introduces a novel tree-shaped 2PC framework for OceanBase that leverages single-machine log streams to address these challenges through three innovations. First, we propose log streams as atomic participants, replacing partition-level coordination. By treating each log stream as the commit unit, a transaction spanning $N$ co-located partitions interacts with one participant, reducing coordination overhead by orders of magnitude (e.g., 99 percent reduction for $N=100$). Second, we design a tree-shaped 2PC protocol with coordinator-rooted DAG topology that dynamically handles partition transfers by recursively constructing commit trees. When a partition migrates during a transaction, the protocol embeds migration contexts as leaf nodes, eliminating explicit participant list updates, resolving circular dependencies, and ensuring linearizable commits under topology changes. Third, we introduce prepare-unknown and trans-unknown states to prevent consistency violations when participants lose context. These states signal uncertainty during retries, avoiding erroneous aborts from so-called lying participants while isolating users from ambiguity. Experimental evaluation demonstrates performance approaching that of single-machine transactions, with reduced latency and bandwidth consumption, validating the framework's effectiveness for modern distributed databases.

</details>


### [184] [A Framework for Transparent Reporting of Data Quality Analysis Across the Clinical Electronic Health Record Data Lifecycle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00921)
*Melinda Wassell,Kerryn Butler-Henderson,Karin Verspoor*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 开发了一个用于临床电子健康记录数据质量评估透明报告的框架，该框架区分数据生成组织和接收组织，定义5个关键生命周期阶段，帮助定位数据质量问题来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据质量和透明度是影响临床AI模型采用和临床医生信任的关键因素，但现有数据质量研究往往未明确质量检查在数据生命周期中的位置，导致数据来源和适用性不确定。

Method: 通过迭代分析识别临床数据生命周期的参与者和阶段，开发了一个报告框架，区分数据生成组织和数据接收组织，允许用户将数据质量参数映射到数据生命周期的各个阶段。

Result: 该框架定义了5个关键生命周期阶段和多个参与者，应用于真实世界数据集时，能够有效揭示数据质量问题可能产生的环节。

Conclusion: 该框架为数据质量评估报告提供了结构化方法，可增强数据适用性的透明度，支持可靠的临床研究、AI模型开发和内部组织治理，为研究人员理解数据来源和组织改进数据质量提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Data quality (DQ) and transparency of secondary data are critical factors that delay the adoption of clinical AI models and affect clinician trust in them. Many DQ studies fail to clarify where, along the lifecycle, quality checks occur, leading to uncertainty about provenance and fitness for reuse. This study develops a framework for transparent reporting of DQ assessments across the clinical electronic health record (EHR) data lifecycle. The reporting framework was developed through iterative analysis to identify actors and phases of the clinical data lifecycle. The framework distinguishes between data-generating organizations and data-receiving organizations to allow users to map DQ parameters to stages across the data lifecycle. The framework defines 5 key lifecycle phases and multiple actors. When applied to the real-world dataset, the framework demonstrated applicability in revealing where DQ issues may originate. The framework provides a structured approach for reporting DQ assessments, which can enhance transparency regarding data fitness for reuse, supporting reliable clinical research, AI model development, and internal organisational governance. This work provides practical guidance for researchers to understand data provenance and for organisations to target DQ improvement efforts across the data lifecycle.

</details>


### [185] [VectorMaton: Efficient Vector Search with Pattern Constraints via an Enhanced Suffix Automaton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01525)
*Haoxuan Xie,Siqiang Luo*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出VectorMaton索引，解决向量数据库中序列属性的模式匹配与相似性搜索联合查询问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实应用中向量常伴随序列属性（如文本、生物序列），现有向量数据库支持分类和数值属性的过滤，但不支持序列属性的模式谓词（如LIKE、CONTAINS）。随着向量数据库采用SQL风格查询接口，需要在向量相似性搜索基础上支持序列模式过滤。

Method: 提出VectorMaton，一种基于自动机的索引，将模式过滤与高效向量搜索集成，同时保持索引大小与数据集大小相当。该方法支持序列属性上的模式谓词（子串匹配）与向量相似性搜索的联合查询。

Result: 在真实数据集上的实验表明，VectorMaton在所有基线方法中表现最优，在相同准确率下实现高达10倍的查询吞吐量提升，索引大小减少高达18倍。

Conclusion: VectorMaton有效解决了向量数据库中序列属性模式匹配与相似性搜索的联合查询问题，为向量数据库系统提供了重要的功能扩展。

Abstract: Approximate nearest neighbor search (ANNS) has become a cornerstone in modern vector database systems. Given a query vector, ANNS retrieves the closest vectors from a set of base vectors. In real-world applications, vectors are often accompanied by additional information, such as sequences or structured attributes, motivating the need for fine-grained vector search with constraints on this auxiliary data. Existing methods support attribute-based filtering or range-based filtering on categorical and numerical attributes, but they do not support pattern predicates over sequence attributes. In relational databases, predicates such as LIKE and CONTAINS are fundamental operators for filtering records based on substring patterns. As vector databases increasingly adopt SQL-style query interfaces, enabling pattern predicates over sequence attributes (e.g., texts and biological sequences) alongside vector similarity search becomes essential. In this paper, we formulate a novel problem: given a set of vectors each associated with a sequence, retrieve the nearest vectors whose sequences contain a given query pattern. To address this challenge, we propose VectorMaton, an automaton-based index that integrates pattern filtering with efficient vector search, while maintaining an index size comparable to the dataset size. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that VectorMaton consistently outperforms all baselines, achieving up to 10x higher query throughput at the same accuracy and up to 18x reduction in index size.

</details>


### [186] [Adversarial Query Synthesis via Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01570)
*Jeffrey Tao,Yimeng Zeng,Haydn Thomas Jones,Natalie Maus,Osbert Bastani,Jacob R. Gardner,Ryan Marcus*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出基于贝叶斯优化的自动搜索方法，用于生成数据库基准测试中的困难查询，相比现有基准测试可将优化空间提升两倍以上


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器学习组件越来越多地集成到数据库系统中，基准测试工作负载对数据库管理研究社区变得至关重要。传统方法需要大量人工努力来寻找困难的基准查询。

Method: 采用贝叶斯优化技术自动搜索困难的基准查询，显著减少通常所需的人工工作量。

Result: 初步实验表明，该方法生成的查询相比现有基准测试具有超过两倍的优化空间（优化头room）。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯优化技术能够有效自动生成困难的数据库基准查询，大幅提升基准测试的挑战性和实用性。

Abstract: Benchmark workloads are extremely important to the database management research community, especially as more machine learning components are integrated into database systems. Here, we propose a Bayesian optimization technique to automatically search for difficult benchmark queries, significantly reducing the amount of manual effort usually required. In preliminary experiments, we show that our approach can generate queries with more than double the optimization headroom compared to existing benchmarks.

</details>


### [187] [Graph-centric Cross-model Data Integration and Analytics in a Unified Multi-model Database](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01598)
*Zepeng Liu,Sheng Wang,Shixun Huang,Hailang Qiu,Yuwei Peng,Jiale Feng,Shunan Liao,Yushuai Ji,Zhiyong Peng*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: GredoDB是一个统一的多模型数据库，原生支持图、关系和文档模型，通过拓扑和属性感知的图算子、统一的跨模型优化框架和并行分析架构，显著提升了图中心跨模型数据集成与分析任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代应用生成的数据日益多样化，需要超越简单检索转向高级分析任务（如预测和推荐）。图中心跨模型数据集成与分析（GCDIA）变得日益重要，但现有的多模型数据库（MMDBs）在支持GCDIA方面存在效率问题：它们通常将图处理与其他模型分离，缺乏全局优化，且采用逐元组执行方式，导致性能和可扩展性受限。

Method: 提出了GredoDB统一多模型数据库，包含三个核心技术：1）拓扑和属性感知的图算子，用于高效的谓词感知遍历；2）统一的GCDI优化框架，利用跨模型相关性进行全局优化；3）并行GCDA架构，将中间结果物化以实现算子级执行。

Result: 在广泛采用的多模型基准测试M2Bench上的实验表明，GredoDB在响应时间方面：对于GCDI任务，相比最先进的MMDBs，最高达到107.89倍、平均10.89倍的加速；对于GCDA任务，最高达到356.72倍、平均37.79倍的加速。

Conclusion: GredoDB通过统一的多模型数据库架构，有效解决了现有系统在图中心跨模型数据集成与分析方面的性能瓶颈，显著提升了GCDIA任务的执行效率，为现代数据密集型应用提供了高效的多模型数据处理解决方案。

Abstract: Graph-centric cross-model data integration and analytics (GCDIA) refer to tasks that leverage the graph model as a central paradigm to integrate relevant information across heterogeneous data models, such as relational and document, and subsequently perform complex analytics such as regression and similarity computation. As modern applications generate increasingly diverse data and move beyond simple retrieval toward advanced analytical objectives (e.g., prediction and recommendation), GCDIA has become increasingly important. Existing multi-model databases (MMDBs) struggle to efficiently support both integration (GCDI) and analytics (GCDA) in GCDIA. They typically separate graph processing from other models without global optimization for GCDI, while relying on tuple-at-a-time execution for GCDA, leading to limited performance and scalability. To address these limitations, we propose GredoDB, a unified MMDB that natively supports storing graph, relational, and document models, while efficiently processing GCDIA. Specifically, we design 1) topology- and attribute-aware graph operators for efficient predicate-aware traversal, 2) a unified GCDI optimization framework to exploit cross-model correlations, and 3) a parallel GCDA architecture that materializes intermediate results for operator-level execution. Experiments on the widely adopted multi-model benchmark M2Bench demonstrate that, in terms of response time, GredoDB achieves up to 107.89 times and an average of 10.89 times speedup on GCDI, and up to 356.72 times and an average of 37.79 times on GCDA, compared to state-of-the-art (SOTA) MMDBs.

</details>


### [188] [Disk-Resident Graph ANN Search: An Experimental Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01779)
*Xiaoyu Chen,Jinxiu Qu,Yitong Song,Shuhang Lu,Huiling Li,Minghui Jiang,Wei Zhou,Jianliang Xu,Xuanhe Zhou,Fan Wu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 该论文对基于磁盘的图近似最近邻（ANN）方法进行了系统性实验研究，揭示了维度感知设计的重要性、现有布局策略的低I/O利用率、页面大小的关键影响以及更新策略的负载依赖权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据量增长而内存容量有限，基于磁盘的图ANN方法已成为内存设计的实用替代方案，但现有技术在设计上存在广泛差异，缺乏对其性能权衡的系统性理解。

Method: 1) 将系统分解为存储策略、磁盘布局、缓存管理、查询执行和更新机制五个关键技术组件，构建统一分类体系；2) 对各组件代表性策略进行细粒度评估；3) 进行端到端实验和参数敏感性分析；4) 揭示非直观发现并推导设计指南。

Result: 研究发现：1) 向量维度从根本上重塑组件有效性，需要维度感知设计；2) 现有布局策略I/O利用率极低（≤15%）；3) 页面大小对可行性和效率至关重要，布局优化时较小页面更优；4) 更新策略在就地与异地设计间存在明确负载依赖权衡。

Conclusion: 基于研究发现提出了系统设计和配置的实用指南，并指出了未来研究方向，强调需要维度感知设计、提高I/O利用率的布局策略、优化页面大小选择以及负载自适应的更新机制。

Abstract: As data volumes grow while memory capacity remains limited, disk-resident graph-based approximate nearest neighbor (ANN) methods have become a practical alternative to memory-resident designs, shifting the bottleneck from computation to disk I/O. However, since their technical designs diverge widely across storage, layout, and execution paradigms, a systematic understanding of their fundamental performance trade-offs remains elusive. This paper presents a comprehensive experimental study of disk-resident graph-based ANN methods. First, we decompose such systems into five key technical components, i.e., storage strategy, disk layout, cache management, query execution, and update mechanism, and build a unified taxonomy of existing designs across these components. Second, we conduct fine-grained evaluations of representative strategies for each technical component to analyze the trade-offs in throughput, recall, and resource utilization. Third, we perform comprehensive end-to-end experiments and parameter-sensitivity analyses to evaluate overall system performance under diverse configurations. Fourth, our study reveals several non-obvious findings: (1) vector dimensionality fundamentally reshapes component effectiveness, necessitating dimension-aware design; (2) existing layout strategies exhibit surprisingly low I/O utilization (less than or equal to 15%); (3) page size critically affects feasibility and efficiency, with smaller pages preferred when layouts are carefully optimized; and (4) update strategies present clear workload-dependent trade-offs between in-place and out-of-place designs. Based on these findings, we derive practical guidelines for system design and configuration, and outline promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [189] [Bespoke OLAP: Synthesizing Workload-Specific One-size-fits-one Database Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02001)
*Johannes Wehrstein,Timo Eckmann,Matthias Jasny,Carsten Binnig*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Bespoke OLAP是一个完全自主的合成流水线，能够为特定工作负载自动生成高性能数据库引擎，相比通用系统获得数量级性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代OLAP引擎为支持任意分析工作负载而设计，这种通用性带来了结构开销（运行时模式解释、间接层、抽象边界）。为固定工作负载专门定制的引擎可以消除这些成本并利用特定于工作负载的数据结构和执行算法，但历史上由于高昂的手动工程成本而不切实际。LLM代码合成的最新进展挑战了这一权衡。

Method: Bespoke OLAP是一个完全自主的合成流水线，集成了迭代性能评估和自动化验证，指导从存储到查询执行的合成过程。该方法通过系统化的性能反馈、结构化精炼和仔细管理深层架构相互依赖关系来克服简单提示LLM生成数据库引擎的不足。

Result: Bespoke OLAP能够在几分钟到几小时内从零开始生成特定于工作负载的引擎，相比现代通用系统（如DuckDB）实现了数量级的加速。

Conclusion: 通过LLM驱动的自动化系统生成，可以为特定工作负载构建高性能定制化数据库引擎，这在经济上变得可行，突破了传统手动工程成本的限制。

Abstract: Modern OLAP engines are designed to support arbitrary analytical workloads, but this generality incurs structural overhead, including runtime schema interpretation, indirection layers, and abstraction boundaries, even in highly optimized systems. An engine specialized to a fixed workload can eliminate these costs and exploit workload-specific data structures and execution algorithms for substantially higher performance. Historically, constructing such bespoke engines has been economically impractical due to the high manual engineering effort. Recent advances in LLM-based code synthesis challenge this tradeoff by enabling automated system generation. However, naively prompting an LLM to produce a database engine does not yield a correct or efficient design, as effective synthesis requires systematic performance feedback, structured refinement, and careful management of deep architectural interdependencies. We present Bespoke OLAP, a fully autonomous synthesis pipeline for constructing high-performance database engines tightly tailored to a given workload. Our approach integrates iterative performance evaluation and automated validation to guide synthesis from storage to query execution. We demonstrate that Bespoke OLAP can generate a workload-specific engine from scratch within minutes to hours, achieving order-of-magnitude speedups over modern general-purpose systems such as DuckDB.

</details>


### [190] [GenDB: The Next Generation of Query Processing -- Synthesized, Not Engineered](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02081)
*Jiale Lao,Immanuel Trummer*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: GenDB：基于大语言模型的查询处理系统，通过为每个查询生成定制化执行代码，替代传统查询引擎的持续开发和维护


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统查询处理引擎需要大量专家优化和工程化，难以快速适应新技术和用户需求变化，同时系统扩展困难且开发新系统成本高昂。大语言模型的发展为下一代查询处理系统提供了新机遇。

Method: 提出GenDB系统，使用LLM为每个传入查询合成执行代码，而不是构建和维护复杂的查询处理引擎。系统采用多智能体架构，使用Claude Code Agent作为底层组件，生成针对特定数据、工作负载和硬件资源的实例优化定制化查询执行代码。

Result: 在OLAP工作负载上评估GenDB原型，使用TPC-H基准查询和专门设计的新基准（减少LLM训练数据泄露风险）。与DuckDB、Umbra、MonetDB、ClickHouse、PostgreSQL等先进查询引擎相比，GenDB取得了显著更好的性能。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的查询处理系统代表下一代发展方向，GenDB展示了通过代码生成实现实例优化的可行性。虽然存在当前限制，但为未来扩展和相关研究挑战指明了方向。

Abstract: Traditional query processing relies on engines that are carefully optimized and engineered by many experts. However, new techniques and user requirements evolve rapidly, and existing systems often cannot keep pace. At the same time, these systems are difficult to extend due to their internal complexity, and developing new systems requires substantial engineering effort and cost. In this paper, we argue that recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) are starting to shape the next generation of query processing systems.
  We propose using LLMs to synthesize execution code for each incoming query, instead of continuously building, extending, and maintaining complex query processing engines. As a proof of concept, we present GenDB, an LLM-powered agentic system that generates instance-optimized and customized query execution code tailored to specific data, workloads, and hardware resources.
  We implemented an early prototype of GenDB that uses Claude Code Agent as the underlying component in the multi-agent system, and we evaluate it on OLAP workloads. We use queries from the well-known TPC-H benchmark and also construct a new benchmark designed to reduce potential data leakage from LLM training data. We compare GenDB with state-of-the-art query engines, including DuckDB, Umbra, MonetDB, ClickHouse, and PostgreSQL. GenDB achieves significantly better performance than these systems. Finally, we discuss the current limitations of GenDB and outline future extensions and related research challenges.

</details>


### [191] [Milliscale: Fast Commit on Low-Latency Object Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02108)
*Jiatang Zhou,Kaisong Huang,Tianzheng Wang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Milliscale是一个针对低延迟对象存储优化的内存OLTP引擎，通过减少提交延迟和对象访问请求数量来提升性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Amazon S3 Express One Zone等低延迟对象存储服务具有毫秒级延迟和可变对象支持，适合OLTP引擎直接提交事务和持久化操作数据，但原生采用仍会导致高提交延迟

Method: 开发Milliscale内存优化OLTP引擎，采用新技术降低提交延迟并减少对象访问请求数量

Result: 评估显示Milliscale相比基线系统提供更低的提交延迟，同时维持高吞吐量

Conclusion: Milliscale成功优化了低延迟对象存储上的OLTP性能，解决了S3 Express One Zone和现代去中心化日志系统的特性带来的延迟问题

Abstract: With millisecond-level latency and support for mutable objects, recent low-latency object storage services as represented by Amazon S3 Express One Zone have become an attractive option for OLTP engines to directly commit transactions and persist operational data with transparent strong consistency, high durability and high availability. But a naïve adoption can still lead to high commit latency due to idiosyncrasies of S3 Express One Zone and modern decentralized logging.
  This paper presents Milliscale, a memory-optimized OLTP engine for low-latency object storage. Milliscale optimizes commit latency with new techniques that lower commit delays and reduce the number of object access requests. Our evaluation using representative benchmarks shows that Milliscale delivers much lower commit latency than baselines while sustaining high throughput.

</details>


### [192] [Catapults to the Rescue: Accelerating Vector Search by Exploiting Query Locality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02164)
*Sami Abuzakuk,Anne-Marie Kermarrec,Rafael Pires,Mathis Randl,Martijn de Vos*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: CatapultDB是一种在近似最近邻搜索的图索引上动态添加捷径边的机制，利用查询局部性提高搜索效率，无需修改底层搜索算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图索引的边集只根据索引数据变化而修改，从不适应查询工作负载。实际查询流具有强时空局部性，但每个查询都必须从固定或随机的入口点重新遍历相同的中继跳转，这造成了资源浪费。

Method: CatapultDB在现有图索引之上添加一个额外的层，注入称为"弹射器"的捷径边，将查询区域连接到频繁访问的目标节点。弹射器动态维护，标准向量搜索算法保持不变，当存在合适弹射器时，查询只需被路由到更好的起始点。

Result: CatapultDB在保持等效或更好召回率的情况下，相比DiskANN将吞吐量提高了最高2.51倍；匹配基于LSH方法的效率，同时不牺牲过滤功能或需要索引重建；能够优雅适应工作负载变化，优于基于缓存的替代方案。

Conclusion: CatapultDB首次实现了在ANN索引中动态确定搜索起始点的机制，通过利用查询局部性显著提升搜索效率，同时保持底层系统的完整功能集，包括过滤搜索、动态插入和磁盘驻留索引。

Abstract: Graph-based indexing is the dominant approach for approximate nearest neighbor search in vector databases, offering high recall with low latency across billions of vectors. However, in such indices, the edge set of the proximity graph is only modified to reflect changes in the indexed data, never to adapt to the query workload. This is wasteful: real-world query streams exhibit strong spatial and temporal locality, yet every query must re-traverse the same intermediate hops from fixed or random entry points. We present CatapultDB, a lightweight mechanism that, for the first time, dynamically determines where to begin the search in an ANN index on the fly, therefore exploiting query locality. CatapultDB injects shortcut edges called catapults that connect query regions to frequently visited destination nodes. Catapults are maintained as an additional layer on top of the graph, so the standard vector search algorithm remains unchanged: queries are simply routed to a better starting point when an appropriate catapult exists. This transparent design preserves the full feature set of the underlying system, including filtered search, dynamic insertions, and disk-resident indices. We implement CatapultDB and evaluate it using four workloads with varying amounts of bias. Our experiments show that CatapultDB increases throughput by up to 2.51x compared to DiskANN at equivalent or better recall, matches the efficiency of LSH-based approaches without sacrificing filtering or requiring index reconstruction, and adapts gracefully to workload shifts, unlike cache-based alternatives.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [193] [StaTS: Spectral Trajectory Schedule Learning for Adaptive Time Series Forecasting with Frequency Guided Denoiser](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00037)
*Jintao Zhang,Zirui Liu,Mingyue Cheng,Xianquan Wang,Zhiding Liu,Qi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StaTS是一种用于概率时间序列预测的扩散模型，通过学习噪声调度和去噪器交替更新，改善结构保持和逐步可逆性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定噪声调度会产生难以反转的中间状态和偏离近噪声假设的终端状态，现有方法依赖时域条件且很少建模调度引起的频谱退化，限制了跨噪声级别的结构恢复

Method: 提出StaTS模型，包含：1) 频谱轨迹调度器(STS)，学习具有频谱正则化的数据自适应噪声调度以改善结构保持和逐步可逆性；2) 频率引导去噪器(FGD)，估计调度引起的频谱失真并用于调制去噪强度以实现跨扩散步长和变量的异质恢复；采用两阶段训练程序稳定调度学习和去噪器优化之间的耦合

Result: 在多个真实世界基准测试中显示出一致的性能提升，同时在使用更少采样步数时保持强大性能

Conclusion: StaTS通过学习自适应噪声调度和频率引导去噪，有效解决了扩散模型在时间序列预测中的结构恢复和可逆性问题，实现了更好的概率预测性能

Abstract: Diffusion models have been used for probabilistic time series forecasting and show strong potential. However, fixed noise schedules often produce intermediate states that are hard to invert and a terminal state that deviates from the near noise assumption. Meanwhile, prior methods rely on time domain conditioning and seldom model schedule induced spectral degradation, which limits structure recovery across noise levels. We propose StaTS, a diffusion model for probabilistic time series forecasting that learns the noise schedule and the denoiser through alternating updates. StaTS includes Spectral Trajectory Scheduler (STS) that learns a data adaptive noise schedule with spectral regularization to improve structural preservation and stepwise invertibility, and Frequency Guided Denoiser (FGD) that estimates schedule induced spectral distortion and uses it to modulate denoising strength for heterogeneous restoration across diffusion steps and variables. A two stage training procedure stabilizes the coupling between schedule learning and denoiser optimization. Experiments on multiple real world benchmarks show consistent gains, while maintaining strong performance with fewer sampling steps. Our code is available at https://github.com/zjt-gpu/StaTS/.

</details>


### [194] [CARE: Confounder-Aware Aggregation for Reliable LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00039)
*Jitian Zhao,Changho Shin,Tzu-Heng Huang,Satya Sai Srinath Namburi GNVV,Frederic Sala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CARE：一个考虑混杂因素的LLM评委聚合框架，通过显式建模真实质量信号和共享混杂因子，解决传统聚合方法因评委相关误差而失效的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM-as-a-judge集成评估的标准聚合机制存在根本缺陷：它们隐含假设评委提供独立的质量估计，但实际上LLM评委存在由共享潜在混杂因素（如冗长性、风格偏好或训练伪影）引起的相关误差，导致多数投票或平均等标准聚合规则增益有限甚至放大系统性错误。

Method: CARE（Confounder-Aware Aggregation）框架显式地将LLM评委评分建模为来自潜在真实质量信号和共享混杂因素。该方法无需访问真实标签即可分离质量和混杂因素，而不是启发式地重新加权评委。提供了在共享混杂因素下的可识别性理论保证和有限样本恢复保证。

Result: 在12个公共基准测试中（涵盖连续评分、二元分类和成对偏好设置），CARE显著提高了聚合准确性，将误差降低了高达26.8%。量化了当聚合模型忽略混杂潜在因素时产生的系统性偏差。

Conclusion: CARE通过显式建模共享混杂因素，解决了LLM评委聚合中的相关误差问题，提供了理论保证并在多个评估场景中实现了显著的准确性提升，为可扩展的LLM评估提供了更可靠的聚合框架。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-judge ensembles are the standard paradigm for scalable evaluation, but their aggregation mechanisms suffer from a fundamental flaw: they implicitly assume that judges provide independent estimates of true quality. However, in practice, LLM judges exhibit correlated errors caused by shared latent confounders -- such as verbosity, stylistic preferences, or training artifacts -- causing standard aggregation rules like majority vote or averaging to provide little gain or even amplify systematic mistakes. To address this, we introduce CARE, a confounder-aware aggregation framework that explicitly models LLM judge scores as arising from both a latent true-quality signal and shared confounding factors. Rather than heuristically re-weighting judges, CARE separates quality from confounders without access to ground-truth labels. We provide theoretical guarantees for identifiability and finite-sample recovery under shared confounders, and we quantify the systematic bias incurred when aggregation models omit confounding latent factors. Across 12 public benchmarks spanning continuous scoring, binary classification, and pairwise preference settings, CARE improves aggregation accuracy, reducing error by up to 26.8\%. Code is released in \href{https://github.com/SprocketLab/CARE}{https://github.com/SprocketLab/CARE}.

</details>


### [195] [SEval-NAS: A Search-Agnostic Evaluation for Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00099)
*Atah Nuh Mih,Jianzhou Wang,Truong Thanh Hung Nguyen,Hung Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SEval-NAS：一种用于神经架构搜索的度量评估机制，可将架构转换为字符串并嵌入为向量来预测性能指标，特别适用于硬件感知NAS中的延迟和内存预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经架构搜索（NAS）的评估程序通常是硬编码的，限制了引入新度量的能力。这在硬件感知NAS中尤为突出，因为目标依赖于边缘硬件等目标设备。需要一种灵活的度量评估机制来克服这一限制。

Method: 提出SEval-NAS度量评估机制：1）将神经架构转换为字符串表示；2）将字符串嵌入为向量；3）使用嵌入向量预测性能指标（如准确率、延迟、内存）。在NATS-Bench和HW-NAS-Bench数据集上评估，并将SEval-NAS集成到FreeREA算法中。

Result: Kendall's τ相关性分析显示，SEval-NAS在延迟和内存预测方面比准确率预测更强，表明其适合作为硬件成本预测器。成功将SEval-NAS集成到FreeREA中，能够评估原始未包含的度量，对FreeREA生成的架构进行排名，保持搜索时间基本不变，且只需最小算法修改。

Conclusion: SEval-NAS提供了一种灵活的度量评估机制，能够有效预测硬件感知NAS中的性能指标，特别在延迟和内存预测方面表现优异。该方法易于集成到现有NAS算法中，为引入新评估标准提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Neural architecture search (NAS) automates the discovery of neural networks that meet specified criteria, yet its evaluation procedures are often hardcoded, limiting the ability to introduce new metrics. This issue is especially pronounced in hardware-aware NAS, where objectives depend on target devices such as edge hardware. To address this limitation, we propose SEval-NAS, a metric-evaluation mechanism that converts architectures to strings, embeds them as vectors, and predicts performance metrics. Using NATS-Bench and HW-NAS-Bench, we evaluated accuracy, latency, and memory. Kendall's $τ$ correlations showed stronger latency and memory predictions than accuracy, indicating the suitability of SEval-NAS as a hardware cost predictor. We further integrated SEval-NAS into FreeREA to evaluate metrics not originally included. The method successfully ranked FreeREA-generated architectures, maintained search time, and required minimal algorithmic changes. Our implementation is available at: https://github.com/Analytics-Everywhere-Lab/neural-architecture-search

</details>


### [196] [Attn-QAT: 4-Bit Attention With Quantization-Aware Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00040)
*Peiyuan Zhang,Matthew Noto,Wenxuan Tan,Chengquan Jiang,Will Lin,Wei Zhou,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文首次系统研究了注意力机制的4位量化感知训练，针对FP4 GPU上注意力计算的关键挑战，提出了Attn-QAT方法，实现了稳定的FP4注意力训练和推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FP4 GPU为实现端到端4位计算提供了硬件支持，但注意力机制由于FP4的极小动态范围和注意力激活的重尾分布，成为主要障碍。现有方法采用"即插即用"的量化感知训练会导致训练不稳定，需要系统性的解决方案。

Method: 提出了Attn-QAT方法，基于两个关键原则：1) 在反向传播中匹配低精度注意力分数重计算；2) 解决Flash Attention梯度计算中的隐式精度假设。实现了融合的Triton内核用于训练和FP4推理内核。

Result: 在扩散模型和语言模型上，Attn-QAT恢复了FP4注意力带来的质量下降，无需先前FP4注意力方法中使用的显式离群值缓解启发式方法，在RTX 5090上实现了高达1.5倍的加速。

Conclusion: 该研究首次系统解决了FP4注意力量化训练的关键挑战，提出的Attn-QAT方法通过匹配精度和解决隐式假设，实现了稳定训练和高效推理，为端到端FP4计算铺平了道路。

Abstract: Achieving reliable 4-bit attention is a prerequisite for end-to-end FP4 computation on emerging FP4-capable GPUs, yet attention remains the main obstacle due to FP4's tiny dynamic range and attention's heavy-tailed activations. This paper presents the first systematic study of 4-bit quantization-aware training (QAT) for attention. We find that "drop-in" QAT, which naively combines an FP4 forward pass with a high-precision Flash Attention (FA)-style backward pass, leads to training instability. We identify two key principles for stable FP4 attention: (1) matching low-precision recomputation of attention scores in the backward pass, and (2) resolving implicit precision assumptions in FA's gradient calculation. Based on these insights, we propose Attn-QAT and implement fused Triton kernels for training as well as FP4 inference kernels. Across diffusion and language models, Attn-QAT recovers the quality drop from FP4 attention without explicit outlier-mitigation heuristics used in prior FP4 attention, and delivers up to a 1.5x speedup on an RTX 5090. Video demos can be found at https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/190F6xbBDUF2kGQYIcXBt3ehSYij5jlim?usp=sharing.

</details>


### [197] [Econometric vs. Causal Structure-Learning for Time-Series Policy Decisions: Evidence from the UK COVID-19 Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00041)
*Bruno Petrungaro,Anthony C. Constantinou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究比较了计量经济学方法和传统因果机器学习算法在时间序列数据中的因果发现性能，使用英国COVID-19案例评估了4种计量方法和11种因果ML算法，并开发了将计量结果转换为bnlearn格式的代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然因果机器学习在横截面数据上取得了进展，但时间序列数据的因果结构恢复仍是研究重点。计量经济学在时间序列因果关系方面有长期积累，本研究旨在比较计量方法与因果ML算法的性能，探索是否可以将计量经济学的经验融入因果ML中。

Method: 使用英国COVID-19真实案例，评估了4种计量经济学方法和11种因果机器学习算法。从图形结构、模型维度和因果效应恢复能力三个维度进行比较，并开发了将计量方法结果转换为bnlearn库格式的代码。

Result: 计量经济学方法提供了清晰的时间结构规则，而因果ML算法通过探索更大的图结构空间实现了更广泛的发现，倾向于生成更密集的图，能够识别更多的因果关系。两种方法各有优势，计量方法在时间结构方面更明确，因果ML在发现潜在关系方面更全面。

Conclusion: 计量经济学方法可以为因果ML提供时间结构方面的有益经验，特别是在政策决策支持场景中。两种方法的结合可能产生更好的因果发现效果，开发的转换代码有助于促进两个领域的交叉融合。

Abstract: Causal machine learning (ML) recovers graphical structures that inform us about potential cause-and-effect relationships. Most progress has focused on cross-sectional data with no explicit time order, whereas recovering causal structures from time series data remains the subject of ongoing research in causal ML. In addition to traditional causal ML, this study assesses econometric methods that some argue can recover causal structures from time series data. The use of these methods can be explained by the significant attention the field of econometrics has given to causality, and specifically to time series, over the years. This presents the possibility of comparing the causal discovery performance between econometric and traditional causal ML algorithms. We seek to understand if there are lessons to be incorporated into causal ML from econometrics, and provide code to translate the results of these econometric methods to the most widely used Bayesian Network R library, bnlearn. We investigate the benefits and challenges that these algorithms present in supporting policy decision-making, using the real-world case of COVID-19 in the UK as an example. Four econometric methods are evaluated in terms of graphical structure, model dimensionality, and their ability to recover causal effects, and these results are compared with those of eleven causal ML algorithms. Amongst our main results, we see that econometric methods provide clear rules for temporal structures, whereas causal-ML algorithms offer broader discovery by exploring a larger space of graph structures that tends to lead to denser graphs that capture more identifiable causal relationships.

</details>


### [198] [Maximizing the Spectral Energy Gain in Sub-1-Bit LLMs via Latent Geometry Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00042)
*Banseok Lee,Youngmin Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LittleBit-2通过几何预对齐技术解决低秩二进制近似中的潜在几何错位问题，在亚1比特压缩领域实现SOTA性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究发现极端模型压缩中存在谱能量增益现象，但现有方法因潜在几何错位问题（标准奇异向量具有高相干性）而无法实现这一潜力，导致性能落后于现有1比特方法

Method: 提出LittleBit-2框架，采用内部潜在旋转和联合迭代量化作为几何预对齐器，将相干潜在分布与二进制超立方体对齐，且不增加推理开销

Result: 在Llama-2和Llama-3模型上，LittleBit-2在亚1比特压缩领域（1~0.1 bpp）建立了新的SOTA，匹配领先1比特基线的保真度

Conclusion: 通过解决潜在几何错位问题，LittleBit-2成功实现了谱能量增益，在极端模型压缩中超越了传统低秩浮点基线，为亚1比特压缩提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: We identify the Spectral Energy Gain in extreme model compression, where low-rank binary approximations outperform tiny-rank floating-point baselines for heavy-tailed spectra. However, prior attempts fail to realize this potential, trailing state-of-the-art 1-bit methods. We attribute this degradation to Latent Geometry Misalignment: standard singular vectors exhibit high coherence (spiky distribution), the worst-case geometry for binary quantization. To realize this gain, we propose LittleBit-2, a framework employing Internal Latent Rotation and Joint Iterative Quantization (Joint-ITQ). This approach acts as a geometric preconditioner, aligning coherent latent distributions with the binary hypercube with zero inference overhead. Empirically, LittleBit-2 establishes a new state-of-the-art in the sub-1-bit regime (1$\sim$0.1 bpp) on Llama-2 and Llama-3, matching the fidelity of leading 1-bit baselines.

</details>


### [199] [PARWiS: Winner determination under shoestring budgets using active pairwise comparisons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01171)
*Shailendra Bhandari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PARWiS算法在有限预算下通过主动成对比较识别最佳项目，其上下文变体和强化学习变体在合成和真实数据集上评估，结果显示PARWiS和RL PARWiS优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在偏好学习中，如何在有限预算下通过主动成对比较确定最佳项目是一个具有挑战性的问题。研究旨在实现和评估PARWiS算法，该算法结合谱排序和破坏性对选择策略，以在极低预算条件下识别最佳项目。

Method: 研究实现了PARWiS算法，并扩展了两种变体：上下文PARWiS（利用上下文特征）和强化学习PARWiS。这些方法在合成数据集和真实数据集（Jester和MovieLens）上进行评估，使用40、60和80次比较的预算处理20个项目。评估包括与Double Thompson Sampling和随机选择策略等基线的比较。

Result: 结果显示PARWiS和RL PARWiS在所有数据集上都优于基线方法，特别是在Δ₁,₂值较高的Jester数据集上表现更佳。在Δ₁,₂值较小的更具挑战性的MovieLens数据集上，性能差距缩小。上下文PARWiS与原始PARWiS性能相当，表明上下文特征可能需要进一步调优才能提供显著优势。

Conclusion: PARWiS算法及其强化学习变体在有限预算的主动成对比较任务中表现优异，特别是在项目间区分度较大的情况下。上下文特征的整合需要进一步优化以发挥其潜力，而强化学习方法展示了在复杂偏好学习场景中的适应性优势。

Abstract: Determining a winner among a set of items using active pairwise comparisons under a limited budget is a challenging problem in preference-based learning. The goal of this study is to implement and evaluate the PARWiS algorithm, which shows spectral ranking and disruptive pair selection to identify the best item under shoestring budgets. This work have extended the PARWiS with a contextual variant (Contextual PARWiS) and a reinforcement learning-based variant (RL PARWiS), comparing them against baselines, including Double Thompson Sampling and a random selection strategy. This evaluation spans synthetic and real-world datasets (Jester and MovieLens), using budgets of 40, 60, and 80 comparisons for 20 items. The performance is measured through recovery fraction, true rank of reported winner, reported rank of true winner, and cumulative regret, alongside the separation metric \(Δ_{1,2}\). Results show that PARWiS and RL PARWiS outperform baselines across all datasets, particularly in the Jester dataset with a higher \(Δ_{1,2}\), while performance gaps narrow in the more challenging MovieLens dataset with a smaller \(Δ_{1,2}\). Contextual PARWiS shows comparable performance to PARWiS, indicating that contextual features may require further tuning to provide significant benefits.

</details>


### [200] [Reinforcement Learning for Control with Probabilistic Stability Guarantee: A Finite-Sample Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00043)
*Minghao Han,Lixian Zhang,Chenliang Liu,Zhipeng Zhou,Jun Wang,Wei Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于有限数据的强化学习方法，利用李雅普诺夫方法提供概率稳定性保证，并开发了L-REINFORCE算法用于稳定策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决强化学习与控制理论之间的关键差距，在模型无关框架下使用有限数据进行稳定性分析和控制器设计，为控制系统的强化学习提供概率稳定性保证。

Method: 基于李雅普诺夫方法提出概率稳定性定理，仅使用有限数量的采样轨迹确保均方稳定性；推导稳定策略学习的策略梯度定理；开发L-REINFORCE算法，扩展经典REINFORCE算法以适应稳定化问题。

Result: 稳定性概率随轨迹数量和长度增加而提高，数据规模增大时收敛到确定性；在Cartpole任务仿真中，L-REINFORCE在确保稳定性方面优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 该工作成功弥合了强化学习与控制理论之间的关键差距，实现了在模型无关框架下使用有限数据进行稳定性分析和控制器设计，为强化学习在控制系统中的应用提供了理论保证。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel approach to reinforcement learning (RL) for control systems that provides probabilistic stability guarantees using finite data. Leveraging Lyapunov's method, we propose a probabilistic stability theorem that ensures mean square stability using only a finite number of sampled trajectories. The probability of stability increases with the number and length of trajectories, converging to certainty as data size grows. Additionally, we derive a policy gradient theorem for stabilizing policy learning and develop an RL algorithm, L-REINFORCE, that extends the classical REINFORCE algorithm to stabilization problems. The effectiveness of L-REINFORCE is demonstrated through simulations on a Cartpole task, where it outperforms the baseline in ensuring stability. This work bridges a critical gap between RL and control theory, enabling stability analysis and controller design in a model-free framework with finite data.

</details>


### [201] [Hereditary Geometric Meta-RL: Nonlocal Generalization via Task Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00396)
*Paul Nitschke,Shahriar Talebi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于几何视角的元强化学习方法，通过发现底层系统的对称性来构建任务空间的"遗传几何"，从而实现对任务空间更广泛的泛化，而不仅仅是基于平滑性的局部泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统元强化学习通过任务编码的平滑性进行泛化，这需要密集覆盖任务空间且无法利用任务空间更丰富的结构。作者希望开发一种能够发现底层系统对称性的方法，实现更广泛的任务空间泛化。

Method: 提出几何视角，在任务空间中引入由底层系统固有对称性诱导的"遗传几何"。通过李群作用转换状态和动作来重用训练时学习的策略，将元强化学习转化为对称性发现问题而非平滑外推。开发了微分对称性发现方法，通过塌缩函数不变性约束来提高数值稳定性和样本效率。

Result: 当任务空间继承自底层系统的对称性时，任务空间可以嵌入到那些具有线性化、连通和紧致性质的对称性子群中，这些性质使得测试时的学习和推理更加高效。在二维导航任务上的实验表明，该方法能有效恢复真实对称性并在整个任务空间泛化，而常见基线方法只能在训练任务附近泛化。

Conclusion: 通过对称性发现而非平滑外推的几何方法能够实现更广泛的任务空间泛化，利用底层系统的固有结构提高元强化学习的效率和泛化能力。

Abstract: Meta-Reinforcement Learning (Meta-RL) commonly generalizes via smoothness in the task encoding. While this enables local generalization around each training task, it requires dense coverage of the task space and leaves richer task space structure untapped. In response, we develop a geometric perspective that endows the task space with a "hereditary geometry" induced by the inherent symmetries of the underlying system. Concretely, the agent reuses a policy learned at the train time by transforming states and actions through actions of a Lie group. This converts Meta-RL into symmetry discovery rather than smooth extrapolation, enabling the agent to generalize to wider regions of the task space. We show that when the task space is inherited from the symmetries of the underlying system, the task space embeds into a subgroup of those symmetries whose actions are linearizable, connected, and compact--properties that enable efficient learning and inference at the test time. To learn these structures, we develop a differential symmetry discovery method. This collapses functional invariance constraints and thereby improves numerical stability and sample efficiency over functional approaches. Empirically, on a two-dimensional navigation task, our method efficiently recovers the ground-truth symmetry and generalizes across the entire task space, while a common baseline generalizes only near training tasks.

</details>


### [202] [REMIND: Rethinking Medical High-Modality Learning under Missingness--A Long-Tailed Distribution Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00046)
*Chenwei Wu,Zitao Shuai,Liyue Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: REMIND：针对高模态缺失场景的长尾视角多模态学习框架，通过组专业化混合专家架构和分布鲁棒优化解决梯度不一致和概念偏移问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床应用中，由于数据收集限制，患者往往无法获得完整的多模态数据，导致"高模态学习下的缺失"问题。这种缺失导致模态组合呈指数增长，并形成长尾分布。先前研究忽视了这一关键现象，导致尾部模态组合组的性能显著下降。

Method: 提出REMIND框架：1）组专业化混合专家架构，可扩展地学习任意模态组合的组特定多模态融合函数；2）组分布鲁棒优化策略，对代表性不足的模态组合进行加权。该框架从长尾视角重新思考高模态缺失下的多模态学习。

Result: 在真实世界医疗数据集上的广泛实验表明，该框架在多种医疗多模态学习应用中一致优于最先进方法，并在高模态缺失下具有鲁棒泛化能力。

Conclusion: 高模态缺失导致的长尾分布是医疗多模态学习的关键挑战，REMIND通过组专业化架构和分布鲁棒优化有效解决了梯度不一致和概念偏移问题，为高模态缺失场景提供了统一的解决方案。

Abstract: Medical multi-modal learning is critical for integrating information from a large set of diverse modalities. However, when leveraging a high number of modalities in real clinical applications, it is often impractical to obtain full-modality observations for every patient due to data collection constraints, a problem we refer to as 'High-Modality Learning under Missingness'. In this study, we identify that such missingness inherently induces an exponential growth in possible modality combinations, followed by long-tail distributions of modality combinations due to varying modality availability. While prior work overlooked this critical phenomenon, we find this long-tailed distribution leads to significant underperformance on tail modality combination groups. Our empirical analysis attributes this problem to two fundamental issues: 1) gradient inconsistency, where tail groups' gradient updates diverge from the overall optimization direction; 2) concept shifts, where each modality combination requires distinct fusion functions. To address these challenges, we propose REMIND, a unified framework that REthinks MultImodal learNing under high-moDality missingness from a long-tail perspective. Our core idea is to propose a novel group-specialized Mixture-of-Experts architecture that scalably learns group-specific multi-modal fusion functions for arbitrary modality combinations, while simultaneously leveraging a group distributionally robust optimization strategy to upweight underrepresented modality combinations. Extensive experiments on real-world medical datasets show that our framework consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, and robustly generalizes across various medical multi-modal learning applications under high-modality missingness.

</details>


### [203] [Mathematical Foundations of Poisoning Attacks on Linear Regression over Cumulative Distribution Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00537)
*Atsuki Sato,Martin Aumüller,Yusuke Matsui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对针对CDF线性回归模型的投毒攻击进行理论分析，证明了单点攻击的最优性，发现多点贪婪攻击并非总是最优，并提出计算攻击影响上界的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习索引通过机器学习模型近似CDF实现快速搜索，但易受投毒攻击影响。现有研究显示少量投毒键值即可显著降低模型精度和索引性能，需要对这些攻击进行深入理论分析。

Method: 针对CDF线性回归模型进行投毒攻击的理论分析：1) 证明单点攻击的最优性；2) 分析多点攻击中贪婪方法的局限性；3) 推导多点攻击影响的上界计算方法。

Result: 1) 理论证明单点攻击的最优性，现有方法确实是最优攻击；2) 发现多点攻击中贪婪方法并非总是最优，推导了最优攻击应满足的关键特性；3) 提出计算多点攻击影响上界的方法，经验证明贪婪方法的损失常接近该上界。

Conclusion: 研究深化了对CDF线性回归模型攻击策略的理论理解，为学习索引的攻击与防御理论评估提供了基础。单点攻击已最优，多点攻击仍有优化空间，但贪婪方法通常接近最优。

Abstract: Learned indexes are a class of index data structures that enable fast search by approximating the cumulative distribution function (CDF) using machine learning models (Kraska et al., SIGMOD'18). However, recent studies have shown that learned indexes are vulnerable to poisoning attacks, where injecting a small number of poison keys into the training data can significantly degrade model accuracy and reduce index performance (Kornaropoulos et al., SIGMOD'22). In this work, we provide a rigorous theoretical analysis of poisoning attacks targeting linear regression models over CDFs, one of the most basic regression models and a core component in many learned indexes. Our main contributions are as follows: (i) We present a theoretical proof characterizing the optimal single-point poisoning attack and show that the existing method yields the optimal attack. (ii) We show that in multi-point attacks, the existing greedy approach is not always optimal, and we rigorously derive the key properties that an optimal attack should satisfy. (iii) We propose a method to compute an upper bound of the multi-point poisoning attack's impact and empirically demonstrate that the loss under the greedy approach is often close to this bound. Our study deepens the theoretical understanding of attack strategies against linear regression models on CDFs and provides a foundation for the theoretical evaluation of attacks and defenses on learned indexes.

</details>


### [204] [BiJEPA: Bi-directional Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture for Symmetric Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00049)
*Yongchao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BiJEPA是一种双向联合嵌入预测架构，通过循环一致性预测和规范正则化解决标准JEPA单向预测的局限性，在多种模态数据上实现稳定收敛和鲁棒表示学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准JEPA模型采用单向预测机制（如上下文→目标），可能忽略了反向关系中的信息信号，从而限制了性能表现。需要开发更全面的表示学习方法。

Method: 提出BiJEPA（双向联合嵌入预测架构），通过强制数据片段间的循环一致可预测性来增强表示学习。为解决对称预测固有的不稳定性（表示爆炸），引入了对表示向量的关键规范正则化机制。

Result: 在三种不同模态上评估：合成周期信号、混沌洛伦兹吸引子轨迹和高维图像数据（MNIST）。BiJEPA实现了稳定收敛而不崩溃，捕获了混沌系统的语义结构，并学习了能够生成和泛化的鲁棒时空表示。

Conclusion: BiJEPA通过双向预测和规范正则化，为表示学习提供了更全面的方法，在多种数据模态上表现出优越性能，解决了标准JEPA的局限性。

Abstract: Self-Supervised Learning (SSL) has shifted from pixel-level reconstruction to latent space prediction, spearheaded by the Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA). While effective, standard JEPA models typically rely on a uni-directional prediction mechanism (e.g. Context $\to$ Target), potentially neglecting the informative signal inherent in the inverse relationship, degrading its performance. In this work, we propose \textbf{BiJEPA}, a \textit{Bi-Directional Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture} that enforces cycle-consistent predictability between data segments. We address the inherent instability of symmetric prediction (representation explosion) by introducing a critical norm regularization mechanism on the representation vectors. We evaluate BiJEPA on three distinct modalities: synthetic periodic signals, chaotic Lorenz attractor trajectories, and high-dimensional image data (MNIST). Our results demonstrate that BiJEPA achieves stable convergence without collapse, captures the semantic structure of chaotic systems, and learns robust temporal and spatial representations capable of generation and generalisation, offering a more holistic approach to representation learning.

</details>


### [205] [Mag-Mamba: Modeling Coupled spatiotemporal Asymmetry for POI Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00053)
*Zhuoxuan Li,Tangwei Ye,Jieyuan Pei,Haina Liang,Zhongyuan Lai,Zihan Liu,Yiming Wu,Qi Zhang,Liang Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Mag-Mamba框架通过将时空不对称性建模为复数域中的相位驱动旋转动力学，解决了POI推荐中的时空不对称挑战，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有POI推荐方法面临时空不对称性的根本挑战：位置间的转移意图具有高度不对称性且随时间动态变化。传统基于图或序列的方法使用对称算子或实值聚合，难以统一建模时间变化的全局方向性。

Method: 提出Mag-Mamba框架：1) 时间条件磁相位编码器，在地理邻接图上构建时间条件磁拉普拉斯算子，利用边相位差表征全局演化的空间方向性；2) 复数域Mamba模块，将传统标量状态衰减推广为联合衰减-旋转动力学，由时间间隔和磁地理先验共同调制。

Result: 在三个真实世界数据集上的广泛实验表明，Mag-Mamba相比最先进的基线方法取得了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过将时空不对称性建模为复数域中的相位驱动旋转动力学，Mag-Mamba有效解决了POI推荐中的时空不对称挑战，为城市移动性建模提供了新视角。

Abstract: Next Point-of-Interest (POI) recommendation is a critical task in location-based services, yet it faces the fundamental challenge of coupled spatiotemporal asymmetry inherent in urban mobility. Specifically, transition intents between locations exhibit high asymmetry and are dynamically conditioned on time. Existing methods, typically built on graph or sequence backbones, rely on symmetric operators or real-valued aggregations, struggling to unify the modeling of time-varying global directionality. To address this limitation, we propose Mag-Mamba, a framework whose core insight lies in modeling spatiotemporal asymmetry as phase-driven rotational dynamics in the complex domain. Based on this, we first devise a Time-conditioned Magnetic Phase Encoder that constructs a time-conditioned Magnetic Laplacian on the geographic adjacency graph, utilizing edge phase differences to characterize the globally evolving spatial directionality. Subsequently, we introduce a Complex-valued Mamba module that generalizes traditional scalar state decay into joint decay-rotation dynamics, explicitly modulated by both time intervals and magnetic geographic priors. Extensive experiments on three real-world datasets demonstrate that Mag-Mamba achieves significant performance improvements over state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [206] [Intent-Context Synergy Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous UAV Decision-Making in Air Combat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00974)
*Jiahao Fu,Feng Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ICS-RL框架，通过意图预测和上下文协同机制解决无人机在动态对抗环境中渗透的挑战，显著提升任务成功率并降低暴露频率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主无人机在动态对抗环境中的渗透面临重大挑战，主要源于威胁的部分可观测性以及任务效率与生存能力之间的冲突目标。传统强化学习方法存在短视决策问题，难以实时平衡这些权衡。

Method: 提出意图-上下文协同强化学习(ICS-RL)框架，包含两个核心创新：1) LSTM意图预测模块预测敌对单位未来轨迹，通过状态增强实现从反应式避让到主动规划的决策范式转变；2) 上下文分析协同机制将任务分解为分层子任务(安全巡航、隐身规划、敌对突破)。设计基于Dueling DQN的异构集成智能体，每个智能体专精特定战术上下文，通过基于最大优势值的动态切换控制器无缝集成这些智能体。

Result: 广泛仿真表明，ICS-RL显著优于基线方法(标准DDQN)和传统方法(PSO、博弈论)。该方法达到88%的任务成功率，并将平均暴露频率降低至每回合0.24次，验证了其在高度动态场景中确保稳健隐身渗透的优越性。

Conclusion: ICS-RL框架通过意图预测和上下文协同机制有效解决了无人机在动态对抗环境中的渗透挑战，实现了从反应式避让到主动规划的决策范式转变，显著提升了任务成功率和隐身性能。

Abstract: Autonomous UAV infiltration in dynamic contested environments remains a significant challenge due to the partially observable nature of threats and the conflicting objectives of mission efficiency versus survivability. Traditional Reinforcement Learning (RL) approaches often suffer from myopic decision-making and struggle to balance these trade-offs in real-time. To address these limitations, this paper proposes an Intent-Context Synergy Reinforcement Learning (ICS-RL) framework. The framework introduces two core innovations: (1) An LSTM-based Intent Prediction Module that forecasts the future trajectories of hostile units, transforming the decision paradigm from reactive avoidance to proactive planning via state augmentation; (2) A Context-Analysis Synergy Mechanism that decomposes the mission into hierarchical sub-tasks (safe cruise, stealth planning, and hostile breakthrough). We design a heterogeneous ensemble of Dueling DQN agents, each specialized in a specific tactical context. A dynamic switching controller based on Max-Advantage values seamlessly integrates these agents, allowing the UAV to adaptively select the optimal policy without hard-coded rules. Extensive simulations demonstrate that ICS-RL significantly outperforms baselines (Standard DDQN) and traditional methods (PSO, Game Theory). The proposed method achieves a mission success rate of 88\% and reduces the average exposure frequency to 0.24 per episode, validating its superiority in ensuring robust and stealthy penetration in high-dynamic scenarios.

</details>


### [207] [Expert Divergence Learning for MoE-based Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00054)
*Jiaang Li,Haibin Chen,Langming Liu,Yujin Yuan,Yadao Wang,Yizhen Zhang,Chengting Yu,Xin Tong,Weidong Zhang,Shilei Liu,Wenbo Su,Bo Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Expert Divergence Learning方法，通过标签驱动的辅助损失函数，利用预训练语料中的领域标签最大化不同数据领域间专家路由分布的Jensen-Shannon散度，以解决MoE架构中的专家同质化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合专家(MoE)架构在扩展语言模型方面很强大，但常面临专家同质化问题，即专家学习冗余功能，限制了MoE的潜力。需要解决专家功能冗余问题以充分发挥MoE架构的优势。

Method: 提出Expert Divergence Learning预训练策略，通过标签驱动的辅助损失函数，利用预训练语料中的领域标签，最大化不同数据领域间专家路由分布的Jensen-Shannon散度。该方法引导模型为不同领域开发差异化的路由策略，为相同领域开发相似的路由策略，从而促进专家功能专业化。

Result: 从零开始预训练了高达150亿参数的MoE模型。实验表明，使用Expert Divergence Learning训练的模型不仅获得更低的语言建模损失，还在多种下游基准测试中表现出显著性能提升。分析证实该方法有效缓解了专家同质化，带来更大的功能专业化，且训练期间计算开销可忽略。

Conclusion: Expert Divergence Learning是一种有效的预训练策略，能够解决MoE架构中的专家同质化问题，促进专家功能专业化，显著提升模型性能，且计算开销极小。

Abstract: The Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture is a powerful technique for scaling language models, yet it often suffers from expert homogenization, where experts learn redundant functionalities, thereby limiting MoE's full potential. To address this, we introduce Expert Divergence Learning, a novel pre-training strategy that explicitly encourages functional specialization among experts. Our method incorporates a label-driven auxiliary loss that leverages domain labels inherent in pre-training corpora to maximize the Jensen-Shannon Divergence between the expert routing distributions of different data domains. This optimization objective guides the model to develop diverged routing policies for varied domains and closer routing policies for the same domain, which leads to emergent and organized expert specialization. We validate our approach by pre-training MoE models of up to 15 billion parameters from scratch. Experimental results demonstrate that models trained with Expert Divergence Learning not only achieve a lower language modeling loss but also exhibit significant performance improvements across a diverse range of downstream benchmarks. Further analysis confirms that our method effectively mitigates expert homogenization and brings greater functional specialization, all with negligible computational overhead during training.

</details>


### [208] [Integrating LTL Constraints into PPO for Safe Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01292)
*Maifang Zhang,Hang Yu,Qian Zuo,Cheng Wang,Vaishak Belle,Fengxiang He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PPO-LTL框架将线性时序逻辑约束集成到PPO中，通过自动机监控安全违规并转换为惩罚信号，在保持性能的同时显著减少安全违规


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人等领域存在复杂的安全规范需要严格遵守，传统强化学习方法难以系统性地处理这些复杂的安全约束，需要一种能够将形式化安全要求集成到策略优化中的框架

Method: 提出PPO-LTL框架：1) 使用LTL表达复杂安全约束；2) 通过极限确定性Büchi自动机监控LTL约束违规；3) 逻辑到成本机制将违规转换为惩罚信号；4) 采用拉格朗日方案指导策略优化

Result: 在Zones和CARLA环境中的大量实验表明，PPO-LTL能够持续减少安全违规，同时保持与最先进方法竞争的性能表现

Conclusion: PPO-LTL成功地将LTL形式化安全约束集成到PPO框架中，提供了一种系统性的安全强化学习方法，能够在复杂环境中有效减少安全违规而不牺牲性能

Abstract: This paper proposes Proximal Policy Optimization with Linear Temporal Logic Constraints (PPO-LTL), a framework that integrates safety constraints written in LTL into PPO for safe reinforcement learning. LTL constraints offer rigorous representations of complex safety requirements, such as regulations that broadly exist in robotics, enabling systematic monitoring of safety requirements. Violations against LTL constraints are monitored by limit-deterministic Büchi automata, and then translated by a logic-to-cost mechanism into penalty signals. The signals are further employed for guiding the policy optimization via the Lagrangian scheme. Extensive experiments on the Zones and CARLA environments show that our PPO-LTL can consistently reduce safety violations, while maintaining competitive performance, against the state-of-the-art methods. The code is at https://github.com/EVIEHub/PPO-LTL.

</details>


### [209] [M3-AD: Reflection-aware Multi-modal, Multi-category, and Multi-dimensional Benchmark and Framework for Industrial Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00055)
*Chao Huang,Yanhui Li,Yunkang Cao,Wei Wang,Hongxi Huang,Jie Wen,Wenqi Ren,Xiaochun Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: M3-AD是一个用于工业异常检测的反射感知多模态框架，通过RA-Monitor组件实现可学习的决策修正过程，提升模型在复杂工业场景中的决策鲁棒性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型在工业异常检测中倾向于产生高置信度但不可靠的决策，特别是在细粒度和结构复杂的工业场景中，且缺乏有效的自我纠正机制

Method: 提出M3-AD框架，包含两个互补数据资源：用于反射对齐微调的M3-AD-FT和用于系统跨类别评估的M3-AD-Bench；在此基础上提出RA-Monitor，将反射建模为可学习的决策修正过程，引导模型在初始判断不可靠时进行受控自我纠正

Result: 在M3-AD-Bench上的大量实验表明，RA-Monitor在零样本异常检测和异常分析任务中优于多个开源和商业MLLM

Conclusion: M3-AD通过反射感知学习框架有效解决了工业异常检测中模型决策不可靠的问题，RA-Monitor的决策修正机制显著提升了模型在复杂工业场景中的鲁棒性

Abstract: Although multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have advanced industrial anomaly detection toward a zero-shot paradigm, they still tend to produce high-confidence yet unreliable decisions in fine-grained and structurally complex industrial scenarios, and lack effective self-corrective mechanisms. To address this issue, we propose M3-AD, a unified reflection-aware multimodal framework for industrial anomaly detection. M3-AD comprises two complementary data resources: M3-AD-FT, designed for reflection-aligned fine-tuning, and M3-AD-Bench, designed for systematic cross-category evaluation, together providing a foundation for reflection-aware learning and reliability assessment. Building upon this foundation, we propose RA-Monitor, which models reflection as a learnable decision revision process and guides models to perform controlled self-correction when initial judgments are unreliable, thereby improving decision robustness. Extensive experiments conducted on M3-AD-Bench demonstrate that RA-Monitor outperforms multiple open-source and commercial MLLMs in zero-shot anomaly detection and anomaly analysis tasks. Code will be released at https://github.com/Yanhui-Lee/M3-AD.

</details>


### [210] [Align and Filter: Improving Performance in Asynchronous On-Policy RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01365)
*Homayoun Honari,Roger Creus Castanyer,Michael Przystupa,Michael Noukhovitch,Pablo Samuel Castro,Glen Berseth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TV-ACO方法解决分布式训练和高更新频率导致的策略滞后问题，提升强化学习算法的可扩展性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式训练和增加梯度更新频率是加速学习和提升性能的实用策略，但都会加剧策略滞后问题——即生成数据的行为策略与正在更新的学习策略之间的不匹配。策略滞后会阻碍在策略学习算法向更大规模问题的扩展。

Method: 提出总变差优势对齐约束策略优化(TV-ACO)方法，基于对分布式学习和高更新频率导致策略滞后来源的分析，设计约束策略优化来缓解策略滞后问题。

Result: 经验验证表明该方法在经典RL任务和现代RL用于LLM数学推理任务中，对策略滞后具有更好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: TV-ACO方法能有效缓解分布式训练和高更新频率引起的策略滞后问题，提升强化学习算法的可扩展性和性能。

Abstract: Distributed training and increasing the gradient update frequency are practical strategies to accelerate learning and improve performance, but both exacerbate a central challenge: \textit{policy lag}, which is the mismatch between the behavior policy generating data and the learning policy being updated. Policy lag can hinder the scaling of on-policy learning algorithms to larger problems. In this paper, we identify the sources of policy lag caused by distributed learning and high update frequency. We use the findings to propose \textit{total Variation-based Advantage aligned Constrained policy Optimization (\methodacronym)} as a practical approach to mitigate policy lag. We empirically validate our method and show that it offers better robustness to policy lag in classic RL tasks and a modern RL for LLM math reasoning task.

</details>


### [211] [Certainty-Validity: A Diagnostic Framework for Discrete Commitment Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00070)
*Datorien L. Anderson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了确定性-有效性（CVS）框架，用于诊断离散承诺系统的模型性能，揭示了标准准确率指标无法检测的"自信错误"行为，并分析了83%模糊性天花板现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准机器学习评估指标（准确率、精确率、召回率、AUROC）假设所有错误都是等价的，但对于离散承诺系统（选择承诺状态{-W, 0, +W}的架构），这种假设存在认识论缺陷。需要新的评估框架来区分模型在模糊数据上的不同行为模式。

Method: 引入确定性-有效性（CVS）框架，将模型性能分解为2x2矩阵，区分高/低确定性和有效/无效预测。通过Fashion-MNIST、EMNIST和IMDB上的消融实验，分析离散架构在噪声基准测试中出现的"83%模糊性天花板"现象。

Result: CVS框架揭示了标准准确率隐藏的关键失败模式：自信错误（CI）行为，即模型在模糊数据中幻觉出结构。离散架构在噪声基准测试中达到83%准确率后停滞不前，而连续模型可以通过记忆纹理或统计噪声超越这个天花板。离散模型拒绝承诺模糊样本，这不是失败而是特征：模型在结构证据结束处停止。

Conclusion: 在模糊数据上的标准训练最终会导致良性过拟合，导致从不确定错误（适当怀疑）到自信错误（幻觉）的病态迁移。推理系统的"良好训练"不应由准确率定义，而应通过最大化确定性-有效性分数（CVS）来确保模型知道何时停止。

Abstract: Standard evaluation metrics for machine learning -- accuracy, precision, recall, and AUROC -- assume that all errors are equivalent: a confident incorrect prediction is penalized identically to an uncertain one. For discrete commitment systems (architectures that select committed states {-W, 0, +W}), this assumption is epistemologically flawed. We introduce the Certainty-Validity (CVS) Framework, a diagnostic method that decomposes model performance into a 2x2 matrix distinguishing high/low certainty from valid/invalid predictions. This framework reveals a critical failure mode hidden by standard accuracy: Confident-Incorrect (CI) behavior, where models hallucinate structure in ambiguous data. Through ablation experiments on Fashion-MNIST, EMNIST, and IMDB, we analyze the "83% Ambiguity Ceiling" -- a stopping point where this specific discrete architecture consistently plateaus on noisy benchmarks. Unlike continuous models that can surpass this ceiling by memorizing texture or statistical noise, the discrete model refuses to commit to ambiguous samples. We show that this refusal is not a failure but a feature: the model stops where structural evidence ends. However, standard training on ambiguous data eventually forces Benign Overfitting, causing a pathological migration from Uncertain-Incorrect (appropriate doubt) to Confident-Incorrect (hallucination). We propose that "good training" for reasoning systems must be defined not by accuracy, but by maximizing the Certainty-Validity Score (CVS) -- ensuring the model knows where to stop.

</details>


### [212] [LIDS: LLM Summary Inference Under the Layered Lens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00105)
*Dylan Park,Yingying Fan,Jinchi Lv*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LIDS方法，结合BERT-SVD方向度量和SOFARI关键词提取，用于评估LLM生成摘要的质量和可解释性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着ChatGPT等大语言模型在摘要生成方面的广泛应用，评估生成摘要的质量变得具有挑战性，需要更有效的评估方法

Method: 提出LIDS方法：1) 使用BERT-SVD方向度量计算摘要与原文的相似度；2) 利用重复提示量化统计不确定性；3) 采用SOFARI方法提取分层主题的关键词，并控制错误发现率

Result: LIDS能够自然嵌入每个摘要实现大规模文本降维，通过综合实证研究验证了方法的实用性和鲁棒性，包括人工验证和与其他相似度度量的比较

Conclusion: LIDS为LLM生成摘要提供了有效的质量评估框架，结合了相似度度量和可解释的关键词分析，具有实际应用价值

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have gained significant attention by many researchers and practitioners in natural language processing (NLP) since the introduction of ChatGPT in 2022. One notable feature of ChatGPT is its ability to generate summaries based on prompts. Yet evaluating the quality of these summaries remains challenging due to the complexity of language. To this end, in this paper we suggest a new method of LLM summary inference with BERT-SVD-based direction metric and SOFARI (LIDS) that assesses the summary accuracy equipped with interpretable key words for layered themes. The LIDS uses a latent SVD-based direction metric to measure the similarity between the summaries and original text, leveraging the BERT embeddings and repeated prompts to quantify the statistical uncertainty. As a result, LIDS gives a natural embedding of each summary for large text reduction. We further exploit SOFARI to uncover important key words associated with each latent theme in the summary with controlled false discovery rate (FDR). Comprehensive empirical studies demonstrate the practical utility and robustness of LIDS through human verification and comparisons to other similarity metrics, including a comparison of different LLMs.

</details>


### [213] [MAML-KT: Addressing Cold Start Problem in Knowledge Tracing for New Students via Few-Shot Model-Agnostic Meta Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00137)
*Indronil Bhattacharjee,Christabel Wayllace*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出MAML-KT方法，通过元学习优化知识追踪模型的初始化参数，以解决新学生冷启动问题，在早期交互中显著提升预测准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统知识追踪模型评估方法忽略了实际部署中的冷启动场景，即模型需要从新学生的少量初始交互中推断其知识状态。现有模型如DKT、DKVMN和SAKT在这种冷启动设置下早期准确性显著下降，需要专门解决新学生快速适应的问题。

Method: 将新学生性能预测框架化为少样本学习问题，提出MAML-KT方法——一种模型无关的元学习方法。该方法学习一个优化的初始化参数，能够通过1-2次梯度更新快速适应新学生。在ASSIST2009、2015、2017数据集上使用受控冷启动协议进行评估，训练集使用学生子集，测试集使用保留学生，评估早期交互窗口（问题3-10和11-15），队列规模从10到50名学生。

Result: 在所有数据集上，MAML-KT在几乎所有冷启动条件下都获得了比先前KT模型更高的早期准确性，且随着队列规模增加，优势持续存在。在ASSIST2017上观察到早期性能的短暂下降，这与许多学生遇到先前未见技能的时间点重合，分析表明这种下降与技能新颖性而非模型不稳定性相关。

Conclusion: 优化知识追踪模型以实现快速适应能够减少新学生的早期预测误差，并为解释早期准确性波动提供了更清晰的视角，能够区分模型限制与真实的学习和知识获取动态。MAML-KT方法有效解决了知识追踪中的冷启动问题。

Abstract: Knowledge tracing (KT) models are commonly evaluated by training on early interactions from all students and testing on later responses. While effective for measuring average predictive performance, this evaluation design obscures a cold start scenario that arises in deployment, where models must infer the knowledge state of previously unseen students from only a few initial interactions. Prior studies have shown that under this setting, standard empirically risk-minimized KT models such as DKT, DKVMN and SAKT exhibit substantially lower early accuracy than previously reported. We frame new-student performance prediction as a few-shot learning problem and introduce MAML-KT, a model-agnostic meta learning approach that learns an initialization optimized for rapid adaptation to new students using one or two gradient updates. We evaluate MAML-KT on ASSIST2009, ASSIST2015 and ASSIST2017 using a controlled cold start protocol that trains on a subset of students and tests on held-out learners across early interaction windows (questions 3-10 and 11-15), scaling cohort sizes from 10 to 50 students. Across datasets, MAML-KT achieves higher early accuracy than prior KT models in nearly all cold start conditions, with gains persisting as cohort size increases. On ASSIST2017, we observe a transient drop in early performance that coincides with many students encountering previously unseen skills. Further analysis suggests that these drops coincide with skill novelty rather than model instability, consistent with prior work on skill-level cold start. Overall, optimizing KT models for rapid adaptation reduces early prediction error for new students and provides a clearer lens for interpreting early accuracy fluctuations, distinguishing model limitations from genuine learning and knowledge acquisition dynamics.

</details>


### [214] [Bridging Policy and Real-World Dynamics: LLM-Augmented Rebalancing for Shared Micromobility Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00176)
*Heng Tan,Hua Yan,Yu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AMPLIFY是一个基于LLM增强的策略自适应框架，用于共享微出行车辆再平衡，通过结合基线再平衡模块和LLM自适应模块，在突发事件下实时调整策略，提高需求满足率和系统收入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共享微出行车辆再平衡方法要么基于平均需求模式优化，要么使用鲁棒优化和强化学习处理预定义不确定性，但忽略了突发事件（如需求激增、车辆故障、监管干预）或在正常条件下牺牲性能。

Method: 提出AMPLIFY框架，包含基线再平衡模块和LLM自适应模块。自适应模块接收系统上下文、需求预测和基线策略，通过自我反思机制实时调整策略以应对突发事件。

Result: 在芝加哥真实电动滑板车数据上的评估表明，该方法相比基线策略提高了需求满足率和系统收入，证明了LLM驱动自适应在管理微出行系统不确定性方面的潜力。

Conclusion: LLM增强的自适应框架为共享微出行车辆再平衡提供了一种灵活解决方案，能够有效应对突发事件，平衡正常条件和异常情况下的性能表现。

Abstract: Shared micromobility services such as e-scooters and bikes have become an integral part of urban transportation, yet their efficiency critically depends on effective vehicle rebalancing. Existing methods either optimize for average demand patterns or employ robust optimization and reinforcement learning to handle predefined uncertainties. However, these approaches overlook emergent events (e.g., demand surges, vehicle outages, regulatory interventions) or sacrifice performance in normal conditions. We introduce AMPLIFY, an LLM-augmented policy adaptation framework for shared micromobility rebalancing. The framework combines a baseline rebalancing module with an LLM-based adaptation module that adjusts strategies in real time under emergent scenarios. The adaptation module ingests system context, demand predictions, and baseline strategies, and refines adjustments through self-reflection. Evaluations on real-world e-scooter data from Chicago show that our approach improves demand satisfaction and system revenue compared to baseline policies, highlighting the potential of LLM-driven adaptation as a flexible solution for managing uncertainty in micromobility systems.

</details>


### [215] [Engineering FAIR Privacy-preserving Applications that Learn Histories of Disease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00181)
*Ines N. Duarte,Praphulla M. S. Bhawsar,Lee K. Mason,Jeya Balaji Balasubramanian,Daniel E. Russ,Arlindo L. Oliveira,Jonas S. Almeida*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文开发了一个基于浏览器的生成式AI医疗应用，通过客户端推理实现隐私保护，验证了FAIR数据原则中的可重用性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在隐私敏感领域（如医疗）部署用户端生成式AI应用面临工程挑战，特别是数据隐私问题限制了这些模型在个性化医疗任务（如预测个体发病风险）中的应用

Method: 设计为浏览器内模型部署练习（"App"），测试客户端推理生成的架构边界（无需下载或安装）。基于参考报告文档开发模型，专门测试FAIR数据原则中的"R"（可重用性）组件。使用ONNX和自定义JavaScript SDK实现模型部署

Result: 成功部署了模型，建立了一个安全、高性能的架构蓝图，为未来医疗领域的私有生成式AI应用提供了可行方案

Conclusion: 该项目展示了在隐私敏感医疗领域部署客户端生成式AI应用的可行性，通过浏览器内推理架构解决了数据隐私问题，为未来医疗AI应用提供了安全、高性能的部署方案

Abstract: A recent report on "Learning the natural history of human disease with generative transformers" created an opportunity to assess the engineering challenge of delivering user-facing Generative AI applications in privacy-sensitive domains. The application of these models, particularly for personalized healthcare tasks like predicting individual morbidity risk, is typically constrained by data privacy concerns. This project was accordingly designed as an in-browser model deployment exercise (an "App") testing the architectural boundaries of client-side inference generation (no downloads or installations). We relied exclusively on the documentation provided in the reference report to develop the model, specifically testing the "R" component of the FAIR data principles: Findability, Accessibility, Interoperability, and Reusability. The successful model deployment, leveraging ONNX and a custom JavaScript SDK, establishes a secure, high-performance architectural blueprint for the future of private generative AI in medicine.

</details>


### [216] [OSF: On Pre-training and Scaling of Sleep Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00190)
*Zitao Shuai,Zongzhe Xu,David Yang,Wei Wang,Yuzhe Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SleepBench：首个全面开源睡眠生理学基础模型基准，通过大规模数据（166,500小时）和系统评估，揭示了通道不变性特征学习的重要性，并提出OSF模型家族在多项任务上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多导睡眠图（PSG）作为睡眠评估的金标准存在设备间和队列间的显著异质性。现有睡眠生理学基础模型缺乏对预训练过程和扩展模式的深入理解，导致泛化能力有限。

Method: 收集9个公共来源的166,500小时睡眠记录构建SleepBench基准，系统评估4类自监督预训练目标，发现通道不变性特征学习的关键作用，提出增强的预训练和扩展方案，开发OSF模型家族。

Result: 发现三个关键结论：1）现有FM无法泛化到推理时的缺失通道；2）通道不变性特征学习对预训练至关重要；3）扩展样本量、模型容量和多源数据混合能持续提升下游性能。OSF在9个数据集上达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: SleepBench为睡眠生理学基础模型提供了首个全面开源基准，揭示了通道不变性学习和扩展策略的重要性。OSF模型家族在样本效率、层次聚合和跨数据集扩展方面展现出有前景的特性，为通用睡眠FM的发展奠定了基础。

Abstract: Polysomnography (PSG) provides the gold standard for sleep assessment but suffers from substantial heterogeneity across recording devices and cohorts. There have been growing efforts to build general-purpose foundation models (FMs) for sleep physiology, but lack an in-depth understanding of the pre-training process and scaling patterns that lead to more generalizable sleep FMs. To fill this gap, we curate a massive corpus of 166,500 hours of sleep recordings from nine public sources and establish SleepBench, a comprehensive, fully open-source benchmark. Leveraging SleepBench, we systematically evaluate four families of self-supervised pre-training objectives and uncover three critical findings: (1) existing FMs fail to generalize to missing channels at inference; (2) channel-invariant feature learning is essential for pre-training; and (3) scaling sample size, model capacity, and multi-source data mixture consistently improves downstream performance.With an enhanced pre-training and scaling recipe, we introduce OSF, a family of sleep FMs that achieves state-of-the-art performance across nine datasets on diverse sleep and disease prediction tasks. Further analysis of OSF also reveals intriguing properties in sample efficiency, hierarchical aggregation, and cross-dataset scaling.

</details>


### [217] [A medical coding language model trained on clinical narratives from a population-wide cohort of 1.8 million patients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00221)
*Joakim Edin,Sedrah Butt Balaganeshan,Annike Kjølby Kristensen,Lars Maaløe,Ioannis Louloudis,Søren Brunak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 基于580万份电子健康记录训练的语言模型能预测ICD-10编码，在27万测试患者中达到71.8%的微F1分数和95.5%的top-10召回率，发现次要诊断存在系统性漏编码问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗编码将临床文档转化为标准化编码用于计费、研究和公共卫生，但人工编码耗时且易错。现有自动化方法依赖的小数据集难以代表真实世界的患者异质性。

Method: 使用丹麦东部2006-2016年间180万患者、580万份电子健康记录（涵盖几乎所有专科）训练语言模型，从临床记录、药物和实验室结果预测ICD-10编码。

Result: 在27万测试患者中，模型达到71.8%的微F1分数和95.5%的top-10召回率。性能因专科而异（F1: 53-91%），诊断标准明确的专科得分更高。主要作为次要诊断的编码F1分数显著较低。对三个此类编码（自杀相关行为、体重障碍、高血压）的验证显示，模型识别的数千个未编码病例中76-86%经人工审查确认为有效，表明存在系统性漏编码而非模型错误。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了丹麦东部该时期次要诊断的系统性漏编码问题，对流行病学研究、公共卫生监测和多病共存理解有重要影响。类似时间限制和报销结构的其他医疗系统可能也存在此问题。该模型可自动化约50%病例的编码，并为大多数其他病例提供准确建议，有助于捕获遗漏的次要病症。

Abstract: Medical coding translates clinical documentation into standardized codes for billing, research, and public health, but manual coding is time-consuming and error-prone. Existing automation efforts rely on small datasets that poorly represent real-world patient heterogeneity. We trained a language model on 5.8 million electronic health records from 1.8 million patients across nearly all specialties in Eastern Denmark (2006--2016) to predict ICD-10 codes from clinical notes, medications, and laboratory results. Evaluated on 270,000 held-out patients, the model achieved a micro F1 of 71.8% and a top-10 recall of 95.5%. Performance varied by specialty (F1: 53--91%), with higher scores in specialties with well-defined diagnostic criteria. Codes appearing predominantly as secondary diagnoses had markedly lower F1 scores. For three such codes (suicide-related behaviors, weight disorders, and hypertension), the model identified thousands of uncoded cases, of which 76-86% were confirmed valid upon manual review, suggesting systematic under-coding rather than model error. These findings suggest under-coding of secondary diagnoses in Eastern Denmark during this period, with potential implications for epidemiological research, public health surveillance, and understanding of multimorbidity. Similar time constraints and reimbursement structures in other healthcare systems suggest this may not be isolated to this dataset. The model can automate coding for approximately 50% of cases and provide accurate suggestions for most others, and may offer a practical solution to help capture missed secondary conditions.

</details>


### [218] [CoPeP: Benchmarking Continual Pretraining for Protein Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00253)
*Darshan Patil,Pranshu Malviya,Mathieu Reymond,Quentin Fournier,Sarath Chandar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoPeP基准：用于评估蛋白质语言模型持续学习的首个基准，基于十年UniProt数据，包含31个蛋白质理解任务，显示时序元信息可提升7%困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质数据库持续更新且具有动态特性，这为应用持续学习提供了机会：不仅能跟上数据增长，还能利用时序元信息提升模型性能。

Method: 创建CoPeP基准，从UniProt知识库中整理跨越十年的蛋白质数据集序列，定义31个蛋白质理解任务的评估指标，评估包括回放、遗忘和可塑性方法在内的多种持续学习方法。

Result: 使用时序元信息可将困惑度提升高达7%（相比所有任务联合训练）；多个持续学习方法在大规模场景下仍优于朴素持续预训练。

Conclusion: CoPeP基准为在大规模真实应用中研究持续学习方法提供了重要平台，证明时序元信息和持续学习能显著提升蛋白质语言模型性能。

Abstract: Protein language models (pLMs) have recently gained significant attention for their ability to uncover relationships between sequence, structure, and function from evolutionary statistics, thereby accelerating therapeutic drug discovery. These models learn from large protein databases that are continuously updated by the biology community and whose dynamic nature motivates the application of continual learning, not only to keep up with the ever-growing data, but also as an opportunity to take advantage of the temporal meta-information that is created during this process. As a result, we introduce the Continual Pretraining of Protein Language Models (CoPeP) benchmark, a novel benchmark for evaluating continual learning approaches on pLMs. Specifically, we curate a sequence of protein datasets derived from the UniProt Knowledgebase spanning a decade and define metrics to assess pLM performance across 31 protein understanding tasks. We evaluate several methods from the continual learning literature, including replay, unlearning, and plasticity-based methods, some of which have never been applied to models and data of this scale. Our findings reveal that incorporating temporal meta-information improves perplexity by up to 7% even when compared to training on data from all tasks jointly. Moreover, even at scale, several continual learning methods outperform naive continual pretraining. The CoPeP benchmark offers an exciting opportunity to study these methods at scale in an impactful real-world application.

</details>


### [219] [When does Chain-of-Thought Help: A Markovian Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00306)
*Zihan Wang,Yijun Dong,Qi Lei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文通过将思维链推理建模为马尔可夫链，提出"转移对齐"是决定CoT有效性的关键因素，并量化了中间步骤噪声对CoT收益的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 思维链提示技术在不同任务上的推理提升效果不均衡，需要理论分析其何时及为何有效，以指导实际应用。

Method: 将CoT的逐步推理轨迹建模为马尔可夫链，每个中间步骤为状态，步骤间依赖关系由转移核捕获。通过理论分析识别转移对齐的关键作用，并设计合成基准进行实证验证。
